          const copy=el('span','cv4-tool-choice-copy');copy.append(el('span','',ref.title),el('small','',toolKind(ref.kind)+(ref.version!=null?' · Version '+text(ref.version).slice(0,24):'')));
          check.addEventListener('change',()=>{
            if(!voiceContextActive(ctx)||ctx.pending||composerReadOnly(ctx)||ctx.toolsBlocked)return;
            if(check.checked&&!ctx.documents.some(d=>d.id===ref.id)){
              if(ctx.documents.length>=3){check.checked=false;ui.state.textContent='Choose up to 3 Tools references.';return;}
              ctx.documents.push(ref);
            }else if(!check.checked)ctx.documents=ctx.documents.filter(d=>d.id!==ref.id);
            ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';storeDraft(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);ui.state.textContent=ctx.documents.length+' of 3 Tools selected.';
            // Chips can grow above the picker. Keep its focused checkbox in
            // both scroll viewports without remounting it or delaying input.
            if(document.activeElement===check)check.scrollIntoView?.({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest',behavior:'instant'});
          });row.append(check,icon('document'),copy);ui.choices.set(ref.id,check);ui.results.append(row);
        }
        ui.cursor=typeof data.nextCursor==='string'&&data.nextCursor.length<=2048&&data.nextCursor!==cursor?data.nextCursor:null;
        ui.more.hidden=!ui.cursor||ui.rows.length>=64;
        ui.state.textContent=!ui.rows.length?'No documents on this page are available to everyone in this conversation.':ui.rows.length>=64&&ui.cursor?'Showing 64 shared Tools. Open Tools to find other documents.':'Only shared with this conversation';
        ui.privateAction.hidden=!!ui.rows.length;
      }catch(error){
        if(!voiceContextActive(ctx)||person.id!==actor||generation!==ui.generation||ui.panel.hidden)return;
        const unavailable=[403,404,409].includes(error.status);
        if(unavailable){ui.rows=[];ui.choices.clear();ui.results.replaceChildren();ui.cursor=null;ui.more.hidden=true;}
        ctx.toolsBlocked=['source_not_shared','source_audience_limit'].includes(error.code);
        ui.state.textContent=unavailable?error.message+' You can ask Cosmos privately instead.':error.message;
        ui.retry.hidden=false;ui.privateAction.hidden=false;
      }finally{
        if(generation===ui.generation){ui.loading=false;ui.abort=null;ui.results.removeAttribute('aria-busy');if(voiceContextActive(ctx))paintToolSelection(ctx);}
      }
    }
    function readDraft(id,rootId='') {
      const key=draftKey(id,rootId);let d=memoryDrafts.get(key);
      try{if(!d)d=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('cv4-draft:'+key)||'null');}catch{}
      const stagedTools=list(d?.documents);
      if(!d){const remote=conversation(id)?.draft;if(remote&&text(remote.reply_to||'')===rootId)d=remote;}
      const restore=f=>({...f,preview:f.blob?makeLocalURL(f.blob):'',status:f.id?'uploaded':'local'});
      const documents=[];for(const raw of stagedTools.slice(0,3)){const ref=toolDocument(raw);if(ref&&!documents.some(d=>d.id===ref.id))documents.push(ref);}
      return {body:text(d?.body),documents,requestId:text(d?.requestId),fingerprint:text(d?.fingerprint),voice:d?.voice?.file?{...d.voice,phase:'preview',file:restore(d.voice.file)}:null,files:list(d?.files).map(f=>typeof f==='string'?{id:f,name:'Saved attachment',content_type:'application/octet-stream',status:'uploaded'}:restore(f))};
    }
    function ownsToolDraft(ctx) {
      const latest=memoryDrafts.get(draftKey(ctx.id,ctx.rootId));
      return !latest||latest.requestId===ctx.requestId&&latest.fingerprint===ctx.fingerprint;
    }
    function storeDraft(ctx) {
      if(!person||person.id!==ctx.actorId)return;
      if(ctx.pending&&(ctx.isCosmos||ctx.documents.length)&&!ownsToolDraft(ctx))return;
      const fileMeta=f=>({id:f.id,localId:f.localId,name:f.name,bytes:f.bytes||f.blob?.size,content_type:f.content_type,voice:f.voice===true,duration_ms:f.duration_ms,recorded_ms:f.recorded_ms});
      const draft={body:ctx.input.value,reply_to:ctx.isCosmos?null:ctx.rootId||null,documents:list(ctx.documents).slice(0,3),requestId:ctx.requestId||'',fingerprint:ctx.fingerprint||'',files:ctx.files.filter(f=>f.id).map(fileMeta)};
      const voice=ctx.voice?.file?{phase:'preview',elapsedMs:ctx.voice.elapsedMs,requestId:ctx.voice.requestId||'',fingerprint:ctx.voice.fingerprint||'',file:fileMeta(ctx.voice.file)}:null;
      if(voice?.file.id)draft.voice=voice;
      const key=draftKey(ctx.id,ctx.rootId);
      // Blobs survive a native remount in memory; only uploaded IDs enter storage.
      memoryDrafts.set(key,{...draft,voice:voice?{...voice,file:{...voice.file,blob:ctx.voice.file.blob}}:null,files:ctx.files.map(f=>({...fileMeta(f),localId:f.localId,blob:f.blob}))});
      try{if(draft.body||draft.files.length||draft.voice||draft.documents.length)sessionStorage.setItem('cv4-draft:'+key,JSON.stringify(draft));else sessionStorage.removeItem('cv4-draft:'+key);}catch{}
      ctx.draft=draft;return draft;
    }
    function persistDraft(ctx) {
      const d=storeDraft(ctx);if(!d||!alive||sessionLocked||ctx.pending||ctx.isCosmos)return;
      const payload={conversation_id:ctx.id,draft:{body:d.body,reply_to:d.reply_to,files:[...d.files.map(f=>f.id),...(d.voice?.file.id?[d.voice.file.id]:[])]}};
      // Serialize preferences: an old draft response cannot overwrite a newer one.
      draftQueue=draftQueue.catch(()=>{}).then(()=>alive&&!sessionLocked&&person?.id===ctx.actorId?write('preferences',payload):null).catch(err=>{
        if(alive&&!sessionLocked&&ctx.form.isConnected)ctx.status.textContent='Draft saved on this device. Could not sync it yet.';
        if(err.status===403)invalidateRoom(ctx.id,err);
      });
    }
    function saveCurrentDrafts() { cancel(draftTimer);for(const ctx of composers)if(ctx.form.isConnected)persistDraft(ctx); }
    function createComposer(id,rootId) {
      const draft=readDraft(id,rootId),form=el('form','cv4-composer'),label=el('label','cv4-compose-label',rootId?'Reply in thread':'Message '+roomTitle(conversation(id)));
      form.dataset.replyTo=rootId;form.dataset.conversation=id;const input=el('textarea','cv4-input cv4-message-input');
      input.id=instanceId+'-compose-'+(++serial);label.htmlFor=input.id;input.rows=2;input.maxLength=4000;input.placeholder=rootId?'Write a reply…':'Write a message…';input.value=draft.body;
      const previews=el('div','cv4-previews'),statusNode=el('p','cv4-form-status'),hint=el('p','cv4-compose-hint','Enter to send · Shift + Enter for a new line');
      statusNode.id=input.id+'-status';statusNode.setAttribute('role','status');input.setAttribute('aria-describedby',statusNode.id);
      const controls=el('div','cv4-compose-controls'),attach=button('Attach files','file',()=>fileInput.click(),'cv4-icon'),record=button('Record a voice message','mic',()=>toggleRecording(ctx),'cv4-icon');
      attach.dataset.action='attach';record.dataset.action='record';
      const fileInput=el('input','cv4-sr');fileInput.type='file';fileInput.multiple=true;fileInput.tabIndex=-1;fileInput.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose attachments');
      // Retained V2/V3 helpers must not decorate this intentionally hidden
      // modern picker. Attach files still invokes it directly on user click.
      fileInput.dataset.cvfixFile='1';fileInput.dataset.cv3File='yes';fileInput.hidden=true;
      const send=button('Send','send',null,'cv4-primary');send.type='submit';send.dataset.action='send';
      const voiceHost=el('section','cv4-voice-mode');voiceHost.hidden=true;voiceHost.setAttribute('aria-label',rootId?'Voice reply':'Voice message composer');
      const ctx={id,rootId,actorId:person.id,form,label,input,controls,voiceHost,previews,status:statusNode,record,send,attach,files:draft.files,documents:draft.documents,voice:draft.voice,pending:false,requestId:draft.requestId,fingerprint:draft.fingerprint,recording:false};
      const toolUI=createToolSelection(ctx);controls.append(attach,record,toolUI.tools,fileInput,hint,send);form.append(label,input,previews,toolUI.selection,toolUI.panel,voiceHost,statusNode,controls);composers.add(ctx);
      if(composerReadOnly(ctx))statusNode.textContent='This conversation is read-only. You can open existing sources or ask Cosmos privately.';
      input.addEventListener('input',()=>{
        if(ctx.requestId&&!ctx.pending){ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';}
        storeDraft(ctx);cancel(draftTimer);draftTimer=delay(()=>persistDraft(ctx),500);sendTyping(!!input.value);
      });
      input.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey&&!e.isComposing){e.preventDefault();form.requestSubmit();}});
      fileInput.addEventListener('change',()=>{addFiles(ctx,[...fileInput.files]);fileInput.value='';});
      form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();sendMessage(ctx);});
      form.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{if(e.dataTransfer?.types.includes('Files')){e.preventDefault();form.classList.add('cv4-dragging');}});
      form.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>form.classList.remove('cv4-dragging'));
      form.addEventListener('drop',e=>{if(e.dataTransfer?.files.length){e.preventDefault();form.classList.remove('cv4-dragging');addFiles(ctx,[...e.dataTransfer.files]);}});
      paintPreviews(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);return ctx;
    }
    function makeLocalURL(blob) { const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);objectURLs.add(url);return url; }
    function releaseURL(url) { if(url&&objectURLs.has(url)){URL.revokeObjectURL(url);objectURLs.delete(url);} }
    function addFiles(ctx,files) {
      if(composerReadOnly(ctx)||!voiceContextActive(ctx))return;
      if(ctx.pending||ctx.voice){ctx.status.textContent='Finish or discard your voice message before adding files.';return;}
      const mediaLimit=Number(capabilities.max_file_bytes||capabilities.maxFileBytes)||32*1024*1024;
      const documentLimit=Number(capabilities.max_document_bytes||capabilities.maxDocumentBytes)||20*1024*1024;
      const textLimit=Number(capabilities.max_text_bytes||capabilities.maxTextBytes)||2*1024*1024;
      const countLimit=Number(capabilities.maxAttachments)||8;
      for(const blob of files){
        if(ctx.files.length>=countLimit){ctx.status.textContent='Attach up to '+countLimit+' files to one message.';break;}
        if(!blob.size){ctx.status.textContent='Empty files cannot be uploaded.';continue;}
        const type=mediaType(blob.type||'application/octet-stream');
        const media=/^(image|video|audio)\//.test(type),plainText=type==='text/plain'||type==='text/csv';
        const limit=Math.min(mediaLimit,media?mediaLimit:plainText?textLimit:documentLimit);
        if(blob.size>limit){ctx.status.textContent='This '+(media?'media file':plainText?'text file':'document')+' is too large. The limit is '+bytes(limit)+'.';continue;}
        const preview=/^(image\/(jpeg|png|webp)|video\/(mp4|webm|quicktime)|audio\/(webm|ogg|mp4|mpeg|wav|aac))$/.test(type)?makeLocalURL(blob):'';
        ctx.files.push({localId:uid(),blob,name:blob.name||'Voice note.webm',bytes:blob.size,content_type:type,preview,status:'local'});
      }
      storeDraft(ctx);paintPreviews(ctx);
    }
    function paintPreviews(ctx) {
      ctx.previews.querySelectorAll('audio,video').forEach(v=>v.pause());ctx.previews.replaceChildren();ctx.previews.hidden=!!ctx.voice||!ctx.files.length;
      for(const f of ctx.files){
        const wrap=el('div','cv4-preview'),card=attachment(f,f.preview||undefined),progress=el('small','cv4-upload-status',f.status==='uploaded'?'Uploaded · ready to send':f.status==='uploading'?'Uploading…':f.error||'Not sent · upload when you send');
        if(card.classList.contains('cv4-audio-card'))wrap.classList.add('cv4-audio-preview');
        const remove=button('Remove '+f.name,'close',()=>{if(ctx.pending||f.status==='uploading')return;ctx.files=ctx.files.filter(x=>x!==f);releaseURL(f.preview);storeDraft(ctx);paintPreviews(ctx);persistDraft(ctx);},'cv4-icon');
        remove.disabled=ctx.pending||f.status==='uploading';wrap.append(card,progress,remove);
        if(!f.id){const upload=button(f.error?'Retry upload':'Upload file',null,async()=>{try{await uploadFile(ctx,f);persistDraft(ctx);}catch(err){ctx.status.textContent=err.message;}});upload.disabled=ctx.pending||f.status==='uploading';wrap.append(upload);}
        ctx.previews.append(wrap);
      }
      paintToolSelection(ctx);if(ctx.voice)paintVoice(ctx);
    }
    async function uploadFile(ctx,f) {
      if(f.id)return f.id;if(f.promise)return f.promise;
      f.status='uploading';f.error='';paintPreviews(ctx);
      f.promise=(async()=>{
        try{
          const query=new URLSearchParams({conversation_id:ctx.id,name:f.name});
          if(f.voice===true){query.set('voice','1');query.set('recorded_ms',String(Math.max(1,Math.min(120000,Math.round(Number(f.recorded_ms)||1)))));}
          const d=await api('files?'+query,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':f.content_type},body:f.blob});
          if(!d.file?.id)throw new Error('The upload did not return a file. Try again.');
          Object.assign(f,d.file,{status:'uploaded',error:''});storeDraft(ctx);return f.id;
        }catch(error){f.status='local';f.error=error.message;if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(ctx.id,error);throw error;}
        finally{delete f.promise;if(alive&&ctx.form.isConnected)paintPreviews(ctx);}
      })();return f.promise;
    }
    async function sendMessage(ctx) {
      if(ctx.pending||sessionLocked||!alive||person?.id!==ctx.actorId)return;
      if(composerReadOnly(ctx)){ctx.status.textContent='This conversation is read-only.';return;}
      if(ctx.documents.length&&ctx.toolsBlocked){ctx.status.textContent='Shared Tools are unavailable in this conversation. Remove the references or ask Cosmos privately.';return;}
      if(ctx.voice){ctx.status.textContent=ctx.voice.phase==='preview'?'Select Send voice message to send the recording.':'Stop recording and preview your voice message before sending.';return;}
      const body=ctx.input.value;
      if(ctx.recording){ctx.status.textContent='Stop recording and preview your voice message before sending.';return;}
      if(!body.trim()&&!ctx.files.length&&!ctx.documents.length){ctx.status.textContent='Write a message, attach a file or choose a Tools reference.';ctx.input.focus();return;}
      if(body.length>4000){ctx.status.textContent='Messages can contain up to 4,000 characters.';return;}
      if(/^\s*@cosmos\b/i.test(body)&&!ctx.rootId&&!ctx.files.length){
        const prompt=body.replace(/^\s*@cosmos\b[:,]?\s*/i,'');
        if(prompt.length>2000){ctx.status.textContent='Cosmos questions can contain up to 2,000 characters. Shorten your question and try again.';return;}
        showCosmos(prompt,ctx);return;
      }
      const documentIds=selectedDocumentIds(ctx),fingerprint=JSON.stringify([ctx.id,ctx.rootId,body,ctx.files.map(f=>f.localId||f.id),...(documentIds.length?[documentIds]:[])]);
      if(ctx.fingerprint!==fingerprint){ctx.requestId=uid();ctx.fingerprint=fingerprint;}
      storeDraft(ctx);
      ctx.pending=true;ctx.send.disabled=true;ctx.attach.disabled=true;ctx.record.disabled=true;ctx.input.readOnly=true;ctx.status.textContent='Sending…';sendTyping(false);
      closeToolPicker(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);
      try{
        for(const f of ctx.files)await uploadFile(ctx,f);
        if(!alive||sessionLocked||person?.id!==ctx.actorId)return;
        const d=await write('messages',{conversation_id:ctx.id,body,reply_to:ctx.rootId||null,files:ctx.files.map(f=>f.id),...(documentIds.length?{document_ids:documentIds}:{}),requestId:ctx.requestId});
        if(!alive||sessionLocked||person?.id!==ctx.actorId)return;
        const ownsDraft=!documentIds.length||ownsToolDraft(ctx);
        ctx.input.value='';for(const f of ctx.files)releaseURL(f.preview);ctx.files=[];ctx.documents=[];ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';ctx.pending=false;
        if(ownsDraft){storeDraft(ctx);persistDraft(ctx);}paintPreviews(ctx);ctx.status.textContent='Sent';
        if(d.message){const st=stateFor(ctx.id);if(!st.messages.some(m=>m.id===d.message.id))st.messages.push(d.message);else st.messages=st.messages.map(m=>m.id===d.message.id?d.message:m);}
        if(active===ctx.id){try{await refreshActive();if(ctx.rootId&&aux?.rootId===ctx.rootId){const st=await loadMessages(ctx.id,{root:ctx.rootId});if(aux?.rootId===ctx.rootId)paintThread(st,auxiliary.querySelector('.cv4-thread-log'));}}catch(error){if(alive&&!sessionLocked)notify('Your message was sent. Updates could not refresh yet.');}}
        announce(ctx.rootId?'Reply sent.':'Message sent.');
      }catch(error){
        if(error.status===403&&!documentIds.length)invalidateRoom(ctx.id,error);
        else if(alive&&!sessionLocked&&person?.id===ctx.actorId){
          ctx.status.textContent=error.message+' Your draft is still here. Retry Send or ask privately.';
          ctx.toolsBlocked=['source_not_shared','source_audience_limit'].includes(error.code);
          if(error.status===403&&!ctx.toolsBlocked)try{await loadMessages(ctx.id);}catch(check){if([403,404].includes(check.status))invalidateRoom(ctx.id,check);}
        }
      }finally{ctx.pending=false;if(alive&&ctx.form.isConnected){paintPreviews(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);}}
    }
    function voiceContextActive(ctx) { return alive&&!sessionLocked&&person?.id===ctx.actorId&&ctx.form.isConnected; }
    function voiceLimit() { return Number(capabilities.max_file_bytes||capabilities.maxFileBytes)||32*1024*1024; }
    function captureTime() { return window.performance?.now?.()??Date.now(); }
    function releaseVoicePreview(ctx) {
      const file=ctx.voice?.file;if(!file)return;
      pauseMedia(ctx.voiceHost);releaseURL(file.preview);file.preview='';ctx.voiceUI?.preview.replaceChildren();storeDraft(ctx);
    }
    function discardVoice(ctx,focus=true,force=false) {
      if(ctx.pending&&!force)return;
      if(recording?.ctx===ctx)stopRecording(true);
      releaseVoicePreview(ctx);ctx.voice=null;ctx.recording=false;storeDraft(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);
      if(focus&&voiceContextActive(ctx)){ctx.status.textContent='Voice message discarded.';ctx.record.focus();}
    }
    function paintVoice(ctx) {
      const voice=ctx.voice,show=!!voice,readonly=composerReadOnly(ctx);
      ctx.label.hidden=show;ctx.input.hidden=show;ctx.controls.hidden=show;ctx.voiceHost.hidden=!show;
      ctx.previews.hidden=show||!ctx.files.length;ctx.input.readOnly=show||ctx.pending||readonly;
      ctx.send.disabled=show||ctx.pending||readonly||!!(ctx.documents.length&&ctx.toolsBlocked);ctx.record.disabled=show||ctx.pending||readonly;ctx.attach.disabled=show||ctx.pending||readonly;
      if(show)closeToolPicker(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);
      if(!voice){delete ctx.form.dataset.voice;ctx.voiceHost.replaceChildren();ctx.voiceUI=null;return;}
      ctx.form.dataset.voice=voice.phase;
      if(!ctx.voiceUI){
        const top=el('div','cv4-voice-top'),indicator=el('span','cv4-voice-indicator'),state=el('span','cv4-voice-state'),clock=el('time','cv4-voice-elapsed','0:00');
        indicator.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');state.setAttribute('role','status');clock.setAttribute('aria-live','off');
        top.append(indicator,state,clock);
        const preview=el('div','cv4-voice-preview'),actions=el('div','cv4-voice-actions');
        const discard=button('Discard recording','trash',()=>discardVoice(ctx),'cv4-icon');discard.dataset.action='voice-discard';
        const stop=button('Stop recording','stop',()=>{if(recording?.ctx===ctx)stopRecording(false);});stop.dataset.action='voice-stop';
        const again=button('Record again','mic',()=>{discardVoice(ctx,false);toggleRecording(ctx);});again.dataset.action='voice-again';
        const send=button('Send voice message','send',()=>sendVoiceMessage(ctx),'cv4-primary');send.dataset.action='voice-send';
        actions.append(discard,stop,again,send);ctx.voiceHost.append(top,preview,actions);ctx.voiceUI={state,clock,preview,discard,stop,again,send};
      }
      const ui=ctx.voiceUI,ready=voice.phase==='preview',capturing=voice.phase==='recording';
      const value=voice.phase==='permission'?'Waiting for microphone…':capturing?'Recording':voice.phase==='processing'?'Preparing voice message…':ctx.pending?'Sending voice message…':'Voice message';
      if(ui.state.textContent!==value)ui.state.textContent=value;
      ui.clock.textContent=audioTime((voice.elapsedMs||0)/1000);ui.clock.setAttribute('aria-label',(capturing?'Recording time: ':'Recorded time: ')+ui.clock.textContent);
      ui.clock.hidden=voice.phase==='permission';ui.stop.hidden=!capturing;ui.stop.disabled=!capturing;
      ui.again.hidden=!ready;ui.send.hidden=!ready;ui.send.disabled=!ready||ctx.pending;ui.again.disabled=ctx.pending;ui.discard.disabled=ctx.pending;
      ui.discard.setAttribute('aria-label',ready?'Delete voice message':'Discard recording');ui.discard.title=ready?'Delete voice message':'Discard recording';
      ui.preview.hidden=!ready;
      if(ready&&voice.file){
        const file=voice.file;if(!file.preview&&file.blob)file.preview=makeLocalURL(file.blob);
        const card=audioCard(file,file.preview||fileURL(file),!!file.preview,ui.preview.querySelector('.cv4-audio-card'));
        if(card.parentNode!==ui.preview){ui.preview.replaceChildren(card);card.querySelector('audio')?.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{if(ctx.voice?.file===file)storeDraft(ctx);});}
      }
    }
    async function sendVoiceMessage(ctx) {
      const voice=ctx.voice,file=voice?.file;
      if(!voiceContextActive(ctx)||ctx.pending||composerReadOnly(ctx)||voice?.phase!=='preview'||!file)return;
      const fingerprint=JSON.stringify([ctx.id,ctx.rootId,file.localId||file.id]);
      if(voice.fingerprint!==fingerprint){voice.requestId=uid();voice.fingerprint=fingerprint;}
      storeDraft(ctx);ctx.pending=true;ctx.status.textContent='Sending voice message…';pauseMedia(ctx.voiceHost);paintVoice(ctx);sendTyping(false);
      try{
        await uploadFile(ctx,file);
        if(!voiceContextActive(ctx)||ctx.voice!==voice)return;
        // Voice metadata travels on the authorized upload, never as a model
        // duration or message-body convention. The message accepts file IDs.
        const d=await write('messages',{conversation_id:ctx.id,body:'',reply_to:ctx.rootId||null,files:[file.id],requestId:voice.requestId});
        if(!alive||sessionLocked||person?.id!==ctx.actorId||ctx.voice!==voice)return;
        releaseVoicePreview(ctx);ctx.voice=null;ctx.pending=false;storeDraft(ctx);persistDraft(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);ctx.status.textContent='Voice message sent.';
        if(d.message){const st=stateFor(ctx.id);if(!st.messages.some(m=>m.id===d.message.id))st.messages.push(d.message);}
        if(active===ctx.id){try{await refreshActive();if(ctx.rootId&&aux?.rootId===ctx.rootId){const st=await loadMessages(ctx.id,{root:ctx.rootId});if(aux?.rootId===ctx.rootId)paintThread(st,auxiliary.querySelector('.cv4-thread-log'));}}catch{if(alive&&!sessionLocked)notify('Your voice message was sent. Updates could not refresh yet.');}}
        announce(ctx.rootId?'Voice reply sent.':'Voice message sent.');
      }catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(ctx.id,error);else if(voiceContextActive(ctx)&&ctx.voice===voice)ctx.status.textContent=error.message+' Select Send voice message to retry.';}
      finally{ctx.pending=false;if(alive&&ctx.form.isConnected)paintVoice(ctx);}
    }
    async function toggleRecording(ctx) {
      if(ctx.pending||composerReadOnly(ctx)||!voiceContextActive(ctx))return;
      if(recording){ctx.status.textContent='Finish or discard the recording in the other composer first.';return;}
      if(ctx.voice){ctx.status.textContent='Send, delete or re-record this voice message first.';return;}
      if(!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia||!window.MediaRecorder){ctx.status.textContent='Voice recording is unavailable in this browser. You can attach an audio file.';return;}
      pauseMedia(root);sendTyping(false);
      const voice={phase:'permission',file:null,elapsedMs:0,requestId:'',fingerprint:''};ctx.voice=voice;ctx.recording=true;
      const session={token:uid(),ctx,voice,stream:null,recorder:null,chunks:[],bytes:0,cancelled:false,startedAt:null};recording=session;
      ctx.status.textContent='Allow microphone access to record. Nothing is sent until you select Send voice message.';paintVoice(ctx);
      const reset=message=>{
        if(recording===session)stopRecording(true);
        if(ctx.voice===voice){ctx.voice=null;ctx.recording=false;storeDraft(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);}
        if(voiceContextActive(ctx))ctx.status.textContent=message;
      };
      try{
        const stream=await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true,video:false});
        if(recording!==session||!voiceContextActive(ctx)){stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());return;}
        session.stream=stream;
        const type=['audio/webm;codecs=opus','audio/webm','audio/mp4','audio/ogg;codecs=opus'].find(x=>MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported?.(x));
        const recorder=type?new MediaRecorder(stream,{mimeType:type}):new MediaRecorder(stream);session.recorder=recorder;
        recorder.addEventListener('dataavailable',e=>{
          if(session.cancelled||!e.data?.size)return;
          session.bytes+=e.data.size;if(session.bytes>voiceLimit()){reset('The recording is too large. Record a shorter voice message.');return;}
          session.chunks.push(e.data);
        });
        recorder.addEventListener('error',()=>reset('Recording failed. Select the microphone to try again.'),{once:true});
        recorder.addEventListener('stop',()=>{
          stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());cancel(session.timer);cancel(session.tickTimer);ctx.recording=false;
          if(recording===session)recording=null;
          if(session.cancelled||!voiceContextActive(ctx)||ctx.voice!==voice)return;
          const reported=mediaType(recorder.mimeType||type||'audio/webm'),mime=reported.replace(/^video\/(mp4|webm)$/,'audio/$1');
          const blob=new Blob(session.chunks,{type:mime});
          if(!blob.size){reset('The recording was empty. Select the microphone to try again.');return;}
          if(blob.size>voiceLimit()){reset('The recording is too large. Record a shorter voice message.');return;}
          if(!/^audio\/(webm|mp4|ogg)$/.test(mime)){reset('This recording format is unsupported. You can attach an audio file.');return;}
          const ext=mime==='audio/mp4'?'m4a':mime==='audio/ogg'?'ogg':'webm';
          voice.file={localId:uid(),blob,name:'Voice message.'+ext,bytes:blob.size,content_type:mime,preview:makeLocalURL(blob),status:'local',voice:true,recorded_ms:Math.max(1,Math.round(voice.elapsedMs))};
          voice.phase='preview';storeDraft(ctx);paintVoice(ctx);ctx.status.textContent='Play your voice message, then send it when ready.';ctx.voiceUI.send.focus();
        },{once:true});
        recorder.start(1000);session.startedAt=captureTime();voice.phase='recording';ctx.status.textContent='Microphone on. Stop to preview · 2-minute limit.';paintVoice(ctx);ctx.voiceUI.stop.focus();
        const tick=()=>{if(recording!==session||voice.phase!=='recording')return;voice.elapsedMs=Math.min(120000,Math.max(0,captureTime()-session.startedAt));paintVoice(ctx);if(voice.elapsedMs>=120000)stopRecording(false);else session.tickTimer=delay(tick,1000);};
        session.tickTimer=delay(tick,1000);session.timer=delay(()=>{if(recording===session)stopRecording(false);},120000);
      }catch(error){
        if(recording!==session)return;
        reset(error.name==='NotAllowedError'?'Microphone permission was denied. Allow access to record, or attach an audio file.':'The microphone could not be opened. Select the microphone to try again.');
      }
    }
    function stopRecording(discard) {
      const r=recording;if(!r)return;r.cancelled=r.cancelled||!!discard;cancel(r.timer);cancel(r.tickTimer);
      if(r.startedAt!==null)r.voice.elapsedMs=Math.min(120000,Math.max(0,captureTime()-r.startedAt));
      if(discard){recording=null;r.ctx.voice=null;r.ctx.recording=false;storeDraft(r.ctx);paintVoice(r.ctx);}
      else{r.voice.phase='processing';paintVoice(r.ctx);r.ctx.status.textContent='Preparing your voice message…';}
      if(r.recorder&&r.recorder.state!=='inactive'){try{r.recorder.stop();}catch{recording=null;r.ctx.voice=null;r.ctx.recording=false;paintVoice(r.ctx);r.ctx.status.textContent='Recording could not finish. Select the microphone to try again.';}}
      r.stream?.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());
      if(!r.recorder){recording=null;r.ctx.recording=false;}
    }
    function showCosmos(prompt='',composer=null) {
      if(!active||sessionLocked||!person)return;
      const id=active,mentionBody=composer?.input.value,mentionDocuments=composer?JSON.stringify(selectedDocumentIds(composer)):'';
      openAux('cosmos');auxiliary.append(auxHeader('Ask Cosmos'));
      const draft=readDraft(id,'cosmos');
      const form=el('form','cv4-detail-form'),label=el('label','','Ask in this conversation'),input=el('textarea','cv4-input'),out=el('p','cv4-form-status');
      input.id=instanceId+'-cosmos';label.htmlFor=input.id;input.value=prompt||draft.body;input.rows=4;input.maxLength=2000;out.setAttribute('role','status');out.id=input.id+'-status';input.setAttribute('aria-describedby',out.id);
      const send=button('Ask Cosmos',null,null,'cv4-primary');send.prepend(orb());send.type='submit';send.dataset.action='ask-cosmos';
      const ctx={id,rootId:'cosmos',isCosmos:true,actorId:person.id,form,input,status:out,send,files:[],documents:composer?[...composer.documents]:draft.documents,requestId:draft.requestId,fingerprint:draft.fingerprint,pending:false};
      const toolUI=createToolSelection(ctx);composers.add(ctx);
      form.append(el('p','cv4-privacy','Only shared with this conversation. The question and answer are visible to everyone in the room. Tools are used only when you select documents everyone can already access.'),label,input,toolUI.tools,toolUI.selection,toolUI.panel,out,send,button('Ask privately instead',null,()=>{
        if(ctx.documents.length===1)openPrivateTool(ctx.documents[0],input.value);
        else if(ctx.documents.length>1)out.textContent='Choose a source’s “Ask privately” action to open that document with your question.';
        else invokePrivate(input.value);
      }));
      input.readOnly=composerReadOnly(ctx);if(input.readOnly)out.textContent='This conversation is read-only. You can ask Cosmos privately instead.';
      input.addEventListener('input',()=>{if(!ctx.pending){ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';storeDraft(ctx);}});
      auxiliary.append(form);input.focus();
      form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
        e.preventDefault();if(ctx.pending||!voiceContextActive(ctx)||active!==id)return;
        if(composerReadOnly(ctx)){out.textContent='This conversation is read-only. Ask Cosmos privately instead.';return;}
        if(ctx.documents.length&&ctx.toolsBlocked){out.textContent='Shared Tools are unavailable in this conversation. Remove the references or ask Cosmos privately.';return;}
        const value=input.value.trim();if(!value){out.textContent='Add a question for Cosmos.';return;}
        if(value.length>2000){out.textContent='Cosmos questions can contain up to 2,000 characters. Shorten your question and try again.';input.focus();return;}
        const documentIds=selectedDocumentIds(ctx),fingerprint=JSON.stringify([id,value,documentIds]);
        if(ctx.fingerprint!==fingerprint){ctx.requestId=uid();ctx.fingerprint=fingerprint;}storeDraft(ctx);
        ctx.pending=true;input.readOnly=true;closeToolPicker(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);out.textContent='Cosmos is thinking…';
        try{
          const d=await write('cosmos',{conversation_id:id,prompt:value,...(documentIds.length?{document_ids:documentIds}:{}),requestId:ctx.requestId});
          if(!alive||sessionLocked||person?.id!==ctx.actorId)return;
          const ownsDraft=ownsToolDraft(ctx);ctx.pending=false;
          ctx.input.value='';ctx.documents=[];ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';if(ownsDraft)storeDraft(ctx);
          if(composer&&person.id===composer.actorId&&composer.input.value===mentionBody&&JSON.stringify(selectedDocumentIds(composer))===mentionDocuments){composer.input.value='';composer.documents=[];composer.requestId='';composer.fingerprint='';storeDraft(composer);persistDraft(composer);paintToolSelection(composer);}
          if(d.message){const st=stateFor(id);const at=st.messages.findIndex(x=>x.id===d.message.id);if(at<0)st.messages.push(d.message);else st.messages[at]=d.message;}
          if(active===id){if(aux?.kind==='cosmos'&&form.isConnected)closeAux();try{await refreshActive();}catch{notify('Cosmos answered. Updates could not refresh yet.');}announce('Cosmos answered in this conversation.');}
        }catch(error){
          if(voiceContextActive(ctx)){out.textContent=error.message+' Your question is still here. Try again or continue privately.';ctx.toolsBlocked=['source_not_shared','source_audience_limit'].includes(error.code);}
          if(error.status===403&&error.code!=='cosmos_disabled'&&alive&&!sessionLocked)notify(error.message);
        }finally{ctx.pending=false;if(voiceContextActive(ctx)){input.readOnly=composerReadOnly(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);}}
      });
    }
    function invokePrivate(prompt) {
      if(typeof options.onPrivateCosmos==='function'){options.onPrivateCosmos(text(prompt));}
      else notify('Private Cosmos is not available here. Open the Cosmos orb in Communiverse.');
    }
    function invokeMeetings() {
      saveCurrentDrafts();if(typeof options.onMeetings==='function')options.onMeetings();else notify('Meetings are managed in the Communiverse Work tab.');
    }

        try{window.cvConnectCalls.mount(widget,{conversation:conversation(),api});}catch{widget.textContent='Call controls could not load. Paid calls remain disabled.';}
      }
    }
    function showMembers() {
      const c=conversation();if(!c)return;openAux('members');auxiliary.append(auxHeader('Members'));
      const body=el('div','cv4-detail-form'),members=stateFor(active).participants.length?stateFor(active).participants:list(c.participants);
      const canManage=typeof c.can_manage==='boolean'?c.can_manage:typeof c.canManage==='boolean'?c.canManage:c.kind!=='dm'&&(c.creator_id===person.id||c.created_by===person.id);
      for(const p of members){
        const row=el('div','cv4-member'),copy=el('div');copy.append(el('span','',p.name||'Member'),el('small','',p.role||'Member'));row.append(avatar(p),copy);
        if(canManage&&p.id!==person.id)row.append(button('Remove',null,()=>memberAction('remove',[p.id])));body.append(row);
      }
      if(canManage){
        const form=el('form','cv4-member-add'),label=el('label','','Add people'),select=el('select','cv4-input');select.id=instanceId+'-member';label.htmlFor=select.id;
        for(const p of people.filter(p=>p.id!=='cosmos'&&!members.some(x=>x.id===p.id))){const o=el('option','',p.name);o.value=p.id;select.append(o);}
        const add=button('Add member','plus',null);add.type='submit';add.disabled=!select.children.length;form.append(label,select,add);form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(select.value)memberAction('add',[select.value]);});body.append(form);
      }
      if(c.kind==='channel')body.append(button('Leave channel',null,()=>memberAction('leave',[]),'cv4-danger'));auxiliary.append(body);
    }
    async function memberAction(action,identities) {
      const id=active;try{await write('members',{conversation_id:id,action,identities});
        if(action==='leave'){invalidateRoom(id,new Error('You left the channel.'));return;}
        await loadMessages(id);if(active===id){renderRoom();showMembers();}
      }catch(error){notify(error.message);}
    }
    function showCreate() {
      if(!person||sessionLocked)return;openAux('new');auxiliary.append(auxHeader('New conversation'));
      const form=el('form','cv4-detail-form'),kindLabel=el('label','','Conversation type'),kind=el('select','cv4-input'),titleLabel=el('label','','Name'),title=el('input','cv4-input'),topicLabel=el('label','','Topic'),topic=el('input','cv4-input');

      kind.id=instanceId+'-kind';kindLabel.htmlFor=kind.id;title.id=instanceId+'-title';titleLabel.htmlFor=title.id;title.maxLength=120;title.required=true;topic.id=instanceId+'-topic';topicLabel.htmlFor=topic.id;topic.maxLength=500;
      const privateLabel=el('label','cv4-checkbox'),priv=el('input');priv.type='checkbox';privateLabel.append(priv,el('span','','Private channel · selected people only'));
      const fieldset=el('fieldset','cv4-participants');fieldset.append(el('legend','','Include people'));
      for(const p of people.filter(p=>p.id!==person.id&&p.id!=='cosmos'&&!p.bot)){
        const label=el('label','cv4-checkbox'),check=el('input');check.type='checkbox';check.name='participants';check.value=p.id;label.append(check,avatar(p),el('span','',p.name));fieldset.append(label);
      }
      const out=el('p','cv4-form-status');out.setAttribute('role','status');const create=button('Create conversation','plus',null,'cv4-primary');create.type='submit';
      kind.addEventListener('change',()=>{priv.checked=kind.value==='group';privateLabel.hidden=kind.value==='group';});
      if(kind.value==='group'){priv.checked=true;privateLabel.hidden=true;}
      form.append(kindLabel,kind,titleLabel,title,topicLabel,topic,privateLabel,fieldset,out,create);auxiliary.append(form);
      let key='';form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
        e.preventDefault();const participants=[...fieldset.querySelectorAll('input:checked')].map(x=>x.value);
        if(kind.value==='group'&&!participants.length){out.textContent='Choose at least one person for a group message.';return;}
        if(!title.value.trim()){out.textContent='Add a conversation name.';return;}create.disabled=true;key=key||uid();
        try{const d=await write('conversations',{kind:kind.value,title:title.value.trim(),participants,private:kind.value==='group'||priv.checked,topic:topic.value,requestId:key});const c=d.conversation||d;updateConversation(c);if(!c.id)throw new Error('The conversation could not be opened.');await openConversation(c.id);}
        catch(error){out.textContent=error.message;}finally{create.disabled=false;}
      });
    }
    function showSearch(query='',inRoom=false) {
      if(!person)return;openAux('search');auxiliary.append(auxHeader(inRoom?'Find in conversation':'Search messages'));
      const form=el('form','cv4-search-form'),label=el('label','','Search your messages'),input=el('input','cv4-input'),submit=button('Search','search',null);submit.type='submit';
      input.id=instanceId+'-room-search';label.htmlFor=input.id;input.type='search';input.value=query;input.maxLength=200;
      const scopeLabel=el('label','cv4-checkbox'),scope=el('input');scope.type='checkbox';scope.checked=inRoom;scope.disabled=!active;scopeLabel.append(scope,el('span','','Only this conversation'));
      form.append(label,input,scopeLabel,submit);const results=el('div','cv4-search-results');results.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');auxiliary.append(form,results);
      const run=async()=>{
        const q=input.value.trim(),token=++searchSerial;if(!q){results.replaceChildren(empty('Find a conversation moment','Search messages you can access.'));return;}
        const params=new URLSearchParams({q});if(scope.checked&&active)params.set('conversation_id',active);
        skeleton(results,'Searching…');submit.disabled=true;
        try{const d=await api('search?'+params);if(!alive||aux?.kind!=='search'||token!==searchSerial)return;
          results.replaceChildren();results.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
          for(const hit of list(d.messages||d.results)){
            const b=button(text(hit.body||'Attachment'),null,async()=>{
              const id=hit.conversation_id||hit.thread_id;await openConversation(id);if(!alive||active!==id)return;
              let target=timeline.querySelector('[data-message="'+CSS.escape(text(hit.id))+'"]');
              // Search results may precede the latest page; walk bounded history.
              for(let n=0;n<6&&!target&&stateFor(id).nextCursor!=null;n++){
                await loadMessages(id,{before:stateFor(id).nextCursor});if(!alive||active!==id)return;renderRoom();target=timeline.querySelector('[data-message="'+CSS.escape(text(hit.id))+'"]');
              }
              if(hit.reply_to){await openThread(hit.reply_to);target=auxiliary.querySelector('[data-message="'+CSS.escape(text(hit.id))+'"]');}
              if(target){target.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});target.focus();target.classList.add('cv4-highlight');delay(()=>target.classList.remove('cv4-highlight'),2500);}
              else notify('This result is earlier in the conversation. Load earlier messages to find it.');
            },'cv4-search-hit');b.replaceChildren(el('small','',roomTitle(conversation(hit.conversation_id||hit.thread_id))+' · '+time(hit.created_at)),el('p','',hit.deleted_at?'Message deleted':hit.body||'Attachment'));b.dataset.searchMessage=hit.id;results.append(b);
          }
          if(!results.children.length)results.append(empty('No messages found','Try another word or change the search scope.'));
        }catch(error){if(alive&&token===searchSerial){results.removeAttribute('aria-busy');results.replaceChildren(empty('Search unavailable',error.message,button('Retry','refresh',run)));}}
        finally{if(token===searchSerial)submit.disabled=false;}
      };
      form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();run();});input.focus();if(query)run();else results.append(empty('Find a conversation moment','Search messages you can access.'));
    }
    function observeReads() {
      if(!window.IntersectionObserver)return;
      readObserver?.disconnect();readObserver=new IntersectionObserver(()=>queueRead(),{threshold:[0,0.5,1]});
      for(const n of root.querySelectorAll('[data-message][data-sequence]'))readObserver.observe(n);
    }
    function queueRead() { if(!alive||sessionLocked)return;cancel(readTimer);readTimer=delay(markVisible,240); }
    function visibleSequence() {
      if(document.hidden||section!=='messages'||!active||pane==='sidebar'||pane==='people'||!root.isConnected)return 0;
      const logs=[timeline];if(aux?.kind==='thread')logs.push(auxiliary.querySelector('.cv4-thread-log'));
      let seen=0;
      for(const log of logs.filter(Boolean)){
        const bounds=log.getBoundingClientRect();if(bounds.width<1||bounds.height<1||!log.getClientRects().length)continue;
        for(const row of log.querySelectorAll('[data-message][data-sequence]')){
          const r=row.getBoundingClientRect(),overlap=Math.min(r.bottom,bounds.bottom,innerHeight)-Math.max(r.top,bounds.top,0);
          // Only rows actually exposed in a scroll viewport can advance a receipt.
          if(r.width>0&&overlap>=Math.min(r.height*.5,80)&&r.right>0&&r.left<innerWidth)seen=Math.max(seen,Number(row.dataset.sequence)||0);
        }
      }return seen;
    }
    async function markVisible() {
      const id=active;if(!id||navigator.onLine===false)return;const st=stateFor(id),seen=visibleSequence();
      if(!seen||seen<=st.ack||readPending.has(id))return;readPending.set(id,seen);
      try{const d=await write('read',{conversation_id:id,sequence:seen});if(!alive)return;
        st.ack=Math.max(st.ack,seen,Number(d.readSequence)||0);const c=conversation(id);
        if(c){if(d.unread!==undefined)c.unread=Number(d.unread)||0;else{
          const newest=Math.max(0,...st.messages.map(m=>Number(m.sequence)||0));if(seen>=newest)c.unread=0;
        }paintSidebar();}
      }catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,error);}
      finally{readPending.delete(id);if(alive&&visibleSequence()>st.ack)readTimer=delay(markVisible,2000);}
    }
    cleanup.push(listen(timeline,'scroll',queueRead,{passive:true}));cleanup.push(listen(auxiliary,'scroll',queueRead,{capture:true,passive:true}));
    function closeSocket() {
      ++socketGeneration;cancel(reconnectTimer);
      if(socket){socket.onopen=socket.onclose=socket.onerror=socket.onmessage=null;try{socket.close();}catch{}socket=null;}
    }
    function sendTyping(value) {
      if(!socket||socket.readyState!==1)return;if(value&&Date.now()-typingAt<2500)return;typingAt=Date.now();
      try{socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'typing',active:!!value}));}catch{}
    }
    function scheduleReconnect(ms) {
      cancel(reconnectTimer);if(!alive||sessionLocked||!active||section!=='messages'||navigator.onLine===false)return;
      reconnectTimer=delay(connect,ms===undefined?Math.min(30000,1000*Math.pow(2,Math.min(retryAttempt++,5)))+Math.floor(Math.random()*400):ms);
    }
    function connect() {
      if(!alive||sessionLocked||!active||section!=='messages')return;
      closeSocket();const generation=socketGeneration,id=active;
      if(navigator.onLine===false){connection('Offline · drafts saved on this device','offline');schedulePoll();return;}
      if(!window.WebSocket){connection('Updates by polling','polling');schedulePoll();return;}
      connection(retryAttempt?'Reconnecting · checking for updates':'Connecting…',retryAttempt?'reconnecting':'connecting');
      try{
        const url=new URL(PREFIX+'events?'+new URLSearchParams({conversation_id:id}),location.origin);url.protocol=url.protocol==='https:'?'wss:':'ws:';
        socket=new WebSocket(url.href);
        socket.onopen=()=>{
          if(!alive||generation!==socketGeneration||active!==id)return;retryAttempt=0;pollFailures=0;cancel(pollTimer);connection('Connected','live');
          refreshActive().catch(()=>{});schedulePoll(30000);
        };
        socket.onmessage=e=>{
          if(!alive||generation!==socketGeneration||active!==id)return;let event;try{event=JSON.parse(e.data);}catch{return;}
          if(event.conversation_id&&text(event.conversation_id)!==id)return;
          if(event.type==='typing'){
            const typing=header.querySelector('.cv4-typing')||el('span','cv4-typing');
            const user=event.person||personFor(event.sender_id||event.person_id||event.identity_id,stateFor(id).participants);
            typing.textContent=event.active&&user.id!==person.id?text(user.name)+' is typing…':'';header.append(typing);delay(()=>{if(typing.isConnected)typing.remove();},5000);return;
          }
          if(event.type==='message'||event.type==='refresh'||event.type==='presence')refreshActive().catch(()=>{});
        };
        const lost=event=>{
          if(!alive||generation!==socketGeneration||active!==id)return;
          if(event?.code===4003){invalidateRoom(id,new Error('Connect access was revoked. Reopen Connect to verify your membership.'));connection('Access revoked','error');return;}
          connection(navigator.onLine===false?'Offline · drafts saved on this device':'Reconnecting · updates by polling',navigator.onLine===false?'offline':'reconnecting');
          schedulePoll(1000);scheduleReconnect();
        };socket.onclose=lost;socket.onerror=()=>{try{socket?.close();}catch{}lost();};
        delay(()=>{if(alive&&generation===socketGeneration&&socket?.readyState===0){try{socket.close();}catch{}connection('Socket unavailable · updates by polling','polling');schedulePoll(0);scheduleReconnect();}},10000);
      }catch{connection('Socket unavailable · updates by polling','polling');schedulePoll(0);scheduleReconnect();}
    }
    async function refreshActive() {
      if(polling||!alive||!active||sessionLocked||document.hidden)return;polling=true;const id=active;
      try{
        await loadMessages(id);if(!alive||active!==id)return;
        const st=stateFor(id);
        if(section==='messages'){paintMessages(timeline,st,false);paintHeader();}
        if(aux?.kind==='thread'){const rootId=aux.rootId,ts=await loadMessages(id,{root:rootId});if(alive&&aux?.rootId===rootId){const log=auxiliary.querySelector('.cv4-thread-log');if(log)paintThread(ts,log);}}
        pollFailures=0;if(socket?.readyState===1)connection('Connected','live');paintSidebar();
      }catch(error){if(error.status===403||error.status===404)invalidateRoom(id,error);else if(alive&&!sessionLocked){pollFailures++;connection(navigator.onLine===false?'Offline · drafts saved on this device':'Updates paused · retrying',navigator.onLine===false?'offline':'error');}throw error;}
      finally{polling=false;}
    }
    function schedulePoll(ms) {
      cancel(pollTimer);if(!alive||sessionLocked||!active)return;
      const wait=ms===undefined?Math.min(60000,8000*Math.pow(2,Math.min(pollFailures,3))):ms;
      pollTimer=delay(async()=>{
        if(!document.hidden&&navigator.onLine!==false){
          try{await refreshActive();await refreshConversations();if(alive&&socket?.readyState!==1)connection('Updates by polling · reconnecting','polling');}catch{}
        }
        if(alive)schedulePoll(socket?.readyState===1?30000:undefined);
      },wait);
    }
    async function initialize() {
      skeleton(sidebarList,'Loading your conversations…');skeleton(timeline,'Opening Connect…');paintTabs();
      try{
        const d=await api('bootstrap');if(!alive)return;
        if(!d.person?.id){lockSession();return;}
        if(toolsAccount&&toolsAccount!==d.person.id)clearToolDrafts(toolsAccount);toolsAccount=d.person.id;
        person=d.person;people=list(d.people);conversations=list(d.conversations);available=list(d.available_channels);capabilities=d.capabilities||{};calls=d.calls||calls;
        paintSidebar();timeline.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
        if(section==='people'){setPane('people');paintPeople();connection('People ready','ready');}
        else if(options.initialConversation||conversations[0]?.id){await openConversation(options.initialConversation||conversations[0].id);}
        else{paintWelcome();connection('No conversation selected','ready');}
      }catch(error){
        if(!alive||sessionLocked)return;connection('Connect unavailable','error');sidebarList.removeAttribute('aria-busy');sidebarList.replaceChildren();
        timeline.removeAttribute('aria-busy');timeline.replaceChildren(empty('Connect could not open',error.message,button('Try again','refresh',initialize)));notify(error.message);
      }
    }
    function unmount() {
      if(!alive)return;
      for(const ctx of composers){storeDraft(ctx);closeToolPicker(ctx);}
      alive=false;closeSocket();stopRecording(true);readObserver?.disconnect();resizeObserver?.disconnect();hostObserver.disconnect();
      for(const cancelListener of cleanup)cancelListener();for(const id of timers)clearTimeout(id);timers.clear();
      for(const controller of pending)controller.abort();pending.clear();
      root.querySelectorAll('audio,video').forEach(v=>v.pause());for(const url of objectURLs)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);objectURLs.clear();
      if(aux?.kind==='calls')window.cvConnectCalls?.unmount?.();
      composers.clear();rooms.clear();threads.clear();if(root.parentNode===host)root.remove();if(current===instance)current=null;
    }
    const instance={ready:null,openConversation,unmount};
    current=instance;instance.ready=initialize();return instance;
  }
  window.cvConnectV4={
    release:RELEASE, mount,
    unmount:()=>current?.unmount(),
    openConversation:id=>current?.openConversation(id)
  };
})();

;

if(window.cvAccountMenu)return;

const paths={profile:'M12 12a4 4 0 1 0 0-8 4 4 0 0 0 0 8M5 21v-2a7 7 0 0 1 14 0v2',tools:'M4 4h16v16H4zM4 9h16M9 9v11',task:'m8 12 3 3 5-6M5 3h14v18H5z',work:'M3 7h18v14H3zM8 7V3h8v4M3 12h18',manage:'M4 4h6v6H4zM14 4h6v6h-6zM4 14h6v6H4zM14 14h6v6h-6z',signout:'M9 4H4v16h5M10 12h11m-4-4 4 4-4 4'};
function icon(key){const svg=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','svg'),path=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','path');svg.setAttribute('viewBox','0 0 24 24');svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');svg.setAttribute('focusable','false');path.setAttribute('d',paths[key]);svg.append(path);return svg}
function principal(value){
 if(!value||typeof value.id!=='string'||!value.id||!['staff','artist','member'].includes(value.kind))return null;
 return {id:value.id,kind:value.kind,role:typeof value.role==='string'?value.role:'',name:typeof value.name==='string'?value.name:'Your account',title:typeof value.title==='string'?value.title:'',photo:photoURL(value.photo||value.photo_url)};
}
function photoURL(value){try{const url=new URL(value,location.origin);return typeof value==='string'&&url.origin===location.origin&&url.pathname.startsWith(ROOT+'_public/')?url.pathname+url.search:''}catch{return ''}}
// Canonical Tools/Work require staff. managementCapabilities grants active staff

function destinations(actor){

 return rows;
}
let actor=null,actorStamp='',trigger=null,original=null,wasProfileChip=false,panel=null,menu=null,summary=null,notice=null;
let open=false,items=[],observer=null,scheduled=false,destroyed=false,suspended=false,epoch=0,request=null,requestTimer=0,pending=null;
let nativeStamp=null,lastHeader=null,lastHeaderStamp='',loggingOut=false,profileBusy=false,profileOwner='',profileContent=null,profileDialog=null,profileReturn=null,historyClosing=false,typeBuffer='',typeTimer=0;
const bindings=[];
const observation={subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden','open','src','aria-label']};

const stamp=person=>person?JSON.stringify(person):'';
function cancelRead(){epoch++;request?.abort();request=null;pending=null;clearTimeout(requestTimer);requestTimer=0}
function dismiss(returnFocus=false){open=false;if(panel)panel.hidden=true;trigger?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(returnFocus&&trigger?.isConnected)trigger.focus();clearTimeout(typeTimer);typeBuffer=''}
function restoreTrigger(){
 if(!trigger)return;trigger.classList.remove('cva-trigger');trigger.removeAttribute('data-cva-trigger');

}
function clearMenu(){dismiss();panel?.remove();panel=menu=summary=null;items=[];restoreTrigger()}
function applyIdentity(person){
 const next=stamp(person);if(next!==actorStamp){dismiss();if(person?.id!==actor?.id){profileOwner='';profileContent=null}actor=person;actorStamp=next;renderMenu()}
 if(actor)mountTrigger();else clearMenu();
}
function nativeIdentity(){
 const app=$('#cv-ws-app'),adapter=window.__cvWorkspaceNative;
 if(!app||!adapter?.getState)return undefined;
 return app.hidden?null:principal(adapter.getState()?.person);
}
function syncNative(){
 const value=nativeIdentity();if(value===undefined)return false;
 const next=stamp(value);if(next!==nativeStamp){nativeStamp=next;cancelRead();if(!loggingOut)applyIdentity(value)}
 return true;
}
async function readIdentity(force=false){
 if(destroyed||suspended||loggingOut)return null;
 // When polish loads before the retained startup, let that startup perform the
 // canonical identity read. Its subsequent DOM changes reconcile this menu.
 if(!force&&$('#cv-workspace')){syncNative();return actor}
 if(pending)return pending;
 const sequence=++epoch,controller=new AbortController();request=controller;
 requestTimer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),8000);
 const task=(async()=>{
  try{
   const response=await fetch(IDENTITY,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',signal:controller.signal});
   if(sequence!==epoch||destroyed||loggingOut)return null;
   if(!response.ok){applyIdentity(null);return null}
   const data=await response.json();if(sequence!==epoch||destroyed||loggingOut)return null;
   const value=principal(data.person);applyIdentity(value);schedule();return value;
  }catch{if(sequence===epoch&&!destroyed&&!loggingOut)applyIdentity(null);return null}
  finally{if(sequence===epoch){clearTimeout(requestTimer);requestTimer=0;request=null;pending=null}}
 })();pending=task;return task;
}
function findTrigger(){return $('#cv-social-header .cv-account a[data-cva-trigger], #cv-social-header .cv-account a[aria-label^="Open your Communiverse profile"], #cv-social-header .cv-account a.cv-profile-chip')}
function sourceStamp(node){return node?JSON.stringify([node.getAttribute('aria-label'),node.querySelector('img')?.getAttribute('src')||'',node.textContent]):''}
function rememberHeader(){lastHeader=findTrigger();lastHeaderStamp=sourceStamp(lastHeader)}
function avatar(person,className='cva-avatar'){
 const node=create('span',className);if(person.photo){const img=create('img');img.src=person.photo;img.alt='';img.width=44;img.height=44;node.append(img)}else node.textContent=Array.from(person.name.trim()).slice(0,2).join('')||'You';return node;
}
function mountTrigger(){
 const found=findTrigger();if(!found)return;
 if(trigger!==found){dismiss();restoreTrigger();trigger=found;original={role:trigger.getAttribute('role'),'aria-label':trigger.getAttribute('aria-label'),'aria-haspopup':trigger.getAttribute('aria-haspopup'),'aria-expanded':trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded'),'aria-controls':trigger.getAttribute('aria-controls'),id:trigger.getAttribute('id')};
  // Retained social-controls owns labels on .cv-profile-chip. A menu is a
  // different control role: transfer that class while mounted so its observer
  // cannot ping-pong aria-label with ours and starve the browser event loop.
  wasProfileChip=trigger.classList.contains('cv-profile-chip');trigger.classList.remove('cv-profile-chip');
  trigger.classList.add('cva-trigger');trigger.setAttribute('data-cva-trigger','');if(!trigger.id)trigger.id='cv-account-menu-trigger';
 }
 if(!panel)renderMenu();
 const label='Account menu for '+actor.name;if(trigger.getAttribute('aria-label')!==label)trigger.setAttribute('aria-label',label);
 trigger.setAttribute('role','button');trigger.setAttribute('aria-haspopup','menu');trigger.setAttribute('aria-controls','cv-account-menu');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));
 menu?.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',trigger.id);
 let image=trigger.querySelector('.cv-avatar');if(!image){image=avatar(actor,'cv-avatar');trigger.prepend(image)}
 const current=image.querySelector('img');
 if(actor.photo){if(current){if(current.getAttribute('src')!==actor.photo)current.src=actor.photo}else{const img=create('img');img.src=actor.photo;img.alt='';img.width=44;img.height=44;image.replaceChildren(img)}}
 else if(current)image.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(Array.from(actor.name.trim()).slice(0,2).join('')||'You'));
 rememberHeader();
}
function activeDestination(key){
 const url=new URL(location.href);if(![WORKSPACE,WORKSPACE.slice(0,-1)].includes(url.pathname))return false;
 if(key==='profile')return url.searchParams.get('profile')==='edit';
 const view=url.searchParams.get('view'),tools=view==='manage'&&(url.searchParams.get('section')==='tools'||url.searchParams.get('manage')==='tools');
 return key==='tools'?tools:key==='task'?view==='my-work':key==='work'?['projects','work','requests'].includes(view):key==='manage'?view==='manage'&&!tools:false;
}
function renderMenu(){
 if(!actor){clearMenu();return}
 if(!panel){panel=create('div','cva-panel');panel.hidden=true;summary=create('div','cva-summary');menu=create('div','cva-menu');menu.id='cv-account-menu';menu.setAttribute('role','menu');menu.setAttribute('aria-label','Account');panel.append(summary,menu);document.body.append(panel)}
 const info=create('div','cva-account-text');info.append(create('p','cva-name',actor.name));if(actor.title)info.append(create('p','cva-account-detail',actor.title));summary.replaceChildren(avatar(actor),info);
 const rows=destinations(actor).map(([key,label,href])=>{const row=create('a','cva-row');row.href=href;row.setAttribute('role','menuitem');row.tabIndex=-1;row.dataset.cvaAction=key;row.append(icon(key),create('span','',label));if(activeDestination(key))row.setAttribute('aria-current','page');return row});
 const separator=create('div','cva-separator');separator.setAttribute('role','separator');
 const signout=create('button','cva-row','');signout.type='button';signout.setAttribute('role','menuitem');signout.tabIndex=-1;signout.dataset.cvaAction='signout';signout.append(icon('signout'),create('span','','Sign out'));
 items=[...rows,signout];menu.replaceChildren(...rows,separator,signout);menu.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',trigger?.id||'cv-account-menu-trigger');
}
function fit(){
 if(!open||!panel||!trigger)return;
 const viewport=window.visualViewport,left=viewport?.offsetLeft||0,top=viewport?.offsetTop||0,width=viewport?.width||window.innerWidth,height=viewport?.height||window.innerHeight;
 panel.style.maxWidth=Math.max(0,width-16)+'px';panel.style.maxHeight=Math.max(0,height-16)+'px';
 const anchor=trigger.getBoundingClientRect(),box=panel.getBoundingClientRect();
 panel.style.left=Math.max(left+8,Math.min(anchor.right-box.width,left+width-box.width-8))+'px';
 panel.style.top=Math.max(top+8,Math.min(anchor.bottom+8,top+height-box.height-8))+'px';
}
function focusItem(index){const row=items[(index+items.length)%items.length];row?.focus();row?.scrollIntoView?.({block:'nearest'})}

function cleanProfileIntent(){const url=new URL(location.href);if(url.searchParams.get('profile')==='edit'){url.searchParams.delete('profile');history.replaceState(history.state,'',url)}}

function ownsProfile(){return profileOwner===actor?.id&&profileContent===$('#cv-ws-dialog-content')?.firstChild}
async function openProfile(){
 const adapter=window.__cvWorkspaceNative,dialog=$('#cv-ws-dialog');
 if(!actor||!adapter?.profile||!dialog||profileBusy)return false;
 if(dialog.open){if(!ownsProfile())message('Close the current editor to open your profile.');return ownsProfile()}
 profileReturn=trigger;watchProfile(dialog);
 // A history close keeps native fields mounted. Forward reopens that same
 // editor, preserving unsaved input rather than asking profileForm to replace it.
 if(ownsProfile()){dialog.showModal();return true}
 const owner=actor.id;profileBusy=true;
 try{const opened=await adapter.profile();if(opened!==true||owner!==actor?.id||destroyed||loggingOut)return false;profileOwner=owner;profileContent=$('#cv-ws-dialog-content')?.firstChild;return true}
 catch{message('Your profile could not open. Please try again.');return false}
 finally{profileBusy=false}
}
function signOut(){
 if(!actor||loggingOut)return;

 if(button){button.click();return}
 // Public pages do not contain Workspace's private native handler. A durable,
 // account-bound intent hands off to it without duplicating either logout API.
 const url=new URL(WORKSPACE,location.origin);url.searchParams.set('account','signout');url.searchParams.set('account_id',person.id);location.assign(url.href);
}
function syncIntent(fromHistory=false){
 const url=new URL(location.href),intent=url.searchParams.get('profile')==='edit';
 if(url.searchParams.get('account')==='signout'&&window.__cvWorkspaceNative){
  const person=nativeIdentity();if(person===undefined)return;
  if(!person&&$('#cv-ws-login')?.hidden!==false)return;
  url.searchParams.delete('account');const expected=url.searchParams.get('account_id');url.searchParams.delete('account_id');history.replaceState(history.state,'',url);
  if(person&&expected===person.id)signOut();return;
 }
 const dialog=$('#cv-ws-dialog');
 if(intent&&actor&&nativeIdentity()&&!dialog?.open&&(!ownsProfile()||fromHistory))void openProfile();
 else if(!intent&&ownsProfile()&&dialog?.open){historyClosing=true;dialog.close()}
}
function reconcile(){
 scheduled=false;if(destroyed||suspended)return;
 const native=syncNative(),found=findTrigger();
 if(!native&&(found!==lastHeader||sourceStamp(found)!==lastHeaderStamp)){
  // Header replacement/photo updates can accompany an account change. Drop the
  // private menu before reading fresh canonical identity; never trust its text.
  lastHeader=found;lastHeaderStamp=sourceStamp(found);cancelRead();applyIdentity(null);if(found)void readIdentity();
 }else if(actor)mountTrigger();
 syncIntent();
}
function schedule(){if(scheduled||destroyed||suspended)return;scheduled=true;queueMicrotask(reconcile)}
function click(event){
 const target=event.target.closest?.('[data-cva-trigger],[data-cva-action]');if(!target){if(open&&!panel?.contains(event.target))dismiss();return}
 if(event.button>0||event.metaKey||event.ctrlKey||event.shiftKey||event.altKey)return;
 if(target===trigger){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();open?dismiss(true):show();return}
 if(!open||!menu?.contains(target))return;
 const action=target.dataset.cvaAction;
 if(action==='signout'){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();signOut();return}
 if(action==='profile'&&window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.profile){
  event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();dismiss(true);
  const dialog=$('#cv-ws-dialog');if(dialog?.open&&!ownsProfile()){message('Close the current editor to open your profile.');return}
  const url=new URL(location.href);if(url.searchParams.get('profile')!=='edit'){url.searchParams.set('profile','edit');history.pushState(history.state,'',url)}void openProfile();return;
 }
 dismiss(); // Navigation remains a real link, including open-in-new-tab behavior.
}
function keydown(event){
 if(open&&event.key==='Escape'&&event.target===trigger){event.preventDefault();dismiss(true);return}
 if(event.target===trigger&&['Enter',' ','ArrowDown','ArrowUp'].includes(event.key)){
  event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(open&&event.key===' ')dismiss(true);else show(event.key==='ArrowUp');return;
 }
 if(!open||!menu?.contains(event.target))return;
 const index=items.indexOf(event.target.closest?.('[data-cva-action]'));
 if(['ArrowDown','ArrowUp','Home','End'].includes(event.key)){event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();focusItem(event.key==='Home'?0:event.key==='End'?items.length-1:index+(event.key==='ArrowDown'?1:-1));return}
 if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();dismiss(true);return}
 if(event.key==='Tab'){dismiss(true);return} // Native Tab leaves the trigger.
 if(event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();event.target.closest?.('[data-cva-action]')?.click();return}
 if(event.key.length===1&&!event.ctrlKey&&!event.metaKey&&!event.altKey){
  clearTimeout(typeTimer);typeBuffer+=event.key.toLocaleLowerCase();typeTimer=setTimeout(()=>typeBuffer='',500);
  const repeated=[...typeBuffer].every(c=>c===typeBuffer[0]),query=repeated?typeBuffer[0]:typeBuffer;
  for(let offset=1;offset<=items.length;offset++){const next=(index+offset)%items.length;if(items[next].textContent.trim().toLocaleLowerCase().startsWith(query)){event.preventDefault();focusItem(next);break}}
 }
}
function destroy(){destroyed=true;cancelRead();observer?.disconnect();bindings.splice(0).forEach(remove=>remove());clearTimeout(typeTimer);clearMenu();notice?.remove();actor=null;profileOwner='';profileContent=null}
function start(){
 if(destroyed)return;
 rememberHeader();
 listen(document,'click',click,true);listen(document,'keydown',keydown,true);
 listen(document,'pointerdown',event=>{if(open&&!panel?.contains(event.target)&&!trigger?.contains(event.target))dismiss()},true);
 listen(document,'focusin',event=>{if(open&&!panel?.contains(event.target)&&event.target!==trigger)dismiss()});
 listen(window,'resize',fit,{passive:true});listen(window.visualViewport,'resize',fit,{passive:true});listen(window.visualViewport,'scroll',fit,{passive:true});
 listen(window,'popstate',()=>{dismiss();syncIntent(true);renderMenu()});
 listen(window,'cv:account-change',()=>{cancelRead();applyIdentity(null);loggingOut=false;void readIdentity(true)});
 listen(window,'focus',()=>{if(!loggingOut)void readIdentity(true)});
 listen(window,'pagehide',event=>{
  if(!event.persisted){destroy();return}
  suspended=true;cancelRead();clearMenu();actor=null;actorStamp='';profileOwner='';profileContent=null;observer?.disconnect();notice?.remove();notice=null;
 });
 listen(window,'pageshow',()=>{if(suspended&&!destroyed){suspended=false;rememberHeader();observer?.observe(document.body,observation);void readIdentity(true);schedule()}});
 observer=new MutationObserver(records=>{if(records.some(record=>!record.target.closest?.('.cva-panel,.cva-notice')))schedule()});
 observer.observe(document.body,observation);
 void readIdentity();schedule();
}
window.cvAccountMenu=Object.freeze({refresh:()=>{loggingOut=false;return readIdentity(true)},close:()=>dismiss(true),destroy,release:'communiverse-account-menu-20261013'});
if(document.readyState==='loading')listen(document,'DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
})();

;
/* In-house Communiverse Tools. Parent owns navigation, authentication and D1.
 * Load tools.css, then mount({target,identity,onNavigate}) or
 * mountAttachments({target,type:'task'|'project',id,identity}). Both return
 * {destroy}. identity accepts a person, {person}, or a live getter for either.
 * Parent must destroy/remount on account replacement or emit cv-chat-reset /
 * communiverse:identity-change ({identity|person}); a live getter is checked
 * before/after every request, every edit, and while the panel is mounted.
 * onNavigate receives {view,section,document,documentId,url} for a document.
 * Set CommuniverseTools.onAsk = ({documentId,title,prompt}) => ... to open
 * PRIVATE Cosmos. This module never sends a chat message or calls a provider.
 * Optional onPickReference({type,documentId,currentId,signal}) returns a
 * currently authorized {id,permissionChecked:true,preview:{label}}, or null.
 * Preview labels live only in this mount and are cleared with private state.
 * onOpenReference({type,id,documentId}) opens the parent's authorized editor.
 * Mount control.openReference({documentId,rowId,columnId}) opens/highlights
 * only after authorized data loads. These hooks never share or auto-submit.
 * Contract: same-origin tools/{documents,document,save,archive,sheet,columns,
 * rows,cells,attach,search}; paged reads use nextCursor. Save/structure/attach
 * versions belong to documents; cells use their own version (0 for a new cell).
 * Notebook pages are original child documents: GET document lists summaries,
 * and only the selected page is loaded/saved with its own ID and version.
 * Canonical Work choices come from the existing read-only workspace overview;
 * tools/search searches authorized documents, never tasks or contacts.
 * No personal browser persistence, upload API, task copy, or external runtime.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.CommuniverseTools?.mount) return;
  const API = '/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/v4/tools/';
  const KINDS = {notebook:'Notebook',sheet:'Sheet',note:'Sticky note',folder:'Folder'};
  const COLORS = [['blue','Soft blue'],['yellow','Warm yellow'],['green','Sage green'],['pink','Soft rose'],['purple','Soft lavender'],['white','White']];
  const TYPES = [['text','Text'],['number','Number'],['date','Date'],['checkbox','Checkbox'],['email','Email'],['phone','Phone'],['url','URL / social profile'],['select','Single select'],['status','Status'],['currency','Currency'],['percentage','Percentage'],['person','Person / assignee'],['task','Task reference'],['project','Project reference'],['document','Document reference']];
  const REFERENCE_TYPES = ['person','task','project','document'];
  const NUMERIC_TYPES = ['number','currency','percentage'];
  const TEMPLATE_COLUMNS = {
    artist:{label:'Artist onboarding',columns:[['Name','text'],['Email','email'],['Phone','phone'],['Social profile','url'],['Country','text'],['Practice','text'],['Stage','status'],['Assigned ambassador','person'],['Follow-up date','date'],['Notes','text']]},
    contacts:{label:'Contacts',columns:[['Name','text'],['Email','email'],['Phone','phone'],['Website','url'],['Country','text'],['Notes','text']]},
    projects:{label:'Project tracker',columns:[['Project','project'],['Task','task'],['Assignee','person'],['Status','status'],['Due date','date'],['Progress','percentage'],['Notes','text']]}
  };
  const LIMIT = {items:50,rows:50,columns:32,body:60000,cell:2000,config:8192,options:100,drafts:100,depth:13};
  const live = new Set(), hosts = new WeakMap();
  const timeFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'});
  let sequence = 0;
  const array = v => Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  const text = v => typeof v === 'string' ? v : '';
  const validId = v => typeof v === 'string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/.test(v);
  const validVersion = v => Number.isSafeInteger(v) && v >= 1;
  const bytes = v => new TextEncoder().encode(v).byteLength;
  const uuid = () => crypto.randomUUID();
  const clone = v => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
  const isEmpty = v => v===null || v===undefined || v==='';
  const typeLabel = type => TYPES.find(([t]) => t===type)?.[1] || type;
  function columnConfig(raw,type) {
    let config=raw ?? {};if (typeof config==='string') {try {config=JSON.parse(config);}catch {throw Error('Column settings could not be read. No content was changed.');}}
    if (!config || typeof config!=='object' || Array.isArray(config)) throw Error('Column settings could not be read.');
    let result={};
    if (type==='select' || type==='status') {
      const values=config.options ?? [],ids=new Set();
      if (!Array.isArray(values) || values.length>LIMIT.options) throw Error('A column supports up to 100 choices.');
      result={options:values.map(o => {
        if (!validId(o?.id) || ids.has(o.id) || !text(o.label).trim() || bytes(o.label)>120) throw Error('A column contains invalid choices.');
        ids.add(o.id);if (o.color!==undefined && !(typeof o.color==='string' && (['yellow','blue','green','pink','purple','white','grey','gray','red','orange'].includes(o.color) || /^#[a-f0-9]{3}(?:[a-f0-9]{3})?$/i.test(o.color)))) throw Error('Choose a supported choice color.');return {id:o.id,label:o.label,...(o.color!==undefined ? {color:o.color} : {})};
      })};
    }
    if (type==='currency') {
      const currency=text(config.currency);
      if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(currency) || (Intl.supportedValuesOf && !Intl.supportedValuesOf('currency').includes(currency))) throw Error('Choose a supported ISO currency code for this column.');
      result={currency};
    }
    if (type==='percentage') {const scale=config.scale ?? 'percent';if (!['percent','fraction'].includes(scale)) throw Error('Choose percent points or a fraction scale.');result.scale=scale;}
    if (['currency','percentage'].includes(type) && config.precision!==undefined) {if (!Number.isInteger(config.precision) || config.precision<0 || config.precision>6) throw Error('Choose display precision between 0 and 6.');result.precision=config.precision;}
    if (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(type)) for (const key of ['min','max']) if (config[key]!==undefined) {if (typeof config[key]!=='number' || !Number.isFinite(config[key]) || Math.abs(config[key])>1e12) throw Error('Choose a finite numeric bound.');result[key]=config[key];}
    if (result.min!==undefined && result.max!==undefined && result.min>result.max) throw Error('Minimum must not exceed maximum.');
    if (bytes(JSON.stringify(result))>LIMIT.config) throw Error('Shorten the column settings to save.');return result;
  }
  function validateCell(col,raw,badInput=false) {
    const type=col.type;let value=raw,message='';
    if (type==='checkbox') return {value:raw===true,message};
    if (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(type)) {
      value=raw==='' ? null : Number(raw);
      if (badInput || (raw!=='' && (!Number.isFinite(value) || Math.abs(value)>1e12))) message='Enter a finite number between −1 trillion and 1 trillion.';
      else if (raw!=='' && ((col.config?.min!==undefined && value<col.config.min) || (col.config?.max!==undefined && value>col.config.max))) message='Enter a number within this column’s configured bounds.';
    } else if (type==='date') {
      value=raw==='' ? null : raw;
      if (badInput || (raw && (!/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d$/.test(raw) || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(raw+'T00:00:00Z')) || new Date(raw+'T00:00:00Z').toISOString().slice(0,10)!==raw))) message='Enter a valid date (YYYY-MM-DD).';
    } else if (type==='select' || type==='status') {
      value=raw==='' ? null : raw;if (raw && !col.config.options.some(o => o.id===raw)) message='Choose a current option or leave this cell empty.';
    } else if (REFERENCE_TYPES.includes(type)) {
      value=raw==='' ? null : raw;if (raw && !validId(raw)) message='Choose a permitted reference or enter its canonical ID.';
    } else {
      if (typeof raw!=='string' || bytes(raw)>LIMIT.cell) message='This cell is too long. Shorten its text to save.';
      else if (type!=='text' && /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(raw)) message='Remove control characters from this field.';
      else if (type==='email' && raw && (raw.length>254 || raw.split('@')[0].length>64 || !/^[A-Za-z0-9.!#$%&'*+/=?^_`{|}~-]+@[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]*[A-Za-z0-9])?(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]*[A-Za-z0-9])?)*$/.test(raw))) message='Enter an email address in name@domain format (up to 254 characters, 64 before @).';
      else if (type==='phone' && raw && (raw.length>100 || !/^\+?[0-9() .\/#-]+(?:\s*(?:ext\.?|x)\s*[0-9]+)?$/i.test(raw) || !/\d/.test(raw))) message='Enter a phone number with digits and optional country code. Your original formatting is kept.';
      else if (type==='url' && raw) {try {
        const u=new URL(raw);if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(raw) || /\s/.test(raw) || !['http:','https:'].includes(u.protocol) || !u.hostname || u.username || u.password) throw Error();
        const sensitive=key => /(?:token|secret|password|passwd|authorization|credential|signature|apikey|^key$|^auth$)/i.test(key.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'')),fragment=new URLSearchParams(u.hash.slice(1).replace(/^.*\?/,''));
        if ([...u.searchParams.keys(),...fragment.keys()].some(sensitive)) {message='Remove secrets or signed credentials from this URL.';}
      }catch {message='Enter a complete http or https URL without embedded sign-in details.';}}
    }
    return {value,message};
  }
  const abortError = () => Object.assign(Error('Request superseded.'),{name:'AbortError'});
  const element = (tag,cls,content) => {
    const n = document.createElement(tag);if (cls) n.className = cls;
    if (content !== undefined) n.textContent = String(content ?? '');return n;
  };
  function button(label,action,kind = '') {
    const b = element('button','cvt-button'+(kind ? ' cvt-button-'+kind : ''),label);b.type = 'button';
    b.addEventListener('click',() => {if (!b.disabled) Promise.resolve().then(action).catch(() => {});});return b;
  }
  function field(label,name,control = element('input')) {
    const wrap = element('label','cvt-field');wrap.append(element('span','',label));
    control.name = name;control.autocomplete = 'off';wrap.append(control);return {wrap,control};
  }
  function options(select,values) {for (const [value,label] of values) {const o = element('option','',label);o.value=value;select.append(o);}}
  function icon(kind) {
    const svg = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','svg');
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox','0 0 24 24');svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');svg.classList.add('cvt-icon');
    const shapes = {notebook:'M6 3h12a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H6V3ZM6 7H3m3 5H3m3 5H3m7-10h6m-6 5h6',sheet:'M3 3h18v18H3V3Zm0 6h18M9 3v18M3 15h18',note:'M4 3h16v13l-5 5H4V3Zm11 18v-5h5M8 8h8M8 12h6',folder:'M3 6h6l2 2h10v12H3V6Zm0 0V4h6l2 2'};
    const p = document.createElementNS(svg.namespaceURI,'path');p.setAttribute('d',shapes[kind] || shapes.note);
    p.setAttribute('fill','none');p.setAttribute('stroke','currentColor');p.setAttribute('stroke-width','1.5');
    p.setAttribute('stroke-linecap','round');p.setAttribute('stroke-linejoin','round');svg.append(p);return svg;
  }
  function person(source) {try {const p = typeof source === 'function' ? source() : source;return p && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p,'person') ? p.person : p ?? null;} catch {return null;}}
  function date(raw) {
    if (!raw) return 'Time unavailable';
    const normalized = typeof raw === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d:\d\d(?:\.\d{1,3})?$/.test(raw) ? raw.replace(' ','T')+'Z' : raw;
