    const d = new Date(normalized);return Number.isFinite(+d) ? timeFormat.format(d) : 'Time unavailable';
  }
  function projectDocument(raw,full = false) {
    if (!validId(raw?.id) || !KINDS[raw.kind] || !validVersion(raw.version)) throw Error('The document response is incomplete. Refresh to try again.');
    const result = {};
    for (const key of ['id','kind','title','color','version','parent_id','project_id','task_id','updated_by_name','updated_name','updated_at','created_at','created_by_name','can_edit','can_archive','can_attach','ancestor_archived','archived','archived_at','scope']) if (raw[key] !== undefined) result[key]=raw[key];
    result.title = text(result.title).slice(0,160) || 'Untitled '+KINDS[result.kind].toLowerCase();
    result.color = COLORS.some(([c]) => c === result.color) || /^#[a-f0-9]{3}(?:[a-f0-9]{3})?$/i.test(text(result.color)) ? result.color : result.color==='' ? '' : 'blue';
    if (full) {
      if (raw.body != null && typeof raw.body !== 'string') throw Error('This document format is not supported. Its content has not been changed.');
      if (bytes(text(raw.body)) > LIMIT.body) throw Error('This document exceeds the editor limit. Its content has not been changed.');
      result.body = text(raw.body);
    }
    return result;
  }
  function sheetFrom(data) {
    const doc = projectDocument(data.document,true),columns=array(data.columns),rows=array(data.rows),cells=array(data.cells);
    if (!Array.isArray(data.columns) || !Array.isArray(data.rows) || !Array.isArray(data.cells) || columns.length > LIMIT.columns || rows.length > LIMIT.rows || cells.length > LIMIT.rows*LIMIT.columns) throw Error('This sheet exceeds the page limit or has an incomplete response. No content was changed.');
    const unique = entries => {const ids = new Set();for (const e of entries) {if (!validId(e.id) || ids.has(e.id)) throw Error('The sheet contains an invalid row or column.');ids.add(e.id);}return ids;};
    if (data.totalRows!==undefined && data.filteredRows!==undefined && (!Number.isSafeInteger(data.totalRows) || !Number.isSafeInteger(data.filteredRows) || data.filteredRows<rows.length || data.totalRows<data.filteredRows || data.totalRows<0)) throw Error('The sheet counts could not be verified. Retry this view.');
    const colIds=unique(columns),rowIds=unique(rows),cellMap=new Map();
    for (const c of columns) if (!TYPES.some(([t]) => t===c.type) || typeof c.name !== 'string') throw Error('The sheet contains an unsupported column.');
    for (const c of cells) {
      if (!rowIds.has(c.row_id) || !colIds.has(c.column_id)) continue;
      const key=c.row_id+'|'+c.column_id;if (cellMap.has(key)) throw Error('The sheet contains duplicate cells.');
      if ((c.value!==null && !['string','number','boolean'].includes(typeof c.value)) || (typeof c.value==='number' && !Number.isFinite(c.value)) || !validVersion(c.version)) throw Error('The sheet contains an unsupported cell value. No content was changed.');
      const type=columns.find(col => col.id===c.column_id).type,reference=projectReference(c.reference,c.value,type);
      cellMap.set(key,{row_id:c.row_id,column_id:c.column_id,value:reference?.available===false ? null : c.value,version:c.version,...recordedMetadata(c),...(reference ? {reference} : {})});
    }
    return {document:doc,columns:columns.map(c => ({id:c.id,name:c.name.slice(0,120),type:c.type,version:c.version,config:columnConfig(c.config,c.type),...recordedMetadata(c)})),rows:rows.map(r => ({id:r.id,version:r.version,position:r.position,...recordedMetadata(r)})),cells:cellMap,nextCursor:cursor(data.nextCursor),
      totalRows:Number.isSafeInteger(data.totalRows) && data.totalRows>=0 ? data.totalRows : null,filteredRows:Number.isSafeInteger(data.filteredRows) && data.filteredRows>=0 ? data.filteredRows : null};
  }
  function recordedMetadata(raw) {const result={};for (const key of ['created_at','created_by_name','updated_at','updated_by_name']) if (raw[key]!=null) result[key]=raw[key];return result;}
  function projectReference(raw,value,type) {
    if (!REFERENCE_TYPES.includes(type) || !raw) return null;
    if (raw.available!==true) return {type,available:false};
    if (raw.type!==type || raw.id!==value || !validId(raw.id)) throw Error('The reference preview could not be verified.');
    return {type,id:raw.id,available:true,label:text(raw.label).slice(0,160)};
  }
  function cursor(v) {return typeof v === 'string' && v.length <= 1024 ? v : null;}
  function scopeLabel(doc) {return doc.task_id ? 'Shared with task' : doc.project_id ? 'Shared with project' : doc.parent_id ? 'Inherits folder access' : doc.scope==='shared' ? 'Shared workspace scope' : 'Private to you';}
  function editorLabel(doc) {const name=text(doc.updated_by_name) || text(doc.updated_name);return 'Last edited'+(name ? ' by '+name : '')+' · '+date(doc.updated_at)+(name ? '' : ' · Historical editor unavailable');}
  function createdLabel(doc) {return 'Created'+(text(doc.created_by_name) ? ' by '+doc.created_by_name : '')+' · '+date(doc.created_at)+(text(doc.created_by_name) ? '' : ' · Historical author unavailable');}
  function docURL(id,rowId,columnId) {return '/communiverse/workspace/?view=manage&section=tools&document='+encodeURIComponent(id)+(validId(rowId) ? '&row='+encodeURIComponent(rowId) : '')+(validId(columnId) ? '&column='+encodeURIComponent(columnId) : '');}

  function createMount(config,context) {
    const target = typeof config.target === 'string' ? document.querySelector(config.target) : config.target;
    if (!target?.replaceChildren) throw new TypeError('Tools needs a valid in-page target.');
    hosts.get(target)?.destroy();
    const embedded=Boolean(target.closest('.cv5-management'));
    let navigateParent=config.onNavigate;
    let identity=config.identity,actor=person(identity),actorId=validId(actor?.id) ? actor.id : '';
    config=null;
    let alive=true,locked=false,generation=0,entry=null,notebookContext=null,records=[],next=null,trail=[],listCursor=null,cursors=[],query='',modal=null,archivedView=false;
    const controllers=new Set(),lanes=new Map(),uid='cvt-'+(++sequence),root=element('section','cvt-tools'+(embedded ? ' cvt-tools-embedded' : '')+(context ? ' cvt-attachments' : ''));
    root.setAttribute('aria-label',context ? 'Task or project documents' : 'Communiverse Tools');root.dataset.instance=uid;
    const head=element('header','cvt-header'),crumbs=element('nav','cvt-breadcrumbs'),status=element('p','cvt-status'),content=element('div','cvt-content');
    crumbs.setAttribute('aria-label','Tools location');status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');status.setAttribute('aria-atomic','true');
    root.append(head,crumbs,status,content);target.replaceChildren(root);
    const control = {destroy,openReference},privateMount={control,id:actorId,lock};live.add(privateMount);hosts.set(target,control);
    async function openReference(reference) {
      const id=reference?.documentId || reference?.document,rowId=reference?.rowId || reference?.row,columnId=reference?.columnId || reference?.column;
      if (!checkIdentity() || !validId(id)) return;
      if (entry?.doc.id===id && entry.sheet?.rows.some(r => r.id===rowId)) {entry.selectedReference={rowId,columnId:validId(columnId) ? columnId : null};highlightReference(entry,true);return;}
      if (dirty()) {notice('Save or resolve the current draft before opening another reference. No save was requested.',true);return;}
      return openDocument(id,true,reference);
    }
    function notice(message,error=false) {if (!alive || locked) return;status.textContent=message;status.classList.toggle('is-error',error);}
    function abortAll() {for (const c of controllers) c.abort();controllers.clear();lanes.clear();}
    function abortLane(lane) {lanes.get(lane)?.abort();lanes.delete(lane);}
    function stopTimers(e) {if (e) {clearTimeout(e.debounce);clearTimeout(e.maximum);e.debounce=null;e.maximum=null;}}
    function purge() {
      stopTimers(entry);if (entry) {entry.doc={};entry.draft={title:'',body:'',color:''};entry.cellDrafts.clear();entry.referencePreviews.clear();entry.columnWidths.clear();entry.columnOrder=[];entry.view={};entry.selectedReference=null;entry.setupPending=null;entry.sheet=null;entry.pending=null;entry.structurePending=null;entry.conflict=null;entry.error='';}
      for (const input of root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select')) {input.value='';if (input.type==='checkbox') input.checked=false;}
      for (const n of content.querySelectorAll('*')) if (!n.children.length) n.textContent='';
      content.replaceChildren();head.replaceChildren();crumbs.replaceChildren();status.replaceChildren();
      entry=null;notebookContext=null;records=[];trail=[];cursors=[];listCursor=null;next=null;query='';actor=null;actorId='';navigateParent=null;
    }
    function revoke(reason) {const id=actorId;for (const mounted of live) if (mounted.id===id) mounted.lock(reason);}
    function closeModal(value=null) {if (!modal) return;const m=modal;modal=null;for (const input of m.dialog.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select')) input.value='';m.dialog.close();m.dialog.remove();m.resolve(value);if (m.focus?.isConnected) m.focus.focus();}
    function lock(reason) {
      if (!alive || locked) return;locked=true;generation++;abortAll();closeModal();purge();identity=null;context=null;privateMount.id='';clearInterval(identityTimer);
      root.replaceChildren(element('div','cvt-empty',reason || 'Your session changed. Sign in again to reopen Tools.'));
    }
    function checkIdentity() {
      if (!alive || locked) return false;
      const now=person(identity);if (!actorId || now?.id!==actorId || now?.status==='inactive') {revoke('Your session changed. Sign in again to reopen Tools.');return false;}return true;
    }
    async function request(endpoint,params={},payload,lane) {
      if (!checkIdentity()) throw abortError();
      if (!['documents','document','save','archive','sheet','columns','rows','cells','attach','search','work-overview'].includes(endpoint) || (endpoint==='work-overview' && payload!==undefined)) throw Error('Unsupported Tools operation.');
      const encoded=payload===undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(payload);
      if (encoded!==undefined && bytes(encoded)>65536) throw Error('This text is too long to save. Shorten it and try again.');
      const gen=generation,controller=new AbortController();controllers.add(controller);
      if (lane) {abortLane(lane);lanes.set(lane,controller);}
      const timeout=setTimeout(() => controller.abort(),18000);
      const search=new URLSearchParams();for (const [key,value] of Object.entries(params)) if (value!==undefined && value!==null && value!=='') search.set(key,String(value));
      try {
        const path=endpoint==='work-overview' ? '/communiverse/api/workspace/overview' : API+endpoint;
        const response=await fetch(path+(search.size ? '?'+search.toString() : ''),{
          method:payload===undefined ? 'GET' : 'POST',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',redirect:'error',signal:controller.signal,
          headers:{Accept:'application/json',...(payload===undefined ? {} : {'Content-Type':'application/json'})},...(payload===undefined ? {} : {body:encoded})
        });
        if (!alive || locked || gen!==generation || (lane && lanes.get(lane)!==controller) || !checkIdentity()) throw abortError();
        if (response.status===401 || response.status===403) {revoke(response.status===401 ? 'Your session expired. Sign in again to reopen Tools.' : 'Tools access changed. Private content has been cleared.');throw abortError();}
        let data;try {data=await response.json();} catch {throw Error('The response could not be read. Retry to confirm whether your change was saved.');}
        if (!alive || locked || gen!==generation || (lane && lanes.get(lane)!==controller) || !checkIdentity()) throw abortError();
        if ((data.person?.id && data.person.id!==actorId) || (data.identity?.id && data.identity.id!==actorId)) {revoke('Your account changed. Private content has been cleared.');throw abortError();}
        if (!response.ok) throw Object.assign(Error(text(data.error).slice(0,400) || 'This action could not finish. Try again.'),{status:response.status,code:data.code});
        return data;
      } catch (error) {
        if (!alive || locked || gen!==generation || (lane && lanes.get(lane)!==controller) || !checkIdentity()) throw abortError();
        if (error.name==='AbortError') throw Error('The request timed out. Retry to confirm whether your change was saved.');throw error;
      } finally {clearTimeout(timeout);controllers.delete(controller);if (lane && lanes.get(lane)===controller) lanes.delete(lane);}
    }
    function beginView() {generation++;abortAll();closeModal();stopTimers(entry);entry=null;notebookContext=null;records=[];next=null;content.replaceChildren(element('p','cvt-loading','Loading…'));}
    function header() {
      const title=element('div','cvt-heading');
      if (!context && !embedded) title.append(element('p','cvt-kicker','Manage Communiverse'));
      title.append(element(context ? 'h3' : embedded ? 'h2' : 'h1','',context ? 'Documents & folders' : 'Tools'));
      if (!context) title.append(element('p','cvt-subtitle','A place for your notebooks, sheets and little ideas.'));
      const actions=element('div','cvt-actions');if (!archivedView) actions.append(button('New tool',createDialog,'primary'));
      if (context) actions.append(button('Attach existing',attachExisting));
      actions.append(button('Refresh',() => navigate(async () => entry ? openDocument(entry.doc.id,false) : loadLibrary(false))));
      actions.append(button(archivedView ? 'Active tools' : 'Archived tools',() => navigate(async () => {archivedView=!archivedView;trail=[];query='';await loadLibrary();}),'quiet'));
      head.replaceChildren(title,actions);breadcrumbs();
    }
    function breadcrumbs() {
      crumbs.replaceChildren();if (!trail.length && !entry) {crumbs.hidden=true;return;}crumbs.hidden=false;
      crumbs.append(button(context ? 'Attachments' : archivedView ? 'Archived tools' : 'All tools',() => navigate(async () => {trail=[];query='';cursors=[];listCursor=null;await loadLibrary();}),'quiet'));
      for (let i=0;i<trail.length;i++) {const item=trail[i];crumbs.append(element('span','cvt-divider','/'),button(item.title,() => navigate(async () => {trail=trail.slice(0,i+1);await openDocument(item.id,false);}),'quiet'));}
      if (notebookContext && notebookContext.doc.id!==entry?.doc.id) crumbs.append(element('span','cvt-divider','/'),button(notebookContext.doc.title,() => navigate(() => openDocument(notebookContext.doc.id)),'quiet'));
      if (entry?.doc.kind!=='folder') crumbs.append(element('span','cvt-divider','/'),element('span','cvt-crumb-current',entry?.doc.title || 'Document'));
    }
    function dirty(e=entry) {return !!e && (e.contentDirty || e.cellDrafts.size>0 || !!e.pending || !!e.structurePending || !!e.setupPending);}
    function modalForm(title,build,submitLabel,onSubmit) {
      if (!checkIdentity()) return Promise.resolve(null);closeModal();
      const d=element('dialog','cvt-dialog'),form=element('form'),heading=element('h3','',title),body=element('div','cvt-dialog-body'),error=element('p','cvt-dialog-error'),actions=element('div','cvt-actions');error.tabIndex=-1;
      const headingId=uid+'-dialog';heading.id=headingId;d.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',headingId);error.setAttribute('role','alert');
      const cancel=button('Cancel',() => closeModal()),submit=element('button','cvt-button cvt-button-primary',submitLabel);submit.type='submit';
      actions.append(cancel,submit);form.append(heading,body,error,actions);d.append(form);const focus=document.activeElement;
      return new Promise(resolve => {
        modal={dialog:d,resolve,focus};root.append(d);build(body,submit);
        d.addEventListener('cancel',e => {e.preventDefault();closeModal();});
        form.addEventListener('submit',async ev => {
          ev.preventDefault();if (!checkIdentity() || submit.disabled) return;submit.disabled=true;error.textContent='';d.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');
          try {const value=await onSubmit(body);if (modal?.dialog===d) closeModal(value===undefined ? true : value);}
          catch (err) {if (modal?.dialog===d && err.name!=='AbortError') {error.textContent=err.message;error.focus();}}
          finally {if (d.isConnected) {submit.disabled=false;d.removeAttribute('aria-busy');}}
        });d.showModal();
      });
    }
    async function navigate(action) {
      if (!checkIdentity()) return;
      if (dirty()) {
        await flush();if (!checkIdentity()) return;
        if (dirty()) {
          const discard=await modalForm('Leave this draft?',body => body.append(element('p','','Some changes are not saved. You can keep editing, or discard this draft and open the other view.')), 'Discard draft & open',() => true);
          if (!discard) return;
        }
      }
      await action();
    }
    function documentLink(doc) {
      const a=element('a','cvt-document-link',doc.title);a.href=docURL(doc.id);
      a.addEventListener('click',ev => {if (ev.button || ev.metaKey || ev.ctrlKey || ev.shiftKey || ev.altKey) return;ev.preventDefault();navigate(() => openDocument(doc.id));});return a;
    }
    function card(doc) {
      const article=element('article','cvt-card');article.dataset.kind=doc.kind;article.dataset.color=doc.color;
      const type=element('div','cvt-card-type');type.append(icon(doc.kind),element('span','',KINDS[doc.kind]));
      const title=element('h3');title.append(documentLink(doc));article.append(type,title,element('p','cvt-scope',scopeLabel(doc)),element('p','cvt-edited',editorLabel(doc)));return article;
    }
    function listRows(data) {
      const rows=array(data.documents ?? data.items ?? data.results);
      if (rows.length>LIMIT.items) throw Error('This list exceeds the page limit. Narrow your search to load a smaller page.');
      const seen=new Set();return rows.map(r => projectDocument(r)).filter(d => !seen.has(d.id) && seen.add(d.id));
    }
    function browseContent() {
      const pane=element('section','cvt-library');pane.setAttribute('aria-label','Tools library');
      if (!context || trail.length) {
        const form=element('form','cvt-search'),search=field('Find a document or folder','q');search.control.type='search';search.control.maxLength=160;search.control.value=query;search.control.placeholder='Search your tools…';
        const submit=element('button','cvt-button','Search');submit.type='submit';form.append(search.wrap,submit);
        form.addEventListener('submit',e => {e.preventDefault();navigate(async () => {query=search.control.value.trim();cursors=[];listCursor=null;await loadLibrary(false);});});pane.append(form);
      }
      const grid=element('div','cvt-grid');for (const doc of records) grid.append(card(doc));
      if (!records.length) {const empty=element('div','cvt-empty');empty.append(element('h3','',query ? 'No tools found' : trail.length ? 'Room for your next idea' : context ? 'Keep the work together' : 'Your tools, all together'),element('p','',query ? 'Try another title or search term.' : context ? 'Attach an existing document or create one for this '+context.type+'.' : 'Create a notebook, a sheet, a sticky note or a folder. New tools are private unless you link them to shared work.'));grid.append(empty);}
      pane.append(grid);
      const pages=element('div','cvt-pagination');pages.append(element('span','',records.length+' tools on this page'));
      if (cursors.length) pages.append(button('Previous page',() => navigate(async () => {listCursor=cursors.pop();await loadLibrary(false);})));
      if (next) {const b=button('Next page',() => navigate(async () => {if (cursors.length>=20) {notice('Refine your search to continue beyond 20 pages.',true);return;}cursors.push(listCursor);listCursor=next;await loadLibrary(false);}));pages.append(b);}
      pane.append(pages);return pane;
    }
    async function loadLibrary(reset=true) {
      if (!checkIdentity()) return;
      const folder=entry?.doc.kind==='folder' ? entry : null;
      if (!folder) beginView();else content.querySelector('.cvt-library')?.replaceChildren(element('p','cvt-loading','Loading…'));
      if (reset) {listCursor=null;cursors=[];}
      header();const gen=generation,parentId=trail.at(-1)?.id;
      try {
        const params={...(parentId ? {parent_id:parentId} : context ? {[context.type+'_id']:context.id} : {}),cursor:listCursor,archived:archivedView ? '1' : '0',limit:LIMIT.items};
        const data=await request(query ? 'search' : 'documents',query ? {...params,q:query,type:'document'} : params);
        if (gen!==generation) return;records=listRows(data);next=cursor(data.nextCursor);
        if (folder) {const old=content.querySelector('.cvt-library');if (old) old.replaceWith(browseContent());else content.append(browseContent());}
        else content.replaceChildren(browseContent());notice('');
      } catch (error) {if (error.name!=='AbortError') {notice(error.message,true);const b=button('Retry loading',() => loadLibrary(false));if (folder) content.append(b);else content.replaceChildren(b);}}
    }
    function makeEntry(doc,sheet) {
      return {doc,draft:{title:doc.title,body:doc.body || '',color:doc.color},sheet:sheet || null,
        cellDrafts:new Map(),cellSeq:0,contentDirty:false,revision:0,pending:null,sending:null,error:'',structurePending:null,structureBusy:false,structureError:'',setupPending:null,setupBusy:false,conflict:null,debounce:null,maximum:null,sheetCursor:null,sheetCursors:[],composition:false,
        view:{query:'',searchColumn:'',column:'',operator:'empty',value:'',sort:'',direction:'asc'},columnWidths:new Map(),columnOrder:[],referencePreviews:new Map(),selectedReference:null};
    }
    async function openDocument(id,notify=true,reference=null) {
      if (!validId(id) || !checkIdentity()) return;beginView();header();const gen=generation;
      try {
        const data=await request('document',{id}),doc=projectDocument(data.document,true);
        if (doc.id!==id) throw Error('The requested document could not be verified.');
        const rowId=reference?.rowId || reference?.row,columnId=reference?.columnId || reference?.column;
        let sheet,sheetCursor=null,sheetCursors=[],referenceView=false;
        if (doc.kind==='sheet') {
          sheet=sheetFrom(data.sheet || await request('sheet',{id,limit:LIMIT.rows}));
          // Canonical row selection uses the authorized bounded query API.
          // Missing IDs never create rows or reveal inaccessible previews.
          if (validId(rowId) && (!columnId || sheet.columns.some(c => c.id===columnId))) {
            if (!sheet.rows.some(r => r.id===rowId)) {
              try {sheet=sheetFrom(await request('sheet',{id,row_ids:JSON.stringify([rowId]),limit:LIMIT.rows}));referenceView=true;}
              catch (err) {if (err.status!==404) throw err;}
            }
          }
        }
        let parentData;
        if (doc.kind==='note' && doc.parent_id) {parentData=await request('document',{id:doc.parent_id});if (parentData.document?.kind!=='notebook') parentData=null;}
        if (gen!==generation) return;entry=makeEntry(sheet?.document || doc,sheet);
        entry.sheetCursor=sheetCursor;entry.sheetCursors=sheetCursors;entry.referenceView=referenceView;
        if (sheet && validId(rowId)) entry.selectedReference={rowId,columnId:validId(columnId) ? columnId : null};
        if (doc.kind==='notebook' || parentData) {
          const source=parentData || data;notebookContext={doc:projectDocument(source.document,true),pages:listRows({documents:source.pages || []}),next:cursor(source.nextCursor),cursor:null,cursors:[]};
        }
        if (doc.kind==='folder') {
          const at=trail.findIndex(p => p.id===id);if (at>=0) trail=trail.slice(0,at+1);else {if (trail.length>=LIMIT.depth) throw Error('Folder nesting is limited to 12 levels. Open this folder from All tools.');trail.push({id,title:doc.title});}
          query='';listCursor=null;cursors=[];
        }
        renderEditor();header();notice('');if (entry.selectedReference) highlightReference(entry,true);
        if (notify && typeof navigateParent==='function') navigateParent({view:'manage',section:'tools',document:id,documentId:id,...(entry.selectedReference ? {row:rowId,column:entry.selectedReference.columnId} : {}),url:docURL(id,rowId,columnId)});
        if (doc.kind==='folder') await loadLibrary();
      } catch (error) {if (error.name!=='AbortError') {entry=null;notice(error.message,true);content.replaceChildren(button('Retry opening',() => openDocument(id,false)),button('Back to tools',() => {trail=[];loadLibrary();}));}}
    }
    function canEdit(e=entry) {return !!e && e.doc.can_edit!==false && !e.doc.archived && !e.doc.archived_at && !e.structurePending && !e.viewBusy;}
    function editorHead(e) {
      const wrap=element('div','cvt-editor-head'),title=field(notebookContext && notebookContext.doc.id!==e.doc.id ? 'Page title' : KINDS[e.doc.kind]+' title','title');title.control.value=e.draft.title;title.control.maxLength=160;title.control.readOnly=!canEdit(e);
      const help=element('p','cvt-field-error');help.id=uid+'-title-error';title.control.setAttribute('aria-describedby',help.id);
      title.control.addEventListener('input',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.draft.title=title.control.value;help.textContent=e.draft.title.trim() ? '' : 'Add a title to save this document.';title.control.setAttribute('aria-invalid',String(!e.draft.title.trim()));changed(e);});composition(title.control,e);title.wrap.append(help);
      const meta=element('div','cvt-meta');meta.append(element('span','cvt-scope',scopeLabel(e.doc)),element('p','cvt-created',createdLabel(e.doc)),element('p','cvt-edited',editorLabel(e.doc)));
      const actions=element('div','cvt-actions cvt-document-actions'),details=element('details','cvt-document-options'),summary=element('summary','cvt-button cvt-button-quiet','Document options'),options=element('div','cvt-document-option-list');
      details.append(summary,options);
      details.addEventListener('keydown',event => {if (event.key==='Escape' && details.open && !event.isComposing) {event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();details.open=false;summary.focus();}});
      const ask=button('Ask Cosmos',async () => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;const hook=window.CommuniverseTools.onAsk;if (typeof hook!=='function') {notice('Private Cosmos is not connected yet.');return;}try {await hook({documentId:e.doc.id,title:e.doc.title,prompt:'Help me with this '+KINDS[e.doc.kind].toLowerCase()+': '+e.doc.title});} catch {notice('Private Cosmos could not open. Try again.',true);}});
      ask.disabled=typeof window.CommuniverseTools.onAsk!=='function';if (ask.disabled) ask.title='Private Cosmos integration is not connected yet.';actions.append(ask);
      const link=element('a','cvt-button cvt-button-quiet','Document link');link.href=docURL(e.doc.id);options.append(link);
      if (canEdit(e) && e.doc.can_attach!==false) {options.append(button('Link to work',linkToWork));options.append(button('Move to folder',moveToFolder));}
      if (canEdit(e) && e.doc.can_archive!==false) options.append(button('Archive',archiveDialog,'danger'));
      if (e.doc.archived_at && e.doc.can_archive!==false && !e.doc.ancestor_archived) options.append(button('Restore',restoreDialog,'primary'));
      actions.append(details);
      wrap.append(title.wrap,meta,actions);return wrap;
    }
    function renderEditor() {
      const e=entry;if (!e || !checkIdentity()) return;
      const editor=element('section','cvt-editor');editor.dataset.kind=notebookContext ? 'notebook' : e.doc.kind;editor.dataset.color=e.draft.color;editor.append(editorHead(e));
      const saving=element('div','cvt-savebar');saving.setAttribute('role','status');saving.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');saving.setAttribute('aria-atomic','true');editor.append(saving);
      const conflict=element('div','cvt-conflict');editor.append(conflict);
      if (notebookContext) editor.append(notebook(e));
      else if (e.doc.kind==='note') {
        const colors=element('fieldset','cvt-colors');colors.append(element('legend','','Note color'));
        for (const [value,label] of COLORS) {const l=element('label','cvt-color'),input=element('input');input.type='radio';input.name=uid+'-color';input.value=value;input.checked=e.draft.color===value;input.disabled=!canEdit(e);l.dataset.color=value;l.append(input,element('span','',label));colors.append(l);input.addEventListener('change',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.draft.color=value;editor.dataset.color=value;changed(e);});}editor.append(colors);
        const body=field('Your note','body',element('textarea'));body.control.value=e.draft.body;body.control.maxLength=LIMIT.body;body.control.readOnly=!canEdit(e);body.control.rows=9;body.control.placeholder='Capture a thought…';
        body.control.addEventListener('input',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.draft.body=body.control.value;changed(e);});composition(body.control,e);editor.append(body.wrap);
      }
      else if (e.doc.kind==='sheet') editor.append(sheetView(e));
      else editor.append(element('p','cvt-subtitle','Documents created here inherit this folder’s access. Linking a folder shares its contents with that work.'));
      content.replaceChildren(editor);saveStatus(e);renderConflict(e);
    }
    function composition(control,e) {control.addEventListener('compositionstart',() => {e.composition=true;stopTimers(e);});control.addEventListener('compositionend',() => {e.composition=false;schedule(e);});}
    function notebook(e) {
      const wrap=element('div','cvt-notebook'),aside=element('nav','cvt-pages');aside.setAttribute('aria-label','Notebook pages');
      const book=notebookContext,overview=button('Overview',() => navigate(() => openDocument(book.doc.id)),'quiet');overview.setAttribute('aria-current',String(e.doc.id===book.doc.id));aside.append(overview);
      for (const page of book.pages) {const b=button(page.title,() => navigate(() => openDocument(page.id)),'quiet');b.setAttribute('aria-current',String(page.id===e.doc.id));aside.append(b);}
      if (book.doc.can_edit!==false && !book.doc.archived_at && !book.doc.ancestor_archived) aside.append(button('Add page',addPage));
      if (book.cursors.length) aside.append(button('Previous pages',() => notebookPage(false),'quiet'));
      if (book.next) aside.append(button('Next pages',() => notebookPage(true),'quiet'));wrap.append(aside);
      const pane=element('section','cvt-page'),body=field(e.doc.id===book.doc.id ? 'Notebook overview' : 'Page notes','body',element('textarea'));body.control.value=e.draft.body;body.control.maxLength=LIMIT.body;body.control.rows=16;body.control.readOnly=!canEdit(e);
      body.control.addEventListener('input',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.draft.body=body.control.value;changed(e);});composition(body.control,e);
      pane.append(element('p','cvt-page-version',(e.doc.id===book.doc.id ? 'Notebook overview' : 'Notebook page')+' · version '+e.doc.version),body.wrap);wrap.append(pane);return wrap;
    }
    async function notebookPage(forward) {
      if (!notebookContext || !await readyToMutate()) return;const book=notebookContext;
      const selected=forward ? book.next : book.cursors.at(-1);if (forward && (!selected || book.cursors.length>=20)) return;
      try {const data=await request('documents',{parent_id:book.doc.id,cursor:selected,limit:LIMIT.items});if (book!==notebookContext) return;const pages=listRows(data);if (forward) book.cursors.push(book.cursor);else book.cursors.pop();book.cursor=selected || null;book.pages=pages;book.next=cursor(data.nextCursor);renderEditor();}catch (error) {if (error.name!=='AbortError') notice(error.message,true);}
    }
    async function addPage() {
      if (!notebookContext || !await readyToMutate()) return;const book=notebookContext,title=field('Page title','page_title');title.control.required=true;title.control.maxLength=160;title.control.value='New page';let previous='',requestId='';
      const saved=await modalForm('Add a notebook page',body => body.append(title.wrap,element('p','','This page inherits the notebook’s permissions.')),'Create page',async () => {
        const name=title.control.value.trim();if (name!==previous) {previous=name;requestId=uuid();}const data=await request('save',{}, {kind:'note',title:name,body:'',color:'white',parent_id:book.doc.id,version:0,requestId});return projectDocument(data.document,true);
      });if (saved?.id && checkIdentity()) await openDocument(saved.id);
    }
    function sheetView(e) {
      const wrap=element('section','cvt-sheet'),actions=element('div','cvt-actions');
      if (canEdit(e) && !e.setupPending) {actions.append(button('Add column',() => columnDialog()));actions.append(button('Add row',() => changeStructure('rows',{action:'add'})));}
      wrap.append(actions,element('p','cvt-sheet-help','Tab follows fields and actions. Enter moves down a row; select fields retain native selection keys. Alt + arrow keys move between cells. Escape restores an unsaved cell. Reference choices check access; they do not share documents.'));
      if (e.setupPending) {
        const setup=e.setupPending,panel=element('div','cvt-template-progress');panel.setAttribute('role','status');
        panel.append(element('p','',setup.label+': '+setup.next+' of '+setup.columns.length+' empty columns confirmed. No records are added.'));if (e.structureError) panel.append(element('p','cvt-field-error',e.structureError));
        if (!e.setupBusy) {panel.append(button('Resume template',resumeTemplate));if (!e.structurePending) panel.append(button('Keep confirmed columns & stop',() => {const count=setup.next;e.setupPending=null;renderEditor();notice(count+' confirmed columns kept. Template setup stopped.');}));}
        wrap.append(panel);
      }
      if (e.referenceView) {const group=element('div','cvt-actions');group.append(element('p','cvt-query-help','Showing the referenced row from this permitted sheet.'),button('Show all rows',async () => {if (!await readyToMutate()) return;await loadSheetView(e);}));wrap.append(group);}
      if (!e.sheet.columns.length) {wrap.append(element('div','cvt-empty','Add a column or choose an empty template to start your sheet.'));if (canEdit(e) && !e.setupPending && !e.sheet.rows.length) wrap.append(button('Choose an empty template',templateDialog));return wrap;}
      wrap.append(sheetQueryControls(e));
      const columns=e.columnOrder.length ? [...e.sheet.columns].sort((a,b) => {const ai=e.columnOrder.indexOf(a.id),bi=e.columnOrder.indexOf(b.id);return (ai<0 ? LIMIT.columns : ai)-(bi<0 ? LIMIT.columns : bi);}) : e.sheet.columns;
      const scroll=element('div','cvt-table-scroll');scroll.tabIndex=0;scroll.setAttribute('role','region');scroll.setAttribute('aria-label','Editable sheet; scroll horizontally for more columns');
      const table=element('table','cvt-table'),caption=element('caption','cvt-sr-only',e.doc.title+' · '+e.sheet.rows.length+' rows on this page');table.append(caption);
      const thead=element('thead'),tr=element('tr');const n=element('th','','Row');n.scope='col';tr.append(n);
      for (const column of columns) {const th=element('th');th.scope='col';const width=e.columnWidths.get(column.id);if (width) {th.style.width=width+'px';th.style.minWidth=width+'px';}th.append(element('span','cvt-column-name',column.name),element('span','cvt-column-type',typeLabel(column.type)+(column.type==='currency' ? ' · '+column.config.currency : '')));if (canEdit(e) && !e.setupPending) {const b=button('Edit',() => columnDialog(column),'quiet');b.setAttribute('aria-label','Edit column '+column.name);th.append(b);}tr.append(th);}
      if (canEdit(e)) {const th=element('th','','Actions');th.scope='col';tr.append(th);}thead.append(tr);table.append(thead);
      const tbody=element('tbody');for (let rowIndex=0;rowIndex<e.sheet.rows.length;rowIndex++) {
        const row=e.sheet.rows[rowIndex],line=element('tr'),th=element('th','',String(rowIndex+1));line.dataset.rowId=row.id;th.scope='row';th.tabIndex=-1;line.append(th);
        for (let colIndex=0;colIndex<columns.length;colIndex++) {
          const col=columns[colIndex],key=row.id+'|'+col.id,cell=e.sheet.cells.get(key),draft=e.cellDrafts.get(key),td=element('td','cvt-cell'),input=element(col.type==='select' || col.type==='status' ? 'select' : 'input','cvt-cell-control');
          td.dataset.cell=key;td.tabIndex=-1;const width=e.columnWidths.get(col.id);if (width) {td.style.width=width+'px';td.style.minWidth=width+'px';}
          if (input.tagName==='SELECT') {options(input,[['','Empty'],...col.config.options.map(o => [o.id,o.label])]);const current=draft ? draft.raw : cell?.value;if (!isEmpty(current) && !col.config.options.some(o => o.id===current)) {const o=element('option','','Unavailable choice');o.value=current;o.disabled=true;input.append(o);}}
          else input.type=col.type==='checkbox' ? 'checkbox' : NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type) ? 'number' : ['date','email','url'].includes(col.type) ? col.type : col.type==='phone' ? 'tel' : 'text';
          input.name='cell-'+row.id+'-'+col.id;input.autocomplete='off';input.dataset.row=String(rowIndex);input.dataset.column=String(colIndex);input.dataset.cell=key;input.setAttribute('aria-label',col.name+', row '+(rowIndex+1));input.disabled=!canEdit(e) || !!e.setupPending;
          if (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type)) {input.step='any';input.inputMode='decimal';if (col.config.min!==undefined) input.min=String(col.config.min);if (col.config.max!==undefined) input.max=String(col.config.max);}else if (col.type==='email') input.inputMode='email';else if (col.type==='url') input.inputMode='url';else if (col.type==='phone') input.inputMode='tel';
          if (input.tagName==='INPUT' && !NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type)) input.maxLength=col.type==='email' ? 254 : LIMIT.cell;
          if (REFERENCE_TYPES.includes(col.type)) {input.readOnly=true;input.setAttribute('aria-label',col.name+', row '+(rowIndex+1)+', canonical reference; use Choose reference');}
          const value=draft ? draft.raw : cell?.value;setCellInput(input,col.type,value);
          const error=element('span','cvt-cell-error');error.id=uid+'-cell-'+rowIndex+'-'+colIndex;error.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');input.setAttribute('aria-describedby',error.id);const recordedError=!draft && !isEmpty(value) && col.type==='date' && validateCell(col,value).message;error.textContent=draft?.error || recordedError || '';input.setAttribute('aria-invalid',String(!!draft?.error || !!recordedError));if (recordedError) error.textContent+=' Recorded value: '+String(value);
          input.addEventListener(col.type==='checkbox' || input.tagName==='SELECT' ? 'change' : 'input',() => editCell(e,row,col,input,error));composition(input,e);input.addEventListener('keydown',ev => {
            if (ev.isComposing) return;
            if (ev.key==='Escape' && !e.pending && !e.sending) {ev.preventDefault();e.cellDrafts.delete(key);setCellInput(input,col.type,e.sheet.cells.get(key)?.value);error.textContent='';input.setAttribute('aria-invalid','false');refreshCellExtras(e,key);saveStatus(e);return;}
            // Native selects use Enter to open/choose. Alt + arrows still
            // provides deterministic table navigation for every field type.
            if (ev.key==='Enter' && input.tagName==='SELECT') return;
            if (ev.key!=='Enter' && !(ev.altKey && ['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(ev.key))) return;
            ev.preventDefault();let r=rowIndex,c=colIndex;if (ev.key==='Enter') r+=ev.shiftKey ? -1 : 1;else if (ev.key==='ArrowUp') r--;else if (ev.key==='ArrowDown') r++;else if (ev.key==='ArrowLeft') c--;else c++;
            scroll.querySelector('.cvt-cell-control[data-row="'+r+'"][data-column="'+c+'"]')?.focus();
          });td.append(input,error);
          const extras=element('div','cvt-cell-extras');extras.addEventListener('focusout',() => Promise.resolve().then(() => {if (entry===e && extras.isConnected) refreshCellExtras(e,key);}));td.append(extras);cellExtras(e,row,col,input,error,extras);
          if (e.conflict && draft) {td.append(element('span','cvt-remote-value','Current saved value: '+displayCell(cell?.value,col)),button('Use saved cell',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.cellDrafts.delete(key);setCellInput(input,col.type,cell?.value);error.textContent='';input.setAttribute('aria-invalid','false');td.querySelector('.cvt-remote-value')?.remove();refreshCellExtras(e,key);saveStatus(e);}));}
          line.append(td);
        }
        if (canEdit(e)) {const td=element('td');const b=button('Remove',() => removeRow(row),'danger');b.setAttribute('aria-label','Remove row '+(rowIndex+1));td.append(b);line.append(td);}tbody.append(line);
      }
      table.append(tbody);scroll.append(table);wrap.append(scroll);
      if (!e.sheet.rows.length) wrap.append(element('p','cvt-empty','No rows yet. Add your first row.'));
      const pager=element('div','cvt-pagination');pager.append(element('span','',e.sheet.rows.length+' loaded rows'+(e.sheet.filteredRows!==null && e.sheet.totalRows!==null ? ' · '+e.sheet.filteredRows+' matching / '+e.sheet.totalRows+' total permitted rows' : ' · Total unavailable')));if (e.sheetCursors.length) pager.append(button('Previous rows',() => sheetPage(false)));if (e.sheet.nextCursor) pager.append(button('Next rows',() => sheetPage(true)));wrap.append(pager);return wrap;
    }
    function sheetParams(e,cursorValue=e.sheetCursor) {
      const v=e.view,filters=[];
      if (v.query && validId(v.searchColumn)) filters.push({column_id:v.searchColumn,op:'contains',value:v.query});
      if (v.column) {
        const col=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===v.column);if (!col) throw Error('Choose a current filter column.');
        if (v.operator==='contains') {if (typeof v.value!=='string' || !v.value.trim() || bytes(v.value)>200) throw Error('Enter a filter search term up to 200 bytes.');filters.push({column_id:v.column,op:v.operator,value:v.value});}
        else if (!['empty','not_empty'].includes(v.operator)) {const result=validateCell(col,v.value);if (result.message) throw Error(result.message);if (['gt','lt','gte','lte'].includes(v.operator) && result.value===null) throw Error('Enter a non-empty comparison value.');filters.push({column_id:v.column,op:v.operator,value:result.value});}
        else filters.push({column_id:v.column,op:v.operator});
      }
      return {id:e.doc.id,cursor:cursorValue,limit:LIMIT.rows,...(v.query && !v.searchColumn ? {q:v.query} : {}),...(filters.length ? {filters:JSON.stringify(filters)} : {}),...(v.sort ? {sort:JSON.stringify({column_id:v.sort,direction:v.direction})} : {})};
    }
    function sheetQueryControls(e) {
      const details=element('details','cvt-sheet-view'),summary=element('summary','cvt-button cvt-button-quiet','Search, filter & sort'),form=element('form','cvt-sheet-query');details.append(summary,form);
      const names=e.sheet.columns.map(c => [c.id,c.name]),searchable=[['','All permitted fields'],...e.sheet.columns.filter(c => !NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(c.type) && !['date','checkbox'].includes(c.type)).map(c => [c.id,c.name])];
      const query=field('Find text','sheet_query'),searchColumn=field('Search column','sheet_search_column',element('select')),filterColumn=field('Filter column','sheet_filter_column',element('select')),operator=field('Condition','sheet_operator',element('select')),value=field('Filter value','sheet_filter_value'),sort=field('Sort column','sheet_sort_column',element('select')),direction=field('Direction','sheet_sort_direction',element('select'));
      query.control.type='search';query.control.maxLength=160;query.control.value=e.view.query;options(searchColumn.control,searchable);searchColumn.control.value=e.view.searchColumn || '';
      options(filterColumn.control,[['','No filter'],...names]);filterColumn.control.value=e.view.column;options(sort.control,[['','Original row order'],...names]);sort.control.value=e.view.sort;options(direction.control,[['asc','Ascending'],['desc','Descending']]);direction.control.value=e.view.direction;
      function filterFields(keepValue=false) {
        const col=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===filterColumn.control.value),ops=[['empty','Is empty'],['not_empty','Is not empty'],['eq','Equals']];
        if (col && !NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type) && !['date','checkbox'].includes(col.type)) ops.push(['contains','Contains']);
        if (col && (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type) || col.type==='date')) ops.push(['gt','Greater than / after'],['lt','Less than / before'],['gte','At least / on or after'],['lte','At most / on or before']);
        const selected=operator.control.value || e.view.operator;operator.control.replaceChildren();options(operator.control,ops);operator.control.value=ops.some(([o]) => o===selected) ? selected : 'empty';operator.control.disabled=!col;
        value.wrap.hidden=!col || ['empty','not_empty'].includes(operator.control.value);
        const old=value.control,previous=keepValue ? old.value : filterColumn.control.value===e.view.column ? e.view.value : '',next=element(col && ['select','status','checkbox'].includes(col.type) && operator.control.value!=='contains' ? 'select' : 'input');next.name='sheet_filter_value';next.value=previous;
        if (next.tagName==='SELECT') {options(next,col.type==='checkbox' ? [['true','Checked'],['false','Unchecked']] : [['','Empty'],...col.config.options.map(o => [o.id,o.label])]);next.value=previous;}
        else {next.type=col?.type==='date' ? 'date' : NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col?.type) ? 'number' : 'text';next.maxLength=LIMIT.cell;if (next.type==='number') next.step='any';}
        old.replaceWith(next);value.control=next;
      }
      filterColumn.control.addEventListener('change',() => filterFields());operator.control.addEventListener('change',() => filterFields(true));filterFields();
      const apply=element('button','cvt-button cvt-button-primary','Apply view');apply.type='submit';
      const error=element('p','cvt-field-error');error.setAttribute('role','alert');
      form.append(query.wrap,searchColumn.wrap,filterColumn.wrap,operator.wrap,value.wrap,sort.wrap,direction.wrap,element('p','cvt-query-help','Queries cover this permitted sheet. Counts describe the saved data at the last load. Saving keeps your focused cell in place; reapply the view to update row membership.'),error,apply,button('Clear view',async () => {if (!await readyToMutate()) return;const previous=e.view;e.view={query:'',searchColumn:'',column:'',operator:'empty',value:'',sort:'',direction:'asc'};if (!await loadSheetView(e)) e.view=previous;}));
      form.addEventListener('submit',async ev => {
        ev.preventDefault();if (apply.disabled || !checkIdentity() || !await readyToMutate()) return;
        const previous=e.view;e.view={query:query.control.value.trim(),searchColumn:searchColumn.control.value,column:filterColumn.control.value,operator:operator.control.value,value:value.control.value,sort:sort.control.value,direction:direction.control.value};
        if (e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===e.view.column)?.type==='checkbox') e.view.value=e.view.value==='true';
        apply.disabled=true;error.textContent='';try {sheetParams(e);if (!await loadSheetView(e)) {e.view=previous;error.textContent=e.error || 'The view could not load. Retry.';}}catch (err) {e.view=previous;error.textContent=err.message;}finally {apply.disabled=false;}
      });return details;
    }
    async function loadSheetView(e) {
      const params=sheetParams(e,null);e.viewBusy=true;for (const input of content.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell-control')) input.disabled=true;const title=content.querySelector('[name=title]');if (title) title.readOnly=true;
      try {const data=await request('sheet',params,undefined,'sheet-view');if (entry!==e) return false;const sheet=sheetFrom(data);e.referencePreviews.clear();e.sheet=sheet;e.doc=sheet.document;e.sheetCursor=null;e.sheetCursors=[];e.referenceView=false;e.error='';e.viewBusy=false;renderEditor();notice('Sheet view updated.');content.querySelector('.cvt-sheet-view>summary')?.focus();return true;}
      catch (error) {if (entry===e && error.name!=='AbortError' && await recheckUnavailableDocument(error,e)) {e.error=error.message;notice(error.message,true);}return false;}
      finally {if (entry===e) {e.viewBusy=false;for (const input of content.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell-control')) input.disabled=!canEdit(e);if (title?.isConnected) title.readOnly=!canEdit(e);}}
    }
    async function recheckUnavailableDocument(error,e) {
      if (entry!==e || !checkIdentity()) return false;if (error.status!==404) return true;
      try {
        const data=await request('document',{id:e.doc.id});if (entry!==e || !checkIdentity()) return false;
        // A bad cursor/filter can return 404 while the sheet remains readable.
        // Reconcile masked references from the canonical response, preserving
        // unrelated drafts and the mounted editors.
        if (data.sheet) {
          const fresh=sheetFrom(data.sheet);for (const [key,cell] of fresh.cells) if (cell.reference?.available===false && e.sheet?.cells.has(key)) {
            e.referencePreviews.clear();e.sheet.cells.set(key,cell);const input=content.querySelector('.cvt-cell-control[data-cell="'+key+'"]');if (input && !e.cellDrafts.has(key)) setCellInput(input,cell.reference.type,null);refreshCellExtras(e,key);
          }
        }
        return true;
      }catch (err) {if (entry!==e || err.name==='AbortError') return false;if (err.status===404) {lock('This document is no longer available. Private content has been cleared.');return false;}return true;}
    }
    function displayCell(value,col) {
      if (isEmpty(value)) return 'Empty';if (col && ['select','status'].includes(col.type)) return col.config.options.find(o => o.id===value)?.label || 'Unavailable choice';
      const precision=col?.config.precision,decimals=precision===undefined ? {maximumFractionDigits:20} : {minimumFractionDigits:precision,maximumFractionDigits:precision};
      if (typeof value==='number' && col?.type==='currency') {try {return new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined,{style:'currency',currency:col.config.currency,...decimals}).format(value);}catch {return col.config.currency+' '+String(value);}}
      if (typeof value==='number' && col?.type==='percentage') return col.config.scale==='fraction' ? new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined,{style:'percent',...decimals}).format(value) : new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined,decimals).format(value)+'%';
      return value===true ? 'Checked' : value===false ? 'Unchecked' : String(value);
    }
    function setCellInput(input,type,value) {if (type==='checkbox') input.checked=value===true || value===1;else input.value=value==null ? '' : String(value);}
    function cellExtras(e,row,col,input,error,extras) {
      const key=row.id+'|'+col.id,cell=e.sheet.cells.get(key),draft=e.cellDrafts.get(key),value=draft ? draft.value : cell?.value;extras.replaceChildren();
      if (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(col.type)) extras.append(element('span','cvt-cell-display',col.type==='percentage' ? (col.config.scale==='fraction' ? 'Fraction scale · ' : 'Percentage points · ')+displayCell(value,col) : displayCell(value,col)));
      if (col.type==='phone') extras.append(element('span','cvt-cell-hint','Original format kept; no country code is inferred.'));
      if (!draft?.error && !isEmpty(value) && ['email','url'].includes(col.type) && !validateCell(col,value).message) {const a=element('a','cvt-cell-action',col.type==='email' ? 'Compose email' : 'Open URL');a.dataset.action=col.type;a.href=col.type==='email' ? 'mailto:'+encodeURIComponent(value) : value;if (col.type==='url') {a.target='_blank';a.rel='noopener noreferrer';}extras.append(a);}
      if (REFERENCE_TYPES.includes(col.type)) {
        const preview=e.referencePreviews.get(col.type+'|'+value),reference=cell?.reference;extras.append(element('span','cvt-reference-preview',reference?.available===false && !draft ? 'Reference unavailable' : preview?.label || (reference?.available && reference.id===value ? reference.label : '') || (isEmpty(value) ? 'No reference selected' : 'Preview unavailable; choose to check access')));
        if (canEdit(e) && !e.setupPending) {
          const choose=button('Choose reference',async () => {choose.disabled=true;try {await pickCellReference(e,row,col,input,error);}finally {if (choose.isConnected) choose.disabled=false;}});choose.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose '+col.name+' reference, row '+(Number(input.dataset.row)+1));extras.append(choose);
          if (!isEmpty(value)) extras.append(button('Clear reference',() => {input.value='';editCell(e,row,col,input,error);input.focus();}));
        }
        if (!isEmpty(value) && validId(value)) {
          if (col.type==='document') {const a=element('a','cvt-cell-action','Open document');a.href=docURL(value);a.addEventListener('click',ev => {if (ev.button || ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.shiftKey || ev.altKey) return;ev.preventDefault();navigate(() => openDocument(value));});extras.append(a);}
          else if (typeof window.CommuniverseTools.onOpenReference==='function') extras.append(button('Open reference',async () => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;try {await window.CommuniverseTools.onOpenReference({type:col.type,id:value,documentId:e.doc.id});}catch {if (entry===e && checkIdentity()) notice('This reference is unavailable.',true);}}));
        }
      }
      const details=element('details','cvt-cell-details'),summary=element('summary','','Recorded details');details.open=extras.dataset.detailsOpen==='true';details.addEventListener('toggle',() => {extras.dataset.detailsOpen=String(details.open);});details.append(summary,element('p','','Cell: '+createdLabel(cell || {})),element('p','','Cell: '+editorLabel(cell || {})),element('p','','Row: '+createdLabel(row)),element('p','','Row: '+editorLabel(row)));
      const a=element('a','cvt-cell-action','Cell link');a.href=docURL(e.doc.id,row.id,col.id);a.addEventListener('click',ev => {if (ev.button || ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey || ev.shiftKey || ev.altKey) return;ev.preventDefault();e.selectedReference={rowId:row.id,columnId:col.id};highlightReference(e,true);if (typeof navigateParent==='function') navigateParent({view:'manage',section:'tools',document:e.doc.id,documentId:e.doc.id,row:row.id,column:col.id,url:a.getAttribute('href')});});details.append(a);extras.append(details);
    }
    function refreshCellExtras(e,key) {
      const td=[...content.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell')].find(n => n.dataset.cell===key),input=td?.querySelector('.cvt-cell-control'),col=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===key.split('|')[1]),row=e.sheet.rows.find(r => r.id===key.split('|')[0]);
      if (td && input && col && row) {
        const extras=td.querySelector('.cvt-cell-extras');
        if (extras.contains(document.activeElement)) {
          const cell=e.sheet.cells.get(key),draft=e.cellDrafts.get(key),value=draft ? draft.value : cell?.value,display=extras.querySelector('.cvt-cell-display');if (display) display.textContent=(col.type==='percentage' ? (col.config.scale==='fraction' ? 'Fraction scale · ' : 'Percentage points · ') : '')+displayCell(value,col);
          const contact=extras.querySelector('a[data-action]');if (contact) {contact.hidden=!!draft?.error || isEmpty(value) || !!validateCell(col,value).message;if (!contact.hidden) contact.href=col.type==='email' ? 'mailto:'+encodeURIComponent(value) : value;}
          const preview=extras.querySelector('.cvt-reference-preview');if (preview) preview.textContent=cell?.reference?.available===false && !draft ? 'Reference unavailable' : e.referencePreviews.get(col.type+'|'+value)?.label || (cell?.reference?.available && cell.reference.id===value ? cell.reference.label : '') || (isEmpty(value) ? 'No reference selected' : 'Preview unavailable; choose to check access');
          const metadata=['Cell: '+createdLabel(cell || {}),'Cell: '+editorLabel(cell || {}),'Row: '+createdLabel(row),'Row: '+editorLabel(row)];extras.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell-details p').forEach((n,i) => {n.textContent=metadata[i];});return;
        }
        cellExtras(e,row,col,input,td.querySelector('.cvt-cell-error'),extras);
      }
    }
    async function pickCellReference(e,row,col,input,error) {
      if (!checkIdentity() || entry!==e || !canEdit(e)) return;const gen=generation,hook=window.CommuniverseTools.onPickReference;
      try {
        let picked;
        if (typeof hook==='function') {
          const controller=new AbortController();controllers.add(controller);
          try {picked=await hook({type:col.type,documentId:e.doc.id,currentId:validId(input.value) ? input.value : null,signal:controller.signal});}finally {controllers.delete(controller);}
          if (picked && picked.permissionChecked!==true) throw Error('The picker must confirm current reference access.');
        } else if (['document','task','project'].includes(col.type)) {
          await searchPicker(col.type,async candidate => {
            if (col.type==='document') {const doc=projectDocument((await request('document',{id:candidate.id})).document);picked={id:doc.id,preview:{label:doc.title},permissionChecked:true};}
            else {const data=await request('work-overview'),raw=array(col.type==='task' ? data.tasks : data.projects).find(r => r.id===candidate.id && r.status!=='archived');if (!raw) throw Error('This reference is unavailable.');picked={id:raw.id,preview:{label:text(raw.title) || text(raw.name)},permissionChecked:true};}
          });
        } else {notice('A permitted people picker is not connected yet. No value has been changed.');return;}
        if (!picked || gen!==generation || entry!==e || !input.isConnected || !checkIdentity()) return;
        if (!validId(picked.id) || picked.permissionChecked!==true) throw Error('Choose a currently permitted canonical reference.');
        const label=text(picked.preview?.label || picked.preview?.title).slice(0,160);
        if (label) e.referencePreviews.set(col.type+'|'+picked.id,{label});input.value=picked.id;editCell(e,row,col,input,error);input.focus();
      }catch (err) {if (entry===e && gen===generation && checkIdentity() && err.name!=='AbortError') {error.textContent=err.message;input.setAttribute('aria-invalid','true');}}
    }
    function highlightReference(e,focus=false) {
      const ref=e.selectedReference;if (!ref || entry!==e || !checkIdentity()) return;
      for (const n of content.querySelectorAll('.is-reference')) {n.classList.remove('is-reference');n.removeAttribute('aria-current');}
      const row=[...content.querySelectorAll('tr[data-row-id]')].find(n => n.dataset.rowId===ref.rowId),target=ref.columnId ? [...(row?.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell') || [])].find(n => n.dataset.cell===ref.rowId+'|'+ref.columnId) : row?.querySelector('th');
      if (!target) {notice('The referenced row or cell is unavailable in this authorized view.',true);return;}
      target.classList.add('is-reference');target.setAttribute('aria-current','true');target.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest',behavior:'auto'});if (focus) target.focus({preventScroll:true});notice(ref.columnId ? 'Referenced cell selected.' : 'Referenced row selected.');
    }
    function editCell(e,row,col,input,error) {
      if (!checkIdentity() || !canEdit(e)) return;const key=row.id+'|'+col.id;
      if (!e.cellDrafts.has(key) && e.cellDrafts.size>=LIMIT.drafts) {error.textContent='Save the current edits before editing more cells.';return;}
      const raw=col.type==='checkbox' ? input.checked : input.value,{value,message}=validateCell(col,raw,input.validity.badInput);
      e.cellDrafts.set(key,{row_id:row.id,column_id:col.id,columnName:col.name,rowNumber:Number(input.dataset.row)+1,value,raw,error:message,seq:++e.cellSeq,type:col.type});error.textContent=message;input.setAttribute('aria-invalid',String(!!message));if (!e.pending) e.error='';schedule(e);saveStatus(e);
      refreshCellExtras(e,key);
    }
    function changed(e) {if (!canEdit(e)) return;e.contentDirty=true;e.revision++;if (!e.pending) e.error='';schedule(e);saveStatus(e);}
    function schedule(e) {
      if (entry!==e || !checkIdentity() || e.composition || e.conflict || e.pending || e.error) return;
      clearTimeout(e.debounce);e.debounce=setTimeout(() => flush(),650);if (!e.maximum) e.maximum=setTimeout(() => flush(),2500);
    }
    function saveStatus(e) {
      if (entry!==e || locked) return;const bar=content.querySelector('.cvt-savebar');if (!bar) return;
      const invalid=[...e.cellDrafts.values()].some(d => d.error) || !e.draft.title.trim();
      const message=e.sending || e.structureBusy ? 'Saving…' : e.conflict ? 'Review required before saving' : e.structurePending ? e.structureError || 'Sheet change not confirmed.' : e.structureError ? e.structureError : e.error ? e.error : invalid ? 'Check the highlighted fields to save.' : dirty(e) ? 'Unsaved changes' : canEdit(e) ? 'Saved' : 'View only';
      bar.replaceChildren(element('span','',message));bar.classList.toggle('is-error',!!e.error || !!e.structureError || invalid);
      if (e.error && !e.conflict) bar.append(button('Retry save',() => {e.error='';return flush();},'quiet'));
      if (e.structurePending && !e.structureBusy && !e.setupBusy) bar.append(button('Retry sheet change',() => e.setupPending ? resumeTemplate() : changeStructure(e.structurePending.endpoint,e.structurePending.patch),'quiet'));
      const edited=content.querySelector('.cvt-meta .cvt-edited');if (edited) edited.textContent=editorLabel(e.doc);
    }
    function nextWrite(e) {
      if (e.pending) return e.pending;
      if (e.contentDirty) {
        const body=e.draft.body;
        if (!e.draft.title.trim()) throw Error('Add a title before saving.');if (bytes(e.draft.title.trim())>160) throw Error('This title is too long. Shorten it to save.');if (bytes(body)>LIMIT.body) throw Error('This page is full. Shorten its text before saving.');
        const payload={id:e.doc.id,version:e.doc.version,kind:e.doc.kind,title:e.draft.title.trim(),...(e.doc.kind==='folder' ? {} : {body}),color:e.draft.color,requestId:uuid()};
        if (bytes(JSON.stringify(payload))>65536) throw Error('This text is too long to save. Shorten it and try again.');
        return {endpoint:'save',payload,revision:e.revision,draft:clone(e.draft)};
      }
      for (const [key,cell] of e.cellDrafts) {if (cell.error) continue;return {endpoint:'cells',key,seq:cell.seq,payload:{document_id:e.doc.id,row_id:cell.row_id,column_id:cell.column_id,value:cell.value,version:e.sheet.cells.get(key)?.version ?? 0,requestId:uuid()}};}
      return null;
    }
    async function flush() {
      const e=entry;if (!e || !checkIdentity() || !canEdit(e) || e.conflict || e.composition) return;
      if (e.sending) return e.sending;if (!dirty(e)) return;stopTimers(e);const gen=generation;
      e.sending=(async () => {
        // Yield before the finally block can clear the assigned promise.
        await Promise.resolve();
        try {
          let writes=0;
          while (entry===e && checkIdentity() && !e.conflict && !e.composition && writes++<=LIMIT.drafts+1) {
            const job=nextWrite(e);if (!job) break;e.pending=job;saveStatus(e);
            const data=await request(job.endpoint,{},job.payload);if (entry!==e || gen!==generation) return;
            const doc=projectDocument({...e.doc,...data.document},true);
            if (!data.document || doc.id!==e.doc.id || (job.endpoint==='save' && doc.version<=job.payload.version) || (job.endpoint==='cells' && (!data.cell || data.cell.version<=job.payload.version))) throw Error('The saved version was not confirmed. Retry this change.');
            e.doc=doc;
            if (job.endpoint==='save') {
              e.contentDirty=e.revision!==job.revision;
              if (!e.contentDirty) {e.draft={title:doc.title,body:doc.body || '',color:doc.color};for (const name of ['title','body']) {const input=content.querySelector('[name="'+name+'"]');if (input && input.value!==e.draft[name]) input.value=e.draft[name];}}
              const version=content.querySelector('.cvt-page-version');if (version) version.textContent=(notebookContext?.doc.id===doc.id ? 'Notebook overview' : 'Notebook page')+' · version '+doc.version;
            } else {
              const savedValue=data.cell.value;
              if (savedValue!==null && (!['string','number','boolean'].includes(typeof savedValue) || (typeof savedValue==='number' && !Number.isFinite(savedValue)))) throw Error('The cell value was not confirmed. Retry this change.');
              const reference=projectReference(data.cell.reference,savedValue,e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===job.payload.column_id)?.type);if (reference?.available===false) e.referencePreviews.clear();
              e.sheet.document=doc;e.sheet.cells.set(job.key,{row_id:job.payload.row_id,column_id:job.payload.column_id,value:reference?.available===false ? null : savedValue,version:data.cell.version,...recordedMetadata(data.cell),...(reference ? {reference} : {})});
              if (e.cellDrafts.get(job.key)?.seq===job.seq) {e.cellDrafts.delete(job.key);const input=content.querySelector('.cvt-cell-control[data-cell="'+job.key+'"]'),type=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===job.payload.column_id)?.type;if (input) {if (type==='checkbox') {if (input.checked!==savedValue) input.checked=savedValue===true;}else if (input.value!==(savedValue==null ? '' : String(savedValue))) setCellInput(input,type,savedValue);input.setAttribute('aria-invalid','false');const error=input.closest('.cvt-cell')?.querySelector('.cvt-cell-error');if (error) error.textContent='';}}
              refreshCellExtras(e,job.key);
            }
            e.pending=null;e.error='';
          }
        } catch (error) {
          if (entry!==e || error.name==='AbortError') return;
          if (error.status===409 || (error.status===404 && e.pending?.endpoint==='cells')) {e.pending=null;await refreshConflict(e);}else {
            e.error=error.message;
            if ([400,413,422].includes(error.status)) {
              const rejected=e.pending;e.pending=null;
              if (rejected?.endpoint==='cells') {const draft=e.cellDrafts.get(rejected.key);if (draft?.seq===rejected.seq) {draft.error=error.message;const input=content.querySelector('.cvt-cell-control[data-cell="'+rejected.key+'"]');if (input) {input.setAttribute('aria-invalid','true');input.closest('.cvt-cell').querySelector('.cvt-cell-error').textContent=error.message;}}}
            }
          }
        } finally {if (entry===e && gen===generation) {e.sending=null;saveStatus(e);}}
      })();saveStatus(e);return e.sending;
    }
    async function refreshConflict(e) {
      e.conflict={loading:true};e.error='';saveStatus(e);renderConflict(e);
      try {
        let data,queryReset=false;
        try {data=await request(e.doc.kind==='sheet' ? 'sheet' : 'document',e.sheet ? sheetParams(e) : {id:e.doc.id});}
        catch (error) {
          if (error.status!==404 || !e.sheet) throw error;
          const current=await request('document',{id:e.doc.id});data=current.sheet || await request('sheet',{id:e.doc.id,limit:LIMIT.rows});queryReset=true;
          e.view={query:'',searchColumn:'',column:'',operator:'empty',value:'',sort:'',direction:'asc'};e.sheetCursor=null;e.sheetCursors=[];
        }
        if (entry!==e) return;
        const doc=projectDocument(data.document,true);e.conflict={loading:false,previous:e.doc,queryReset};e.doc=doc;
        if (doc.kind==='sheet') {e.referencePreviews.clear();e.sheet=sheetFrom(data);}
        renderEditor();content.querySelector('.cvt-conflict')?.focus();
      } catch (error) {if (entry===e && error.name!=='AbortError') {if (error.status===404) {lock('This document is no longer available. Private content has been cleared.');return;}e.conflict={loading:false,failed:true};e.error='The current version could not load. Your draft is kept here. Retry the refresh.';renderConflict(e);}}
    }
    function renderConflict(e) {
      const box=content.querySelector('.cvt-conflict');if (!box) return;box.replaceChildren();box.hidden=!e.conflict;if (!e.conflict) return;
      box.tabIndex=-1;box.setAttribute('role','alert');box.append(element('h3','',e.conflict.loading ? 'Loading the current version…' : 'This document changed'));
      if (e.conflict.loading) return;
      if (e.conflict.failed) {box.append(element('p','',e.error),button('Retry refresh',() => refreshConflict(e)));return;}
      box.append(element('p','','The current version is loaded. Your unsaved edits are kept in this panel. Review both before choosing which changes to keep.'));
      if (e.conflict.queryReset) box.append(element('p','','The previous filter or page is unavailable. The current first page is shown; unavailable draft cells remain below for review.'));
      if (e.doc.kind!=='sheet') {const preview=element('details','cvt-latest'),summary=element('summary','','Review the current saved content');preview.append(summary,element('h4','',e.doc.title),element('pre','',e.doc.body));box.append(preview);}
      else for (const [key,draft] of e.cellDrafts) {
        if (e.sheet.rows.some(r => r.id===draft.row_id) && e.sheet.columns.some(c => c.id===draft.column_id && c.type===draft.type)) continue;
        const panel=element('div','cvt-orphan-draft');panel.append(element('p','','Unavailable draft: '+draft.columnName+', row '+draft.rowNumber+' of the previous page ('+typeLabel(draft.type)+')'),element('pre','',displayCell(draft.raw)),button('Discard this unavailable cell draft',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.cellDrafts.delete(key);renderEditor();}));box.append(panel);
      }
      box.append(button('Keep my edits',() => {
        if (!checkIdentity() || !canEdit(e)) return;
        if (e.doc.kind==='sheet') {for (const d of e.cellDrafts.values()) {const col=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===d.column_id && c.type===d.type);if (!e.sheet.rows.some(r => r.id===d.row_id) || !col) {notice('A row or column changed. Use the current version; these edits cannot be safely reapplied.',true);return;}const validation=validateCell(col,d.raw);if (validation.message) {d.error=validation.message;notice(validation.message,true);renderEditor();return;}}}
        // Rebase only locally changed document fields, preserving unrelated edits.
        const old=e.conflict.previous;if (e.draft.title===old.title) e.draft.title=e.doc.title;if (e.draft.color===old.color) e.draft.color=e.doc.color;
        if (e.draft.body===old.body) e.draft.body=e.doc.body;
        e.conflict=null;e.error='';renderEditor();flush();
      },'primary'),button('Use current version',() => {if (!checkIdentity()) return;e.pending=null;e.cellDrafts.clear();e.contentDirty=false;e.draft={title:e.doc.title,body:e.doc.body,color:e.doc.color};e.conflict=null;e.error='';renderEditor();}));
    }
    async function readyToMutate(continuingSetup=false) {await flush();if (!checkIdentity()) return false;const e=entry;if (e?.conflict || e?.viewBusy || (dirty() && !(continuingSetup && e?.setupPending && !e.contentDirty && !e.cellDrafts.size && !e.pending && !e.structurePending))) {notice('Save or resolve the current draft before changing structure or access.',true);return false;}return true;}
    async function sheetPage(forward) {
      const e=entry;if (!e?.sheet || !await readyToMutate()) return;
      let value;if (forward) {if (!e.sheet.nextCursor || e.sheetCursors.length>=20) return;value=e.sheet.nextCursor;}else value=e.sheetCursors.at(-1);
      e.viewBusy=true;for (const input of content.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell-control')) input.disabled=true;
      try {const sheet=sheetFrom(await request('sheet',sheetParams(e,value),undefined,'sheet-view'));if (entry!==e) return;if (forward) e.sheetCursors.push(e.sheetCursor);else e.sheetCursors.pop();e.referencePreviews.clear();e.sheet=sheet;e.doc=sheet.document;e.sheetCursor=value || null;e.viewBusy=false;renderEditor();highlightReference(e);} catch (error) {if (entry===e && error.name!=='AbortError' && await recheckUnavailableDocument(error,e)) notice(error.message,true);}finally {if (entry===e) {e.viewBusy=false;for (const input of content.querySelectorAll('.cvt-cell-control')) input.disabled=!canEdit(e);}}
    }
    async function changeStructure(endpoint,patch,requestId=uuid(),continuingSetup=false) {
      const e=entry;if (!e?.sheet || e.structureBusy || !checkIdentity()) return false;
      if (!e.structurePending) {if (!canEdit(e) || !await readyToMutate(continuingSetup)) return false;e.structurePending={endpoint,patch:clone(patch),payload:{document_id:e.doc.id,version:e.doc.version,...patch,requestId}};}
      else if (e.structurePending.endpoint!==endpoint || JSON.stringify(e.structurePending.patch)!==JSON.stringify(patch)) {notice('Retry the unconfirmed sheet change before making another one.',true);return false;}
      const job=e.structurePending;e.structureBusy=true;e.structureError='';renderEditor();
      try {
        const data=await request(job.endpoint,{},job.payload);if (entry!==e) return false;
        if (!data.document) throw Error('The sheet change was not confirmed. Retry it.');const updated=projectDocument(data.document,true);if (updated.id!==e.doc.id || updated.version<=job.payload.version) throw Error('The sheet version was not confirmed. Retry it.');e.doc=updated;
        if (patch.action==='delete' && endpoint==='columns') {if (e.view.column===patch.id) e.view.column='';if (e.view.searchColumn===patch.id) {e.view.searchColumn='';e.view.query='';}if (e.view.sort===patch.id) e.view.sort='';}
        e.sheet=sheetFrom(await request('sheet',sheetParams(e,null)));e.doc=e.sheet.document;
        if (endpoint==='columns' && patch.action!=='delete') {const saved=e.sheet.columns.find(c => c.id===data.column?.id);if (!saved || saved.name!==patch.name || saved.type!==patch.type || (patch.config && JSON.stringify(saved.config)!==JSON.stringify(patch.config))) throw Error('The column settings were not confirmed. Retry the same change.');e.lastStructureId=saved.id;}
        e.sheetCursor=null;e.sheetCursors=[];e.structurePending=null;notice('Sheet updated.');return true;
      } catch (error) {if (entry===e && error.name!=='AbortError') {if (error.status===409 && error.code!=='incompatible_cells') {e.structurePending=null;await refreshConflict(e);}else {e.structureError=error.message;if ([400,404,409,413,422].includes(error.status)) e.structurePending=null;}}return false;}
      finally {if (entry===e) {e.structureBusy=false;renderEditor();}}
    }
    async function columnDialog(column) {
      if (!await readyToMutate()) return;const e=entry;if (!e?.sheet || (!column && e.sheet.columns.length>=LIMIT.columns)) {notice('A sheet can show up to 32 columns.',true);return;}
      const name=field('Column name','column_name'),type=field('Column type','column_type',element('select')),currency=field('Currency (ISO code)','column_currency'),width=field('Column width in this view (pixels)','column_width'),choices=element('div','cvt-column-choices'),settings=element('div','cvt-column-settings'),numericSettings=element('div','cvt-column-settings'),scale=field('Percentage scale','column_scale',element('select')),precision=field('Display decimal places','column_precision',element('select')),minimum=field('Minimum (optional)','column_minimum'),maximum=field('Maximum (optional)','column_maximum'),preview=element('p','cvt-column-preview');
      name.control.value=column?.name || '';name.control.maxLength=120;name.control.required=true;options(type.control,TYPES);type.control.value=column?.type || 'text';currency.control.value=column?.config.currency || '';currency.control.maxLength=3;currency.control.placeholder='ISO code, e.g. AED';width.control.type='number';width.control.min='160';width.control.max='640';width.control.step='20';width.control.value=String(e.columnWidths.get(column?.id) || 220);
      const optionRows=[];let signature='',requestId='',optionSequence=0;
      options(scale.control,[['percent','Percentage points: 25 = 25%'],['fraction','Fraction: 0.25 = 25%']]);scale.control.value=column?.config.scale || 'percent';options(precision.control,[['','Automatic'],...[0,1,2,3,4,5,6].map(n => [String(n),String(n)])]);precision.control.value=column?.config.precision===undefined ? '' : String(column.config.precision);
      for (const [f,key] of [[minimum,'min'],[maximum,'max']]) {f.control.type='number';f.control.step='any';f.control.inputMode='decimal';f.control.value=column?.config[key]===undefined ? '' : String(column.config[key]);f.control.addEventListener('input',updatePreview);}
      numericSettings.append(scale.wrap,precision.wrap,minimum.wrap,maximum.wrap,element('p','','Display precision does not round the stored number. Currency and percentage scale changes are blocked for populated columns; explicitly review or migrate existing values first.'));
      function addOption(option={id:uuid(),label:''}) {
        if (optionRows.length>=LIMIT.options) {notice('A column supports up to 100 choices.',true);return;}
        const index=optionSequence++,wrap=element('div','cvt-option-row'),label=field('Choice label '+(index+1),'option_label_'+index),color=field('Choice color '+(index+1),'option_color_'+index,element('select'));label.control.value=option.label;label.control.required=true;label.control.maxLength=120;options(color.control,[['','No color'],...COLORS]);if (option.color && !COLORS.some(([c]) => c===option.color)) options(color.control,[[option.color,'Existing custom color']]);color.control.value=option.color || '';
        const item={id:option.id,wrap,label:label.control,color:color.control};optionRows.push(item);wrap.append(label.wrap,color.wrap,button('Remove choice',() => {optionRows.splice(optionRows.indexOf(item),1);wrap.remove();updatePreview();}));choices.append(wrap);label.control.addEventListener('input',updatePreview);
      }
      for (const o of column?.config.options || []) addOption(o);
      function configValue() {
        const selected=type.control.value,config=selected==='currency' ? {currency:currency.control.value.trim().toUpperCase()} : selected==='percentage' ? {scale:scale.control.value} : ['select','status'].includes(selected) ? {options:optionRows.map(o => ({id:o.id,label:o.label.value.trim(),...(o.color.value ? {color:o.color.value} : {})}))} : {};
        if (NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(selected)) for (const [f,key] of [[minimum,'min'],[maximum,'max']]) {if (f.control.validity.badInput) throw Error('Enter a finite numeric bound.');if (f.control.value!=='') config[key]=Number(f.control.value);}
        if (['currency','percentage'].includes(selected) && precision.control.value!=='') config.precision=Number(precision.control.value);return columnConfig(config,selected);
      }
      function updatePreview() {
        const selected=type.control.value;currency.wrap.hidden=selected!=='currency';currency.control.required=selected==='currency';settings.hidden=!['select','status'].includes(selected);for (const o of optionRows) o.label.required=!settings.hidden;
        numericSettings.hidden=!NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(selected);scale.wrap.hidden=selected!=='percentage';precision.wrap.hidden=!['currency','percentage'].includes(selected);
        if (!column) {preview.textContent=selected==='percentage' ? (scale.control.value==='fraction' ? 'Fraction scale: 0.25 means 25%.' : 'Percentage points: 25 means 25%.')+' Stored numbers are not converted.' : REFERENCE_TYPES.includes(selected) ? 'Choose permitted canonical records. A reference never grants access.' : 'Existing records are never filled in automatically.';return;}
