        try {const proposal={type:selected,config:configValue()},incompatible=[];for (let i=0;i<e.sheet.rows.length;i++) {const saved=e.sheet.cells.get(e.sheet.rows[i].id+'|'+column.id)?.value;if (isEmpty(saved)) continue;const mismatch=NUMERIC_TYPES.includes(selected) ? typeof saved!=='number' : selected==='checkbox' ? typeof saved!=='boolean' : typeof saved!=='string';if (mismatch || validateCell(proposal,saved).message) incompatible.push(i+1);}
          const changedSemantics=proposal.type===column.type && ((selected==='currency' && proposal.config.currency!==column.config.currency) || (selected==='percentage' && proposal.config.scale!==column.config.scale));
          preview.textContent=(changedSemantics ? 'Numeric semantics changed; populated columns require explicit migration. ' : '')+incompatible.length+' incompatible saved cells on this loaded page'+(incompatible.length ? ' (rows '+incompatible.join(', ')+')' : '')+'. The server validates the entire column. Values are not converted or deleted.';
        }catch (err) {preview.textContent=err.message;}
      }
      type.control.addEventListener('change',updatePreview);currency.control.addEventListener('input',updatePreview);scale.control.addEventListener('change',updatePreview);precision.control.addEventListener('change',updatePreview);
      settings.append(element('p','','Choices keep stable IDs when you rename their labels. Removing a used choice requires server validation.'),choices,button('Add choice',() => {addOption();updatePreview();}));updatePreview();
      await modalForm(column ? 'Customize column' : 'Add a column',body => {
        body.append(name.wrap,type.wrap,currency.wrap,settings,numericSettings,preview,width.wrap);
        if (column) {
          const order=e.columnOrder.length ? [...e.columnOrder] : e.sheet.columns.map(c => c.id),index=order.indexOf(column.id),moves=element('div','cvt-actions');
          for (const [label,offset] of [['Move left in this view',-1],['Move right in this view',1]]) {const b=button(label,() => {const next=index+offset;if (!checkIdentity() || next<0 || next>=order.length) return;[order[index],order[next]]=[order[next],order[index]];e.columnOrder=order;closeModal();renderEditor();notice('Column order changed in this view.');});b.disabled=index+offset<0 || index+offset>=order.length;moves.append(b);}body.append(moves,element('p','','Width and order apply to this open view; they do not change the canonical sheet schema.'),button('Remove column',async () => {closeModal();await modalForm('Remove this column?',b => b.append(element('p','','Removing “'+column.name+'” also removes its values from this sheet. Workspace history is retained for recovery.')),'Remove column',async () => {if (!await changeStructure('columns',{action:'delete',id:column.id},uuid())) throw Error(e.structureError || 'The column was not confirmed removed. Review the sheet and retry.');return true;});},'danger'));
        }
      },column ? 'Save column' : 'Add column',async () => {
        const patch={action:column ? 'update' : 'add',...(column ? {id:column.id} : {}),name:name.control.value.trim(),type:type.control.value,config:configValue()},encoded=JSON.stringify(patch),size=Number(width.control.value);
        if (!Number.isInteger(size) || size<160 || size>640) throw Error('Choose a column width between 160 and 640 pixels.');
        const unchanged=column && patch.name===column.name && patch.type===column.type && JSON.stringify(patch.config)===JSON.stringify(column.config);
        if (!unchanged) {if (signature!==encoded) {signature=encoded;requestId=uuid();}if (!await changeStructure('columns',patch,requestId)) throw Error(e.structureError || 'The column change was not confirmed. Review the sheet and retry.');}
        const id=column?.id || e.lastStructureId;if (id) e.columnWidths.set(id,size);renderEditor();return true;
      });
    }
    async function templateDialog() {
      const e=entry;if (!e?.sheet || !canEdit(e) || e.sheet.columns.length || e.sheet.rows.length || !await readyToMutate()) return;
      const choice=field('Empty sheet template','sheet_template',element('select'));options(choice.control,Object.entries(TEMPLATE_COLUMNS).map(([key,t]) => [key,t.label]));const preview=element('p','cvt-template-preview');
      const update=() => {preview.textContent=TEMPLATE_COLUMNS[choice.control.value].columns.map(([name,type]) => name+' ('+typeLabel(type)+')').join(' · ');};choice.control.addEventListener('change',update);update();
      const selected=await modalForm('Choose an empty template',body => body.append(choice.wrap,preview,element('p','','This adds empty columns only, one confirmed column at a time. No people, contacts, tasks, projects or rows are created. If a step fails, confirmed columns stay visible and Retry resumes the unconfirmed step.')),'Add empty columns',() => choice.control.value);
      if (!selected || entry!==e || !checkIdentity()) return;
      const template=TEMPLATE_COLUMNS[selected];e.setupPending={label:template.label,columns:template.columns.map(([name,type]) => ({name,type,config:columnConfig({},type)})),next:0,confirmed:[]};await resumeTemplate();
    }
    async function resumeTemplate() {
      const e=entry,setup=e?.setupPending;if (!setup || e.setupBusy || e.conflict || !checkIdentity()) return;e.setupBusy=true;
      try {
        while (entry===e && checkIdentity() && setup.next<setup.columns.length) {
          const patch={action:'add',...setup.columns[setup.next]};if (!await changeStructure('columns',patch,e.structurePending?.payload.requestId || uuid(),true)) break;
          if (!validId(e.lastStructureId)) {e.structureError='The created column was not returned. Review the sheet before resuming.';break;}setup.confirmed.push(e.lastStructureId);setup.next++;
        }
        if (entry===e && setup.next===setup.columns.length) {e.setupPending=null;notice(setup.label+' is ready: '+setup.columns.length+' columns, no records.');}
      } finally {if (entry===e) {e.setupBusy=false;renderEditor();}}
    }
    async function removeRow(row) {if (!await readyToMutate()) return;const requestId=uuid();await modalForm('Remove this row?',body => body.append(element('p','','The row and its values will leave this sheet. Workspace history is retained for recovery.')),'Remove row',async () => {if (!await changeStructure('rows',{action:'delete',id:row.id},requestId)) throw Error('The row removal was not confirmed. Review the sheet and retry.');return true;});}
    function shareExplanation(type,title,kind) {return '“'+title+'” will be linked to this '+type+'. People who can access the '+type+' may be able to read and edit it. '+(kind==='folder' ? 'Documents inside this folder inherit that shared scope. ' : '')+'The server checks the shared permissions before linking.';}
    async function createDialog() {
      if (!checkIdentity() || !await readyToMutate()) return;
      const kind=field('Tool type','kind',element('select')),title=field('Title','new_title');options(kind.control,Object.entries(KINDS));title.control.required=true;title.control.maxLength=160;title.control.placeholder='Give it a name…';let previous='',requestId='';
      const parentId=trail.at(-1)?.id,scope=context && !parentId ? {[context.type+'_id']:context.id} : parentId ? {parent_id:parentId} : {};
      const saved=await modalForm('Create a tool',body => {body.append(kind.wrap,title.wrap,element('p','',parentId ? 'This tool inherits the folder’s access.' : context ? 'This tool will be shared with people who can access this '+context.type+'. Folders share this scope with their children. Create it only if this shared access is appropriate.' : 'Private to you. You can explicitly link it to a task or project later.'));},context && !parentId ? 'Create & share' : 'Create tool',async () => {
        const patch={kind:kind.control.value,title:title.control.value.trim(),version:0,color:'blue',...scope};if (patch.kind==='notebook' || patch.kind==='note') patch.body='';
        const signature=JSON.stringify(patch);if (signature!==previous) {previous=signature;requestId=uuid();}
        const data=await request('save',{}, {...patch,requestId});return projectDocument(data.document,true);
      });if (saved?.id && checkIdentity()) await openDocument(saved.id);
    }
    async function searchPicker(type,select) {
      let searchSequence=0;
      const picked=await modalForm(type==='document' ? 'Attach an existing tool' : type==='folder' ? 'Choose a folder' : 'Link to a '+type,(body,submit) => {
        submit.hidden=true;submit.disabled=true;
        const f=field(type==='document' ? 'Search documents & folders' : 'Search '+(type==='folder' ? 'folders' : type+'s'),'link_search');f.control.type='search';f.control.maxLength=160;f.control.placeholder='Search by title…';const results=element('div','cvt-picker-results');
        const searchButton=button('Search',async () => {
          if (!checkIdentity()) return;const serial=++searchSequence;results.replaceChildren(element('p','cvt-loading','Searching…'));
          try {
            const q=f.control.value.trim();if (!q) throw Error('Enter a title or search term first.');
            const work=type==='task' || type==='project',data=await request(work ? 'work-overview' : 'search',work ? {} : {q,limit:LIMIT.items},undefined,'picker');if (serial!==searchSequence || !results.isConnected) return;
            let items=array(work ? type==='task' ? data.tasks : data.projects : data.documents ?? data.items ?? data.results);
            if (work) items=items.filter(item => item.status!=='archived' && text(item.title || item.name).toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q.toLocaleLowerCase())).slice(0,LIMIT.items);
            if (items.length>LIMIT.items) throw Error('Narrow the search to 50 results or fewer.');
            results.replaceChildren();for (const raw of items) {
              if (!validId(raw.id) || (type==='folder' && raw.kind!=='folder') || ((type==='document' || type==='folder') && !KINDS[raw.kind])) continue;
              if (raw.id===entry?.doc.id && (type==='folder' || type==='document')) continue;
              const row=element('div','cvt-picker-row'),name=text(raw.title) || text(raw.name) || 'Untitled '+type;row.append(element('span','',name));const b=button('Choose',() => {closeModal({id:raw.id,title:name,kind:raw.kind,version:raw.version});});b.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose '+name);row.append(b);results.append(row);
            }if (!results.children.length) results.append(element('p','','No authorized results. Try another search.'));
          } catch (error) {if (serial===searchSequence && results.isConnected && error.name!=='AbortError') results.replaceChildren(element('p','cvt-field-error',error.message));}
        });
        f.control.addEventListener('keydown',event => {if (event.key==='Enter' && !event.isComposing) {event.preventDefault();searchButton.click();}});
        body.append(f.wrap,searchButton,results);
      },'Choose a result',() => null);
      searchSequence++;abortLane('picker');if (picked?.id) return select(picked);
    }
    async function attachDocument(id,scope,type,scopeTitle) {
      const data=await request('document',{id}),doc=projectDocument(data.document,true);if (doc.can_edit===false || doc.can_attach===false) throw Error('You cannot change this document’s scope.');
      const requestId=uuid();const saved=await modalForm(type==='folder' ? 'Move document into folder?' : 'Share this '+KINDS[doc.kind].toLowerCase()+'?',body => {
        body.append(element('p','',type==='folder' ? '“'+doc.title+'” will inherit the access of “'+scopeTitle+'”. Moving a folder also moves its children into that scope. Choose a folder whose permissions are appropriate.' : shareExplanation(type,doc.title,doc.kind)));
        if (type!=='folder' && doc.parent_id) body.append(element('p','','Linking to this work moves the original document out of its current notebook or folder.'));
        if (type!=='folder' && (doc.task_id || doc.project_id)) body.append(element('p','','The selected work replaces the document’s current task or project link.'));
        if (type!=='folder') body.append(element('p','cvt-scope','Destination: '+scopeTitle));
      },type==='folder' ? 'Move & inherit access' : 'Link & share',async () => projectDocument((await request('attach',{}, {id:doc.id,version:doc.version,...(type!=='folder' && doc.parent_id ? {parent_id:null} : {}),...scope,requestId})).document,true));
      if (saved?.id && checkIdentity()) {if (entry?.doc.id===saved.id) await openDocument(saved.id,false);else await loadLibrary();notice('The original document is linked.');}
    }
    async function linkToWork() {
      if (!entry || !await readyToMutate()) return;const id=entry.doc.id,type=field('Link to','scope_type',element('select'));options(type.control,[['task','Task'],['project','Project']]);
      const chosen=await modalForm('Link this tool to work',body => body.append(type.wrap,element('p','','Choose existing work. Linking changes who can access this document; you will review its shared scope before saving.')),'Find work',() => type.control.value);
      if (chosen) await searchPicker(chosen,p => attachDocument(id,{[chosen+'_id']:p.id},chosen,p.title));
    }
    async function moveToFolder() {if (!entry || !await readyToMutate()) return;const id=entry.doc.id;await searchPicker('folder',p => attachDocument(id,{parent_id:p.id},'folder',p.title));}
    async function attachExisting() {if (!context || !await readyToMutate()) return;await searchPicker('document',p => attachDocument(p.id,{[context.type+'_id']:context.id},context.type,'This '+context.type));}
    async function archiveDialog() {
      const e=entry;if (!e || !canEdit(e) || !await readyToMutate()) return;
      try {
        const fresh=projectDocument((await request('document',{id:e.doc.id})).document,true);if (fresh.version!==e.doc.version) {await refreshConflict(e);return;}
        const requestId=uuid(),saved=await modalForm('Archive “'+e.doc.title+'”?',body => body.append(element('p','','This '+KINDS[e.doc.kind].toLowerCase()+' will leave the active Tools list. '+(['folder','notebook'].includes(e.doc.kind) ? 'Its children will also be hidden. ' : '')+'Content and history are retained. Recover it from Archived tools. Restore an archived parent first; this action does not permanently delete anything.')),'Archive tool',async () => {
          try {await request('archive',{}, {id:e.doc.id,version:e.doc.version,requestId});return true;}
          catch (error) {if (error.status===409) {closeModal();await refreshConflict(e);}throw error;}
        });
        if (saved && checkIdentity()) {if (e.doc.kind==='folder') trail=trail.filter(p => p.id!==e.doc.id);beginView();await loadLibrary();notice('Archived. Recover it from Archived tools.');}
      } catch (error) {if (error.name!=='AbortError') notice(error.message,true);}
    }
    async function restoreDialog() {
      const e=entry;if (!e?.doc.archived_at || e.doc.can_archive===false || e.doc.ancestor_archived) return;const requestId=uuid();
      const restored=await modalForm('Restore “'+e.doc.title+'”?',body => body.append(element('p','','This document will return to its active location with the same identity and permissions. Its retained content and history stay with it.')),'Restore tool',async () => {
        try {return projectDocument((await request('archive',{}, {id:e.doc.id,version:e.doc.version,archived:false,requestId})).document,true);}catch (error) {if (error.status===409) {closeModal();await refreshConflict(e);}throw error;}
      });if (restored?.id && checkIdentity()) {archivedView=false;await openDocument(restored.id,false);notice('Restored to active Tools.');}
    }
    function identityEvent(event) {
      if (event.type==='cv-chat-reset') {lock();return;}
      const incoming=event.detail?.identity ?? event.detail?.person ?? event.detail;
      if (person(incoming)?.id!==actorId) lock('Your account changed. Private content has been cleared.');
    }
    function beforeLeave(event) {if (dirty()) {event.preventDefault();event.returnValue='';}}
    function destroy() {
      if (!alive) return;alive=false;generation++;abortAll();closeModal();purge();identity=null;context=null;privateMount.id='';clearInterval(identityTimer);
      window.removeEventListener('cv-chat-reset',identityEvent);window.removeEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityEvent);window.removeEventListener('beforeunload',beforeLeave);window.removeEventListener('pagehide',destroy);
      root.remove();live.delete(privateMount);if (hosts.get(target)===control) hosts.delete(target);
    }
    window.addEventListener('cv-chat-reset',identityEvent);window.addEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityEvent);window.addEventListener('beforeunload',beforeLeave);window.addEventListener('pagehide',destroy);
    const identityTimer=setInterval(() => {if (!root.isConnected) destroy();else checkIdentity();},1000);
    if (checkIdentity()) {
      header();const params=new URLSearchParams(location.search),initial=!context && params.get('view')==='manage' && params.get('section')==='tools' ? params.get('document') : null;
      if (validId(initial)) openDocument(initial,false,{row:params.get('row'),column:params.get('column')});else loadLibrary();
    }
    return control;
  }
  const exported={mount:options => createMount(options || {},null),mountAttachments:options => {
    if (!['task','project'].includes(options?.type) || !validId(options?.id)) throw new TypeError('Attachments need an original task or project ID.');
    return createMount(options,{type:options.type,id:options.id});
  },onAsk:null,onPickReference:null,onOpenReference:null};
  window.CommuniverseTools=exported;
})();

;
/* Read-only reference picker/navigation for Communiverse Tools.
 * Load after tools-client.js and tools.css, before mounting the parent.
 * Auto-installs absent global hooks; existing onAsk/custom hooks stay intact.
 * Parent may pass CommuniverseToolsReferences.pickReference/openReference as
 * per-mount hooks, or create({getContext,navigate}) for an owned live context.
 * getContext returns the TRUSTED native workspace state (person/team/tasks/
 * projects), never model/cell data. Default: __cvWorkspaceNative.getState().
 * Only existing GET APIs: Tools document/search, workspace overview, Connect
 * bootstrap. Every choice rechecks the source document and current actor.
 * Person choices intersect scoped workspace staff with the active canonical
 * directory. No contacts, role controls, invitations, messages or writes.
 * Returns {type,id,label,available:true,permissionChecked:true,preview:{label}}
 * or null. The wrapper is for the existing sheet hook; reference DTOs retain
 * the canonical available/type/id/label shape, never title/kind substitutions.
 * Work URLs below are the RETAINED reader's actual project/task deep links;
 * person navigation uses its People adapter, never a guessed profile slug.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.CommuniverseToolsReferences) return;
  const ROOT='/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/v4/',WORKSPACE='/communiverse/workspace/';
  const TYPES=['person','task','project','document'],LIMIT={records:500,page:20,query:160,bytes:16777216};
  const validId=value => typeof value==='string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/.test(value);
  const text=value => typeof value==='string' ? value : '';
  const list=value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  const staff=p => validId(p?.id) && p.kind==='staff' && p.verified===true && (p.status===undefined || p.status==='active');
  const abortError=() => Object.assign(Error('Reference selection canceled.'),{name:'AbortError'});
  function node(tag,cls,value) {const n=document.createElement(tag);if (cls) n.className=cls;if (value!==undefined) n.textContent=String(value);return n;}
  function button(label,action) {const b=node('button','cvt-button',label);b.type='button';b.addEventListener('click',()=>{if (!b.disabled) Promise.resolve().then(action).catch(()=>{});});return b;}
  function ref(type,raw) {
    if (!validId(raw?.id) || raw.archived || raw.archived_at || raw.status==='archived' || (raw.status!==undefined && type==='person' && raw.status!=='active')) return null;
    const label=text(type==='person' ? raw.name : raw.title || raw.name).trim().slice(0,160);if (!label) return null;
    return {type,id:raw.id,label,available:true,...(type==='task' && validId(raw.project_id) ? {project_id:raw.project_id} : {})};
  }
  function projectRows(type,rows) {
    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length>LIMIT.records) throw Error('The permitted reference list exceeds its limit. Reopen the canonical directory or Work view.');
    const seen=new Set();return rows.map(r=>ref(type,r)).filter(r=>r && !seen.has(r.id) && seen.add(r.id));
  }
  function create(options={}) {
    const getContext=options.getContext || (()=>window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.getState()),navigate=options.navigate || (view=>window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.navigate(view));
    let alive=true,epoch=0,picker=null,pickJob=null,sequence=0;const jobs=new Set();
    function context() {try {return getContext() || null;}catch {return null;}}
    function actor() {const p=context()?.person;if (!staff(p)) throw Error('A verified staff workspace is required to choose references.');return p.id;}
    function guard(job) {if (!alive || job.epoch!==epoch || job.signal.aborted) throw abortError();let current;try {current=actor();}catch {throw abortError();}if (current!==job.actor) throw abortError();}
    function newJob(signal) {
      const controller=new AbortController(),job={actor:actor(),epoch,controller,signal:controller.signal,detach:null};
      if (signal?.aborted) throw abortError();if (signal) {const stop=()=>controller.abort();signal.addEventListener('abort',stop,{once:true});job.detach=()=>signal.removeEventListener('abort',stop);}
      jobs.add(job);return job;
    }
    function finish(job) {job.controller.abort();job.detach?.();jobs.delete(job);if (pickJob===job) pickJob=null;}
    function clearDialog(dialog) {for (const n of dialog.querySelectorAll('input')) n.value='';for (const n of dialog.querySelectorAll('*')) if (!n.children.length) n.textContent='';}
    function closePicker(value=null) {
      const p=picker;if (!p) return;picker=null;p.items=[];clearInterval(p.poll);p.job.signal.removeEventListener('abort',p.abort);clearDialog(p.dialog);if (p.dialog.open) p.dialog.close();p.dialog.remove();p.resolve(value);finish(p.job);if (p.focus?.isConnected) p.focus.focus();
    }
    function reset() {epoch++;closePicker();for (const job of [...jobs]) finish(job);}
    function identityChanged(event) {
      if (event.type==='cv-chat-reset') {reset();return;}
      const incoming=event.detail?.identity ?? event.detail?.person ?? event.detail,p=incoming?.person ?? incoming;
      if (!staff(p) || [...jobs].some(j=>j.actor!==p.id)) reset();
    }
    async function read(path,params,job) {
      guard(job);
      const allowed=[ROOT+'tools/document',ROOT+'tools/search',ROOT+'bootstrap','/communiverse/api/workspace/overview'];if (!allowed.includes(path)) throw Error('Unsupported reference read.');
      const query=new URLSearchParams();for (const [key,value] of Object.entries(params || {})) if (value!==undefined && value!==null && value!=='') query.set(key,String(value));
      const controller=new AbortController(),abort=()=>controller.abort();job.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});const timeout=setTimeout(abort,18000);
      try {
        const response=await fetch(path+(query.size ? '?'+query.toString() : ''),{method:'GET',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',redirect:'error',signal:controller.signal,headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});guard(job);
        if (response.status===401 || response.status===403) {reset();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('cv-chat-reset'));throw abortError();}
        const length=Number(response.headers.get('Content-Length'));if (length>LIMIT.bytes) throw Error('The reference response exceeds its limit.');
        const encoded=await response.text();guard(job);if (new TextEncoder().encode(encoded).length>LIMIT.bytes) throw Error('The reference response exceeds its limit.');
        let data;try {data=JSON.parse(encoded);}catch {throw Error('The reference response could not be read. Retry.');}
        if (!response.ok) throw Object.assign(Error(text(data.error).slice(0,400) || 'This reference is unavailable.'),{status:response.status,code:data.code});
        if (data.person && (data.person.id!==job.actor || data.person.kind!=='staff' || data.person.verified===false || (data.person.status!==undefined && data.person.status!=='active'))) {reset();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('cv-chat-reset'));throw abortError();}
        guard(job);return data;
      }catch (error) {guard(job);if (error.name==='AbortError') throw Error('The reference read timed out. Retry.');throw error;}
      finally {clearTimeout(timeout);job.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abort);}
    }
    async function sourceAccess(id,job) {
      if (!validId(id)) throw Error('Choose a canonical source document.');
      const data=await read(ROOT+'tools/document',{id},job);guard(job);if (data.document?.id!==id || !['sheet','notebook','note','folder'].includes(data.document.kind) || !Number.isSafeInteger(data.document.version) || data.document.version<1 || data.document.archived_at) throw Error('The source document is unavailable.');return data;
    }
    async function choices(type,query,job) {
      if (type==='person') {
        // Bootstrap selects ACTIVE public staff profiles in D1 and omits
        // contacts/grants. Intersect with the existing scoped workspace team.
        const state=context();let team=state?.team ?? state?.staff;
        if (!Array.isArray(team)) {const workspace=await read('/communiverse/api/workspace/overview',{},job);team=workspace.team;}
        if (!Array.isArray(team) || team.length>LIMIT.records) throw Error('The authorized workspace staff list is unavailable.');
        const allowed=new Set(team.filter(p=>validId(p?.id) && (p.status===undefined || p.status==='active')).map(p=>p.id));
        const data=await read(ROOT+'bootstrap',{},job);if (!staff(data.person) || data.person.id!==job.actor) throw Error('Verified workspace identity could not be confirmed.');if (!Array.isArray(data.people) || data.people.length>LIMIT.records) throw Error('The active directory exceeds its supported limit.');
        const people=projectRows('person',list(data.people).filter(p=>p?.kind==='staff' && allowed.has(p.id)));
        return people.filter(p=>!query || p.label.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query.toLocaleLowerCase()));
      }
      if (type==='task' || type==='project') {
        const data=await read('/communiverse/api/workspace/overview',{},job),records=projectRows(type,data[type==='task' ? 'tasks' : 'projects']);
        return records.filter(r=>!query || r.label.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query.toLocaleLowerCase()));
      }
      if (!query) return [];
      const data=await read(ROOT+'tools/search',{q:query,limit:50},job);if (list(data.documents).length>50) throw Error('Narrow the document search to 50 results or fewer.');
      return projectRows(type,list(data.documents).filter(r=>['sheet','notebook','note','folder'].includes(r.kind))).filter(r=>r.id!==picker?.documentId);
    }
    function pickerPaint(p) {
      if (picker!==p) return;p.rows.replaceChildren();const page=p.items.slice(p.page*LIMIT.page,(p.page+1)*LIMIT.page);
      for (const item of page) {
        const row=node('div','cvt-picker-row'),choose=button('Choose',async()=>{
          choose.disabled=true;p.status.textContent='Checking current access…';
          try {
            await sourceAccess(p.documentId,p.job);let selected;
            if (item.type==='document') {const data=await sourceAccess(item.id,p.job);selected=ref('document',data.document);}
            else selected=(await choices(item.type,'',p.job)).find(r=>r.id===item.id);
            guard(p.job);if (picker!==p) return;if (!selected) throw Error('This reference is unavailable. Refresh the choices.');
            const result={type:selected.type,id:selected.id,label:selected.label,available:true,permissionChecked:true,preview:{label:selected.label}};closePicker(result);
          }catch (error) {if (picker===p && error.name!=='AbortError') {p.items=[];p.rows.replaceChildren();p.status.textContent=error.message;p.status.setAttribute('role','alert');}}
          finally {if (choose.isConnected) choose.disabled=false;}
        });choose.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose '+item.label);row.append(node('span','',item.label),choose);p.rows.append(row);
      }
      if (!page.length) p.rows.append(node('p','','No permitted references found. Try another search.'));
      p.previous.disabled=p.page===0;p.next.disabled=(p.page+1)*LIMIT.page>=p.items.length;p.count.textContent=page.length+' shown · '+p.items.length+' permitted matches in this returned list';
    }
    async function pickReference(request={}) {
      if (!TYPES.includes(request.type) || !validId(request.documentId)) throw Error('Choose a supported canonical reference.');closePicker();if (pickJob) finish(pickJob);
      const job=newJob(request.signal);pickJob=job;try {await sourceAccess(request.documentId,job);guard(job);}catch (error) {finish(job);throw error;}
      const focus=document.activeElement,host=focus?.closest('.cvt-tools') || document.querySelector('.cvt-tools') || document.body;
      const dialog=node('dialog','cvt-dialog'),heading=node('h3','',request.type==='person' ? 'Choose a permitted person' : 'Choose a permitted '+request.type),body=node('div','cvt-dialog-body'),form=node('form','cvt-search'),label=node('label','cvt-field'),input=node('input'),status=node('p','cvt-status'),rows=node('div','cvt-picker-results'),pages=node('div','cvt-pagination');
      const id='cvt-reference-'+(++sequence);heading.id=id;dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',id);label.append(node('span','','Search '+(request.type==='person' ? 'workspace people' : request.type+'s')),input);input.type='search';input.name='reference_search';input.maxLength=LIMIT.query;input.autocomplete='off';status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');rows.setAttribute('aria-label','Permitted reference choices');
      const search=node('button','cvt-button','Search');search.type='submit';form.append(label,search);body.append(form,node('p','','Choose an existing permitted record. This selection does not invite anyone, send a message, change roles, or share the sheet.'),status,rows,pages);dialog.append(heading,body,button('Cancel',()=>closePicker()));
      return new Promise(resolve=>{
        const p={job,documentId:request.documentId,type:request.type,dialog,resolve,focus,input,status,rows,items:[],page:0,readSequence:0,poll:null,abort:null};picker=p;
        p.previous=button('Previous choices',()=>{p.page--;pickerPaint(p);});p.next=button('Next choices',()=>{p.page++;pickerPaint(p);});p.count=node('span');pages.append(p.count,p.previous,p.next);
        async function load() {
          const serial=++p.readSequence;search.disabled=true;p.status.textContent='Loading permitted references…';p.rows.replaceChildren();
          try {const query=input.value.trim();if (new TextEncoder().encode(query).length>200) throw Error('Shorten the search to 200 bytes.');const records=await choices(p.type,query,p.job);guard(p.job);if (picker!==p || serial!==p.readSequence) return;p.items=records;p.page=0;pickerPaint(p);p.status.textContent=p.type==='document' && !query ? 'Enter a document title to search.' : '';p.status.setAttribute('role','status');}
          catch (error) {if (picker===p && serial===p.readSequence && error.name!=='AbortError') {p.items=[];p.rows.replaceChildren();p.status.textContent=error.message;p.status.setAttribute('role','alert');}}
          finally {if (search.isConnected && serial===p.readSequence) search.disabled=false;}
        }
        form.addEventListener('submit',event=>{event.preventDefault();void load();});dialog.addEventListener('cancel',event=>{event.preventDefault();closePicker();});
        p.abort=()=>{if (picker===p) closePicker();};job.signal.addEventListener('abort',p.abort,{once:true});
        p.poll=setInterval(()=>{try {guard(job);if (!dialog.isConnected) closePicker();}catch {if (picker===p) reset();}},1000);
        host.append(dialog);dialog.showModal();input.focus();void load();
      });
    }
    function sourceClean(root) {
      if (!root) return;if (!root.isConnected) throw abortError();
      const status=root.querySelector('.cvt-savebar>span')?.textContent;if (status && !['Saved','View only'].includes(status)) throw Error('Save or resolve sheet edits before opening this reference. No save was requested.');
    }
    async function waitForPerson(id,job) {
      const start=performance.now();while (performance.now()-start<5000) {
        guard(job);const match=[...document.querySelectorAll('#cv-ws-content .cv4-person-card button[data-person]')].find(b=>b.dataset.person===id)?.closest('.cv4-person-card');
        if (match) {match.tabIndex=-1;match.setAttribute('aria-current','true');match.style.outline='3px solid var(--cv-glass-blue, #245ccc)';match.style.outlineOffset='3px';match.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest',behavior:'auto'});match.focus({preventScroll:true});return true;}
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,40));
      }
      throw Error('The person is no longer available in the canonical People view.');
    }
    async function openReference(request={}) {
      if (!TYPES.includes(request.type) || !validId(request.id) || !validId(request.documentId)) throw Error('Choose a canonical reference.');const root=document.activeElement?.closest('.cvt-tools') || document.querySelector('.cvt-tools');sourceClean(root);const job=newJob(request.signal);
      try {
        await sourceAccess(request.documentId,job);let selected;
        if (request.type==='document') selected=ref('document',(await sourceAccess(request.id,job)).document);
        else selected=(await choices(request.type,'',job)).find(r=>r.id===request.id);
        guard(job);sourceClean(root);if (!selected) throw Error('This reference is unavailable.');
        if (selected.type==='person') {
          if (selected.id===job.actor) {const adapter=window.__cvWorkspaceNative;if (typeof adapter?.profile!=='function') throw Error('The canonical profile editor is unavailable.');const opened=await adapter.profile();guard(job);if (opened!==true) throw Error('Close the current editor before opening your profile.');return true;}
          if (typeof navigate!=='function') throw Error('The canonical People view is unavailable.');await navigate('people');guard(job);return await waitForPerson(selected.id,job);
        }
        const params=new URLSearchParams();
        if (selected.type==='document') {params.set('view','manage');params.set('section','tools');params.set('document',selected.id);}
        else {params.set('view','projects');if (selected.type==='project') params.set('project',selected.id);else {if (selected.project_id) params.set('project',selected.project_id);params.set('task',selected.id);}}
        guard(job);sourceClean(root);location.assign(WORKSPACE+'?'+params.toString());return true;
      }finally {finish(job);}
    }
    function destroy() {if (!alive) return;reset();alive=false;window.removeEventListener('cv-chat-reset',identityChanged);window.removeEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityChanged);window.removeEventListener('pagehide',destroy);}
    window.addEventListener('cv-chat-reset',identityChanged);window.addEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityChanged);window.addEventListener('pagehide',destroy);
    return {pickReference,openReference,destroy};
  }
  let current=null;
  function install(options={}) {
    const previous=current;previous?.destroy();current=create(options);const tools=window.CommuniverseTools;
    if (tools) {if (typeof tools.onPickReference!=='function' || tools.onPickReference===previous?.pickReference) tools.onPickReference=current.pickReference;if (typeof tools.onOpenReference!=='function' || tools.onOpenReference===previous?.openReference) tools.onOpenReference=current.openReference;}
    return current;
  }
  window.CommuniverseToolsReferences={create,install,pickReference:request=>(current || install()).pickReference(request),openReference:request=>(current || install()).openReference(request)};
  if (window.CommuniverseTools?.mount) install();
})();

;
/* In-page Manage Communiverse. Parent owns navigation and server authorization.
 * mount(#cv-ws-content,{person,api?,legacyApi?,initialTab?,onOpenCommunity?}).
 * api accepts relative Connect v4 paths and {method,json,signal}; legacyApi
 * accepts admin list/save/delete-preview/delete. Defaults are same-origin.
 * No account creation, invitation, password edit, provider call or storage cache.
 * Franklin's management.mjs is authoritative. List responses are projected
 * immediately; contacts are revealed only in explicit private lead details,
 * never in lists, notifications, logs, URLs or persisted drafts.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.cvManagement) return;
  const ROOT = '/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/v4/';
  const ADMIN = '/communiverse/api/admin/';
  const TABS = [['overview','Overview'],['people','People'],['onboarding','Onboarding'],['communities','Communities'],['activity','Activity'],['tools','Tools']];
  const TYPES = [['artist','Artist'],['ambassador','Ambassador'],['unspecified','Unspecified']];
  const STAGES = ['new','contacted','in-discussion','invited','onboarded','paused'];
  const CRAFTS = ['ceramics','textiles','paper','sculpture','glass','wood','metal','painting','photography','digital','mixed-media','community'];
  const REGIONS = ['uae','saudi','pakistan','philippines','malaysia','china','colombia','peru','india','uzbekistan'];
  let current = null;
  function node(tag,className,text) {
    const n = document.createElement(tag); if (className) n.className = className;
    if (text !== undefined) n.textContent = String(text ?? ''); return n;
  }
  function button(text,action,kind = '') {
    const n = node('button','cv5-management-button'+(kind ? ' cv5-management-button-'+kind : ''),text);n.type = 'button';
    n.addEventListener('click',() => { if (!n.disabled) Promise.resolve().then(action).catch(() => {}); });return n;
  }
  const list = value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  const value = v => typeof v === 'string' ? v : '';
  const label = v => value(v).replace(/[_-]/g,' ').replace(/\b\w/g,c => c.toUpperCase());
  function date(raw){const normalized=typeof raw==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d:\d\d$/.test(raw)?raw.replace(' ','T')+'Z':raw;const d=new Date(normalized);return Number.isFinite(+d)?new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'}).format(d):'Time unavailable';}
  const pill = (text,kind = '') => node('span','cv5-management-pill'+(kind ? ' cv5-management-pill-'+kind : ''),text);
  function avatar(name) {
    const a = node('span','cv5-management-avatar',value(name).trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(w=>Array.from(w)[0]||'').join('').toUpperCase() || 'C');a.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');return a;
  }
  async function responseData(response) {
    if (response && typeof response.json === 'function') {
      let data;try {data = await response.json();}catch {throw Object.assign(Error('This response could not be read. Try again.'),{status:502});}
      if (!response.ok) throw Object.assign(Error(typeof data.error === 'string' ? data.error : 'This action is unavailable.'),{status:response.status,code:data.code});return data;
    }
    if (response?.error) throw Object.assign(Error(typeof response.error === 'string' ? response.error : 'This action is unavailable.'),{status:response.status,code:response.code});
    return response || {};
  }
  const fetcher = prefix => async (path,options = {}) => {
    if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9/_-]*(?:\?.*)?$/.test(path) || path.includes('..')) throw Error('Invalid management path.');
    const {json,...init} = options;
    return responseData(await fetch(prefix+path,{...init,credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',
      headers:{Accept:'application/json',...(json === undefined ? {} : {'Content-Type':'application/json'}),...init.headers},
      ...(json === undefined ? {} : {body:JSON.stringify(json)})}));
  };
  const empty = (title,description) => {const n = node('section','cv5-management-empty');n.append(node('h2','',title),node('p','',description));return n;};
  // Whitelist summary fields. Never retain contacts or freeform notes from a
  // list response, even if a future server accidentally returns those fields.
  function summary(record) {
    const keys = ['id','name','kind','type','role','title','username','city','location','craft','stage','status','source_status','account_exists','account_status','version','can_edit','can_archive','can_assign','description','region','tags','rules','cover','official','can_manage'];
    const out = {};for (const key of keys) if (record?.[key] !== undefined) out[key] = record[key];
    // Lead summaries must not include potentially identifying notes/description.
    if (['artist','ambassador','unspecified'].includes(out.type)) {delete out.description;out.archived=record.archived===true || Boolean(record.archived_at);out.missing_fields=list(record.missing_fields).filter(v=>['name','type','location','craft','phone','email','instagram'].includes(v));}
    return out;
  }
  function mount(host,options = {}) {
    if (!host?.replaceChildren) throw new TypeError('Management needs an in-page host.');
    if (current) current.unmount();
    const params=new URL(location.href).searchParams,requested=params.get('manage'),initialTab=TABS.some(([key])=>key===options.initialTab) ? options.initialTab : TABS.some(([key])=>key===requested) ? requested : params.get('section')==='tools' ? 'tools' : 'overview';
    let person = options.person || null, metadata = {}, capabilities = {}, rows = [], tab = initialTab;
    let alive = true, initialized = false, serial = 0, detailSerial = 0, timer = null, cursor = null, busy = false;
    let filters = {q:'',type:'',stage:'',archived:'0'}, detailRecord = null, detailKind = '', dirty = false, form = null;
    let toolsControl=null,toolsGeneration=0;
    const pending = new Set(), readControllers = new Set();
    const api = options.api || fetcher(ROOT), legacyApi = options.legacyApi || fetcher(ADMIN);
    const root = node('section','cv5-management');root.setAttribute('aria-label','Manage Communiverse');
    const header = node('header','cv5-management-header'), nav = node('nav','cv5-management-tabs');nav.setAttribute('role','tablist');nav.setAttribute('aria-label','Management sections');
    const panel = node('section','cv5-management-panel');panel.id='cv5-management-panel';panel.setAttribute('role','tabpanel');panel.tabIndex = -1;
    const status = node('p','cv5-management-status');status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');status.setAttribute('aria-atomic','true');
    const content = node('div'), detail = node('section','cv5-management-drawer');detail.hidden = true;
    panel.append(status,content,detail);root.append(header,nav,panel);host.replaceChildren(root);
    const tabButtons = new Map();
    for (const [key,title] of TABS) {
      const b = node('button','cv5-management-tab',title);b.type='button';b.id='cv5-management-tab-'+key;b.setAttribute('role','tab');
      b.setAttribute('aria-controls',panel.id);b.addEventListener('click',()=>navigate(key));tabButtons.set(key,b);nav.append(b);
    }
    nav.addEventListener('keydown',e => {
      if (!['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Home','End'].includes(e.key)) return;
      const buttons = [...tabButtons.values()];let i = buttons.indexOf(document.activeElement);if (i < 0) return;
      e.preventDefault();i = e.key === 'Home' ? 0 : e.key === 'End' ? buttons.length-1 : (i+(e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1)+buttons.length)%buttons.length;
      // Manual activation preserves a user's unfinished form during arrow keys.
      buttons.forEach((b,index)=>b.tabIndex=index===i ? 0 : -1);buttons[i].focus();
    });
    function notice(text,error = false) {status.textContent = text || '';status.classList.toggle('is-error',error);}
    function setTabs() {
      for (const [key,b] of tabButtons) {b.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(tab === key));b.tabIndex = tab === key ? 0 : -1;}
      panel.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','cv5-management-tab-'+tab);
    }
    function renderHeader() {
      const heading = node('div');heading.append(node('h1','','Manage Communiverse'),node('p','cv5-management-subtitle','People, recruitment, communities and Tools, with clear account boundaries.'));
      const identity = node('div','cv5-management-identity');identity.append(avatar(person?.name),node('span','',person?.name || 'Your private workspace'));
      header.replaceChildren(heading,identity);
    }
    function can(area,action = 'read',record) {
      if(area==='tools')return initialized && verifiedStaff(person) && capabilities.canViewProspects===true;
      if(area==='people')return capabilities.legacyAccountAdmin===true;
      if(area==='communities')return capabilities.canPublishOfficialCommunities===true && record?.can_manage!==false;
      if(area!=='onboarding')return area==='overview';
      if(action==='read')return capabilities.canViewProspects===true;
      if(action==='archive')return capabilities.canViewProspects===true && record?.can_archive===true;
      if(!record?.id)return capabilities.canCreateProspects===true;
      return record.can_edit===true && (capabilities.canManageProspects===true || capabilities.canEditOwnOrAssignedProspects===true);
    }
    async function request(path,opts = {},legacy = false,isRead = true) {
      const controller = new AbortController();pending.add(controller);if (isRead) readControllers.add(controller);
      const timeout = window.setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),20000);
      try {
        const response=await (legacy ? legacyApi : api)(path,{...opts,signal:controller.signal});
        if(!alive || controller.signal.aborted)throw new DOMException('This request is no longer current.','AbortError');
        const data=await responseData(response);
        if(!alive || controller.signal.aborted)throw new DOMException('This request is no longer current.','AbortError');
        if(!legacy && path.split('?')[0]==='management/overview'){
          if(typeof data.person?.id!=='string' || !data.person.id)throw Object.assign(Error('Your workspace identity could not be checked.'),{status:403});
          if(person?.id && data.person.id!==person.id)throw Object.assign(Error('Your session changed.'),{status:401});
          if(Object.keys(capabilities).some(key=>capabilities[key]===true && data.capabilities?.[key]!==true))throw Object.assign(Error('Management access changed.'),{status:403});
        }
        return data;
      }
      catch (error) {
        if (alive && !controller.signal.aborted && (error.status === 401 || error.status === 403)) lock(error);
        if (error.name === 'AbortError') throw Error('The request timed out. No completed change has been confirmed.');
        throw error;
      } finally {window.clearTimeout(timeout);pending.delete(controller);readControllers.delete(controller);}
    }
    const get = (path,legacy = false) => request(path,{},legacy,true);
    const post = (path,json,legacy = false) => request(path,{method:'POST',json},legacy,false);
    function sender(path) {
      let previous='',requestId='';
      return payload=>{const encoded=JSON.stringify(payload);if(encoded!==previous){previous=encoded;requestId=crypto.randomUUID();}return post(path,{...payload,requestId});};
    }
    function lock(error) {
      initialized = false;serial++;detailSerial++;for (const c of pending)c.abort();
      destroyTools();
      if(timer)window.clearTimeout(timer);timer=null;cursor=null;busy=false;detailKind='';
      metadata = {};capabilities = {};rows = [];detailRecord = null;dirty = false;form = null;person=null;renderHeader();
      detail.replaceChildren();detail.hidden = true;
      const forbidden=error?.status===403,card=empty(forbidden ? 'Management is not available to your account' : 'Sign in to manage Communiverse',forbidden ? 'Management access changed or could not be checked. Private records and edits have been cleared.' : 'Your session changed. Open management again from your own workspace.');
      card.append(button('Recheck access',()=>boot()));content.replaceChildren(card);notice('');
    }
    function unavailable(error,area) {
      const forbidden = error?.status === 403;
      const card = empty(forbidden ? 'This section is not available to your account' : area+' could not load',
        forbidden ? 'Management access is checked by the server. Ask the workspace owner to review your permissions.' : error?.message || 'Your information could not be checked. Try again.');
      card.append(button('Try again',()=>forbidden || !initialized ? boot() : loadTab()));content.replaceChildren(card);notice('',false);
    }
    function abortReads() {for (const c of readControllers)c.abort();readControllers.clear();}
    async function boot() {
      const sequence = ++serial;abortReads();destroyTools();content.replaceChildren(node('div','cv5-management-loading','Checking your management access…'));
      try {
        const data = await get('management/overview');if (!alive || sequence !== serial) return;
        if (data.person?.id && person?.id && data.person.id !== person.id) {lock();return;}
        person = data.person || person;metadata = data;capabilities = data.capabilities || {};
        initialized = true;renderHeader();await loadTab();
      } catch(error) {if (alive && sequence === serial && error.status !== 401) unavailable(error,'Management');}
    }
    function discardPrompt(action) {
      detail.querySelector('.cv5-management-unsaved')?.remove();
      const warning = node('section','cv5-management-warning cv5-management-unsaved');warning.append(node('p','','You have unsaved edits. Keep editing or discard them before changing sections.'));
      const actions = node('div','cv5-management-card-actions');actions.append(button('Keep editing',()=>{warning.remove();form?.querySelector('input,textarea,select')?.focus();}),button('Discard edits',()=>{dirty=false;action();},'danger'));
      warning.append(actions);detail.prepend(warning);actions.querySelector('button')?.focus();
    }
    function navigate(next) {
      if (!alive || !tabButtons.has(next) || busy) return;
      const go = () => {destroyTools();if(timer)window.clearTimeout(timer);tab=next;filters={q:'',type:'',stage:'',archived:'0'};cursor=null;rows=[];closeDetail(false);setTabs();managementLocation(next);if (initialized) loadTab();panel.focus({preventScroll:true});};
      if (dirty) discardPrompt(go);else go();
    }
    function closeDetail(focus = true) {
      detailSerial++;detailRecord = null;detailKind = '';form = null;dirty = false;detail.replaceChildren();detail.hidden = true;
      if (focus) tabButtons.get(tab)?.focus();
    }
    const accountText = r => r.account_exists === true || r.account_status === 'linked' ? 'Account linked' : r.account_exists === false || r.account_status === 'not_linked' ? 'No account linked' : 'Account link not verified';
    function overview() {
      const prospects=metadata.prospects || {},grid = node('div','cv5-management-grid');
      const cards = [
        ['people','People',null,can('people') ? 'Manage existing accounts and catalogue profiles. Counts are checked when this section opens.' : 'Account administration requires separate server-granted access.'],
        ['onboarding','Active recruitment leads',prospects.active,'Recruitment progress and missing information. '+(Number.isSafeInteger(prospects.archived) ? prospects.archived+' archived. ' : '')+(prospects.scope==='all' ? 'All workspace leads.' : 'Your own or assigned leads.')],
        ['communities','Official communities',can('communities') ? metadata.communities?.total : null,can('communities') ? 'Canonical official communities, public details and visibility.' : 'Official-community publishing is unavailable for your account.'],
        ['tools','Tools',null,'Notebooks, editable sheets, sticky notes and folders. Keep them private or attach them to canonical tasks and projects.']
      ];
      for (const [key,title,count,copy] of cards) {
        const card = node('article','cv5-management-card');card.append(node('h2','',title),node('div','cv5-management-metric',Number.isFinite(Number(count)) && count !== null && count !== undefined ? new Intl.NumberFormat().format(count) : '—'),node('p','',copy));
        const actions = node('div','cv5-management-card-actions');actions.append(button('Open '+Object.fromEntries(TABS)[key].toLowerCase(),()=>navigate(key)));card.append(actions);grid.append(card);
      }
      const stages=node('article','cv5-management-card'),stageList=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions');stages.append(node('h2','','Recruitment stages'));
      for(const stage of STAGES){const count=prospects.byStage?.[stage];stageList.append(pill(label(stage)+' · '+(Number.isSafeInteger(count) ? count : '—')));}stages.append(stageList);
      const note = node('article','cv5-management-card');note.append(node('h2','','Recruitment is not an account'),node('p','','“Onboarded” records a recruitment stage. Account access exists only when the server confirms a linked account. Nothing here creates an account or sends an invitation automatically.'));
      content.replaceChildren(grid,stages,note);
    }
    function control(name,title,type = 'input',choices = []) {
      const wrap = node('div','cv5-management-field'),labelNode = node('label','',title),input = node(type);
      input.name = name;input.id = 'cv5-management-filter-'+name;labelNode.htmlFor=input.id;
      if (type === 'select') for (const [key,text] of choices) {const option=node('option','',text);option.value=key;input.append(option);}
      else input.type='search';input.value=filters[name] || '';wrap.append(labelNode,input);return {wrap,input};
    }
    function toolbar() {
      const bar = node('div','cv5-management-toolbar'),fields = node('div','cv5-management-filters');
      const search = control('q',tab === 'onboarding' ? 'Find a recruitment lead' : 'Search '+tab);search.input.maxLength=120;fields.append(search.wrap);
      if(tab!=='communities'){
        const optionsType = tab === 'onboarding' ? [['','All types'],...TYPES] : [['','All records'],['staff','Workspace accounts'],['member','Member accounts'],['artist','Catalogue profiles']];
        const type = control('type','Record type','select',optionsType);fields.append(type.wrap);type.input.addEventListener('change',()=>{filters.type=type.input.value;loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});});
      }
      if (tab === 'onboarding') {
        const stage = control('stage','Recruitment stage','select',[['','All stages'],...STAGES.map(s=>[s,label(s)])]);fields.append(stage.wrap);stage.input.addEventListener('change',()=>{filters.stage=stage.input.value;loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});});
        const archive=control('archived','Lead visibility','select',[['0','Active leads'],['1','Archived leads'],['all','All leads']]);fields.append(archive.wrap);archive.input.addEventListener('change',()=>{filters.archived=archive.input.value;loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});});
      }
      search.input.addEventListener('input',()=>{filters.q=search.input.value;if (timer)window.clearTimeout(timer);timer=window.setTimeout(()=>loadTab({preserveToolbar:true}),220);});
      bar.append(fields);
      if (tab === 'onboarding' && can('onboarding','write')) bar.append(button('New lead',()=>openLead(null),'primary'));
      if(tab==='communities' && can('communities','write'))bar.append(button('New official community',()=>openCommunity(null),'primary'));
      if(tab==='communities')bar.append(node('p','cv5-management-help','Search checks loaded official communities. Load more to include the next page.'));
      content.replaceChildren(bar,node('div','cv5-management-list'));
    }
    async function loadTab({append = false,preserveToolbar = false} = {}) {
      if (!alive || !initialized) return;const sequence = ++serial;abortReads();destroyTools();notice('');
      if (tab === 'overview') {overview();return;}
      const titles = Object.fromEntries(TABS);
      if(tab==='tools'){mountTools(sequence);return;}
      if(tab==='activity'){await activity(sequence,append);return;}
      if(!can(tab)){unavailable({status:403},titles[tab]);return;}
      if (!preserveToolbar && !append) toolbar();
      const resultHost = content.querySelector('.cv5-management-list');if (resultHost && !append)resultHost.replaceChildren(node('div','cv5-management-loading','Loading '+titles[tab].toLowerCase()+'…'));
      const query = new URLSearchParams();let path,legacy=false;
      if(tab==='people'){path='list';legacy=true;}
      else if(tab==='communities'){query.set('community_limit','25');if(append && cursor)query.set('community_cursor',cursor);path='management/overview?'+query;}
      else {query.set('limit','25');query.set('archived',filters.archived);if(filters.q)query.set('q',filters.q);if(filters.type)query.set('type',filters.type);if(filters.stage)query.set('stage',filters.stage);if(append && cursor)query.set('cursor',cursor);path='management/prospects?'+query;}
      try {
        const result = await get(path,legacy);if (!alive || sequence !== serial) return;
        if (result.capabilities){capabilities=result.capabilities;metadata=result;if(!can(tab)){unavailable({status:403},titles[tab]);return;}}
        const page=tab==='communities' ? result.communities || {} : result;
        cursor=page.nextCursor || null;
        const next=list(tab==='onboarding' ? page.prospects : page.items).filter(r=>tab!=='people' || ['staff','member','artist'].includes(r.kind)).map(summary);
        rows=append ? [...rows,...next.filter(r=>!rows.some(old=>old.id===r.id))] : next;
        renderRows(resultHost,page.total);
      } catch(error) {if(alive && sequence === serial && error.status !== 401)unavailable(error,titles[tab]);}
    }
    function destroyTools(){toolsGeneration++;const previous=toolsControl;toolsControl=null;try{previous?.destroy()}catch{/* Clear management DOM/state even if a delegated cleanup fails. */}}
    // Overview proves active staff in D1; its public person omits status. Any
    // explicit status from a refreshed native identity must still be active.
    function verifiedStaff(identity){return identity?.kind==='staff' && identity.verified===true && (identity.status===undefined || identity.status==='active');}
    function managementLocation(next,documentId,push=false){
      const url=new URL(location.href);if(!/^\/communiverse\/workspace\/?$/.test(url.pathname))return;
      url.searchParams.set('view','manage');
      if(next==='tools'){url.searchParams.set('section','tools');if(documentId)url.searchParams.set('document',documentId);if(TABS.some(([key])=>key===url.searchParams.get('manage')))url.searchParams.delete('manage');}
      else {url.searchParams.set('manage',next);if(url.searchParams.get('section')==='tools'){url.searchParams.delete('section');url.searchParams.delete('document');}}
      if(url.href!==location.href)history[push?'pushState':'replaceState'](history.state,'',url);
    }
    function mountTools(sequence){
      if(!can('tools')){unavailable({status:403},'Tools');return;}
      const renderer=window.CommuniverseTools;if(typeof renderer?.mount!=='function'){unavailable(Error('Tools is not loaded yet. Recheck this section after the workspace finishes loading.'),'Tools');return;}
      managementLocation('tools');const target=node('div','cv5-management-tools-host');content.replaceChildren(target);const generation=toolsGeneration;
      const identity=()=>{
        if(!alive || sequence!==serial || generation!==toolsGeneration || tab!=='tools' || !can('tools'))return null;
        if(typeof window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.getState!=='function')return person;
        const native=window.__cvWorkspaceNative.getState()?.person;
        if(native?.id!==person.id || !verifiedStaff(native)){lock();return null;}
        return native;
      };
      try{
        if(!identity())return;
        // The parent's renderer owns Tools APIs, document ACLs, saving and
        // private Cosmos via CommuniverseTools.onAsk. Do not replace that hook.
        const control=renderer.mount({target,identity,onNavigate:destination=>{
          if(!identity())return;const id=destination?.documentId??destination?.document;
          if(typeof id!=='string'||!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/.test(id))return;
          managementLocation('tools',id,true);
        }});
        if(!alive || sequence!==serial || generation!==toolsGeneration){control?.destroy();return;}
        if(typeof control?.destroy!=='function')throw Error('Tools could not initialize its private view.');toolsControl=control;
      }catch(error){if(alive && sequence===serial)unavailable(error,'Tools');}
    }
    function renderRows(hostList,total) {
      hostList.replaceChildren();
      const shown=tab==='onboarding' ? rows : rows.filter(r=>(!filters.type || r.kind===filters.type) && (!filters.q || [r.name,r.title,r.city,r.craft,r.region,r.username].some(t=>value(t).toLocaleLowerCase().includes(filters.q.toLocaleLowerCase()))));
      if (!shown.length)hostList.append(empty('No matching '+(tab === 'onboarding' ? 'leads' : tab),'Try another search or filter. Records appear here only after the server confirms access.'));
      for (const record of shown) {
        const row = node('article','cv5-management-row'),copy=node('div','cv5-management-row-copy'),meta=node('div','cv5-management-row-meta'),state=node('div','cv5-management-row-status');
        copy.append(node('h3','cv5-management-row-name',record.name || (tab==='onboarding' ? 'Unnamed lead' : 'Untitled record')));
        if (tab === 'onboarding') {
          meta.append(node('span','',label(record.type || 'unspecified')),node('span','',record.location || 'Location missing'));
          state.append(pill(label(record.stage || 'Unspecified stage')),pill(accountText(record),'muted'));
          if(record.archived)state.append(pill('Archived','warning'));
          state.append(pill(record.source_status === 'verified' ? 'Source verified' : 'User-provided · not verified',record.source_status === 'verified' ? 'muted' : 'warning'));
          const missing = list(record.missing_fields).length ? record.missing_fields : ['name','location','craft'].filter(k=>!value(record[k]).trim());
          if(missing.length)meta.append(node('span','',missing.length+' missing '+(missing.length===1?'field':'fields')));
        } else if (tab === 'people') {
          meta.append(node('span','',label(record.kind || record.role)),node('span','',record.title || record.city || ''));
          state.append(pill(['staff','member'].includes(record.kind) ? 'Existing account' : 'Catalogue profile','muted'));
          if(record.status)state.append(pill(label(record.status)));
        } else {meta.append(node('span','',label(record.craft)),node('span','',record.region ? label(record.region) : 'Region not supplied'));state.append(pill('Official community','muted'),pill(label(record.status)));}
        copy.append(meta);row.append(avatar(record.name),copy,state,button('View details',()=>tab === 'onboarding' ? openLead(record.id) : tab==='communities' ? openCommunity(record) : openAccount(record)));hostList.append(row);
      }
      const pages = node('div','cv5-management-pages');pages.append(node('span','',shown.length+' shown'+(Number.isFinite(Number(total)) && total != null ? ' of '+new Intl.NumberFormat().format(total) : tab==='people' ? ' of '+rows.length+' accessible records' : '')));
      if(cursor)pages.append(button('Load more',()=>loadTab({append:true})));hostList.append(pages);
    }
    async function activity(sequence,append=false) {
      if(!capabilities.canViewProspects){unavailable({status:403},'Activity');return;}
      if(!append)content.replaceChildren(node('p','cv5-management-loading','Loading confirmed management activity…'));
      const query=new URLSearchParams({limit:'25'});if(append&&cursor)query.set('cursor',cursor);
      try{
        const data=await get('management/activity?'+query);if(!alive||sequence!==serial)return;
        const events=list(data.items);let grid=append?content.querySelector('.cv5-management-activity'):null;
        if(!grid){grid=node('div','cv5-management-activity');content.replaceChildren(node('p','cv5-management-help','Confirmed recruitment and official-community changes you can access.'),grid)}
        const titles={'prospect-created':'Recruitment lead added','prospect-updated':'Recruitment lead updated','prospect-imported':'Recruitment lead imported','prospect-archived':'Recruitment lead archived','prospect-restored':'Recruitment lead restored','official-community-created':'Official community created','official-community-updated':'Official community updated'};
        for(const event of events){const card=node('article','cv5-management-card');card.append(node('h3','',titles[event.action]||'Management change'),node('p','',value(event.record_name)||'Authorized record'),node('p','cv5-management-help',(value(event.actor_name)||'Workspace member')+' · '+date(event.created_at)));
          if(typeof event.target==='string'&&/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,119}$/.test(event.target)){const actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions');if(event.record_kind==='prospect')actions.append(button('View lead',()=>openLead(event.target)));if(event.record_kind==='community'&&can('communities'))actions.append(button('View community',()=>openCommunity({id:event.target})));card.append(actions)}grid.append(card)}
        if(!grid.children.length)grid.append(empty('No management changes yet','Confirmed recruitment and community changes will appear here.'));
        content.querySelector('[data-activity-more]')?.remove();cursor=data.nextCursor||null;if(cursor){const more=button('Load earlier activity',()=>loadTab({append:true}));more.dataset.activityMore='';content.append(more)}
      }catch(error){if(alive&&sequence===serial&&error.status!==401)unavailable(error,'Activity')}
    }
    function detailHeader(title) {
      detail.hidden=false;detail.replaceChildren();
      const heading=node('div','cv5-management-detail-header');heading.append(node('h2','',title),button('Close details',()=>{if(busy)return;if(dirty)discardPrompt(()=>closeDetail());else closeDetail();}));detail.append(heading);
      heading.querySelector('h2').tabIndex=-1;heading.querySelector('h2').focus({preventScroll:true});
    }
    function factList(facts) {const dl=node('dl','cv5-management-facts');for(const [name,text] of facts){const group=node('div');group.append(node('dt','',name),node('dd','',text || 'Not provided'));dl.append(group);}return dl;}
    function field(parent,key,title,initial = '',options = {}) {
      const wrap=node('div','cv5-management-field'+(options.wide ? ' cv5-management-form-wide' : ''));
      const titleNode=node('label','',title),input=node(options.choices ? 'select' : options.multiline ? 'textarea' : 'input');
      input.name=key;input.id='cv5-management-field-'+key;titleNode.htmlFor=input.id;
      if(options.choices)for(const [k,t] of options.choices){const opt=node('option','',t);opt.value=k;input.append(opt);}
      else if(!options.multiline)input.type=options.type || 'text';
      input.value=String(initial ?? '');input.required=Boolean(options.required);input.maxLength=options.max || 250;input.readOnly=Boolean(options.readonly);input.autocomplete='off';
      if(options.min)input.minLength=options.min;
      if(options.multiline)input.rows=4;
      const error=node('p','cv5-management-error');error.id=input.id+'-error';error.hidden=true;input.setAttribute('aria-describedby',error.id);
      wrap.append(titleNode,input);
      if(options.help)wrap.append(node('p','cv5-management-help',options.help));wrap.append(error);parent.append(wrap);return input;
    }
    function makeForm() {
      form=node('form');form.noValidate=true;const summary=node('div','cv5-management-error-summary');summary.hidden=true;summary.tabIndex=-1;summary.setAttribute('role','alert');
      const fields=node('div','cv5-management-form-grid'),out=node('p','cv5-management-status');out.setAttribute('role','status');form.append(summary,fields,out);detail.append(form);
      form.addEventListener('input',()=>{dirty=true;});form.addEventListener('change',()=>{dirty=true;});return {form,fields,out,summary};
    }
    function validate(view) {
      const invalid=[];
      for(const control of view.form.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')){
        const error=document.getElementById(control.id+'-error');const valid=control.checkValidity();control.setAttribute('aria-invalid',String(!valid));
        if(error){error.hidden=valid;error.textContent=valid ? '' : control.validationMessage;}
        if(!valid){invalid.push(control);const disclosure=control.closest('details');if(disclosure)disclosure.open=true;}
      }
      view.summary.hidden=!invalid.length;view.summary.replaceChildren();
      if(invalid.length){view.summary.append(node('p','','Review the highlighted fields.'));const errors=node('ul');for(const control of invalid){const li=node('li'),a=node('a','',view.form.querySelector('label[for="'+control.id+'"]')?.textContent || control.name);a.href='#'+control.id;a.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();control.focus();});li.append(a);errors.append(li);}view.summary.append(errors);view.summary.focus();return false;}return true;
    }
    async function save(view,operation,success) {
      if(busy || !alive || !initialized || !view.form.isConnected || !validate(view))return;const sequence=detailSerial;busy=true;const controls=[...view.form.querySelectorAll('button')];controls.forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);view.out.textContent='Saving…';
      try{await operation();if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial || !view.form.isConnected)return;dirty=false;view.out.textContent=success;notice(success);await loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});}
      catch(error){if(alive && view.form.isConnected)view.out.textContent=(error.status===409 ? 'This record changed or could not be saved. Your edits are kept here. Review the current record before retrying.' : error.message || 'The change could not be saved. No completion has been confirmed.');}
      finally{if(sequence===detailSerial)busy=false;controls.forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);}
    }
    async function openLead(id) {
      if(busy)return;
      if(dirty){discardPrompt(()=>{dirty=false;openLead(id);});return;}
      const sequence=++detailSerial;detailHeader(id ? 'Private lead details' : 'New recruitment lead');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status','Loading private details…'));
      try{
        const result=id ? await get('management/prospects?id='+encodeURIComponent(id)) : {prospect:{type:'unspecified',stage:'new',source_status:'user-provided',version:0,assigned_to:''}};
        if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;let record=result.prospect;if(!record)throw Error('The private prospect response is unavailable.');detailRecord=record;detailKind='onboarding';detailHeader(id ? record.name || 'Unnamed lead' : 'New recruitment lead');
        detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-help','Private recruitment record. Saving a lead does not create an account or send an invitation.'));
        if(id){const missing=['name','location','craft','instagram'].filter(k=>!value(record[k]).trim());if(!record.email && !record.phone)missing.push('contact');detail.append(factList([['Recruitment stage',label(record.stage)],['Account access',accountText(record)],['Source verification',record.source_status==='verified' ? 'Source verified' : 'User-provided · not verified'],['Missing information',missing.map(label).join(', ') || 'No missing fields reported']]));}
        const editable=can('onboarding','write',record);
        const view=makeForm(),name=field(view.fields,'name','Name',record.name,{max:120,help:'A name or an explicitly supplied contact is required. Unknown names may stay blank.'});field(view.fields,'type','Recruitment type',record.type || 'unspecified',{choices:TYPES});
        field(view.fields,'location','Location',record.location,{max:200});field(view.fields,'craft','Craft / discipline',record.craft,{max:160});
        field(view.fields,'stage','Recruitment stage',record.stage,{choices:STAGES.map(s=>[s,label(s)]),help:'Onboarded is a recruitment stage, not proof of account access.'});
        field(view.fields,'instagram','Instagram handle / profile',record.instagram,{max:300,wide:true,help:'Only an explicitly supplied handle or Instagram profile. A link alone does not verify a source.'});
        field(view.fields,'source_status','Source verification',record.source_status || 'user-provided',{choices:[['user-provided','User-provided · not verified'],['verified','Verified against source']]});
        if(capabilities.canAssignProspects===true){const staff=list(options.staff).filter(p=>p&&typeof p.id==='string'&&typeof p.name==='string'&&!['member','artist','bot'].includes(p.kind)&&(!p.status||p.status==='active'));const choices=[['','Unassigned'],...staff.map(p=>[p.id,p.name])];if(record.assigned_to&&!choices.some(([id])=>id===record.assigned_to))choices.push([record.assigned_to,'Previously assigned account']);field(view.fields,'assigned_to','Assignee (optional)',record.assigned_to,{max:120,...(staff.length?{choices}:{}),help:staff.length?'Choose the staff member responsible for this lead.':'Enter an active staff account ID, or leave this unassigned.'});}
        field(view.fields,'notes','Private recruitment notes',record.notes,{multiline:true,max:4000,wide:true});
        const contacts=node('details','cv5-management-form-wide'),reveal=node('summary','cv5-management-button','Reveal private contact details');contacts.append(reveal,node('p','cv5-management-help','These contact fields stay inside this private lead detail. They are never shown in list summaries.'));
        const contactFields=node('div','cv5-management-form-grid');contacts.append(contactFields);view.fields.append(contacts);
        field(contactFields,'email','Email',record.email,{type:'email',max:254});field(contactFields,'phone','Phone',record.phone,{type:'tel',max:64});
        if(!editable)for(const control of view.form.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')){if(control.tagName==='SELECT')control.disabled=true;else control.readOnly=true;}
        const actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions');const submit=node('button','cv5-management-button cv5-management-button-primary','Save lead');submit.type='submit';submit.disabled=!editable;actions.append(submit,button('Cancel',()=>{if(busy)return;if(dirty)discardPrompt(()=>closeDetail());else closeDetail();}));view.form.append(actions);
        if(!editable)view.out.textContent='Your account can view this lead. Editing is unavailable.';
        const send=sender('management/prospects/save');view.form.addEventListener('input',()=>name.setCustomValidity(''));
        view.form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!editable || sequence!==detailSerial)return;
          const supplied=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(view.form));name.setCustomValidity([supplied.name,supplied.phone,supplied.email,supplied.instagram].some(v=>value(v).trim()) ? '' : 'Add a name or an explicitly supplied contact.');
          save(view,async()=>{const result=await send({...supplied,assigned_to:value(supplied.assigned_to ?? record.assigned_to),version:record.id ? record.version : 0,...(record.id ? {id:record.id} : {})});if(result.prospect){record=result.prospect;detailRecord=record;}},'Recruitment lead saved. No account or invitation was created.');
        });
        if(id && can('onboarding','archive',record))detail.append(button(record.archived ? 'Restore lead' : 'Archive lead',()=>previewVisibility(record,'onboarding'),record.archived ? '' : 'danger'));
      }catch(error){if(alive && sequence===detailSerial && error.status!==401){detailHeader('Lead details unavailable');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status',error.message || 'Private lead details could not load.'));}}
    }
    async function openAccount(record) {
      if(busy)return;
      if(dirty){discardPrompt(()=>{dirty=false;openAccount(record);});return;}
      const sequence=++detailSerial;detailRecord=record;detailKind=record.kind || (tab==='communities' ? 'community' : 'member');detailHeader(record.name || 'Record details');
      // This authorized list is supplied by the server. No synthetic credentials,
      // name-based permission shortcut or optional create-account path exists.
      const editable=can(tab,'write',record),view=makeForm();
      field(view.fields,'name','Name',record.name,{required:true,max:100});
      if(['artist','community'].includes(detailKind)){
        field(view.fields,'description','Description',record.description,{multiline:true,max:2500,wide:true});if(detailKind==='artist')field(view.fields,'city','City',record.city || record.title,{max:100});
      }else{
        field(view.fields,'username','Username',record.username,{readonly:true,help:'Sign-in credentials are managed separately.'});field(view.fields,'title','Role / craft title',record.title,{max:140});field(view.fields,'city','City',record.city,{max:100});
        detail.append(factList([['Account kind',label(detailKind)],['Role',label(record.role)],['Status',label(record.status)]]));
      }
      if(!editable)for(const c of view.form.querySelectorAll('input,textarea'))c.readOnly=true;
      const actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions'),submit=node('button','cv5-management-button cv5-management-button-primary','Save details');submit.type='submit';submit.disabled=!editable;actions.append(submit);view.form.append(actions);
      if(!editable)view.out.textContent='Editing this record is unavailable for your account.';
      view.form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!editable || sequence!==detailSerial)return;save(view,()=>post('save',{...Object.fromEntries(new FormData(view.form)),id:record.id,kind:detailKind,role:record.role,username:record.username},true),'Record details saved.');});
      if(can(tab,'delete',record) && record.id !== person?.id)detail.append(button('Preview permanent deletion',()=>previewChange(record,detailKind),'danger'));
      if(tab==='communities' && options.onOpenCommunity)detail.append(button('Open community',()=>options.onOpenCommunity(record.id)));
    }
    function safeCover(raw){
      if(!raw)return true;if(/[\\\s]/.test(raw)||/%(?:2e|2f|5c|00|0[ad])/i.test(raw)||/(?:^|\/)\.\.(?:\/|$)/.test(raw))return false;
      try{const url=new URL(raw,location.origin);return url.origin===location.origin && ['http:','https:'].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password && !url.hash && /^\/communiverse\/_public\/(?:[0-9]{8}-marketplace-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\/[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+|community\/[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{1,120}\/[a-zA-Z0-9-]+)\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(url.pathname) && [...url.searchParams].every(([k,v])=>k==='v' && /^\d{1,12}$/.test(v));}catch{return false;}
    }
    async function openCommunity(existing){
      if(busy)return;if(dirty){discardPrompt(()=>{dirty=false;openCommunity(existing);});return;}
      const sequence=++detailSerial;detailHeader(existing ? 'Checking community details…' : 'New official community');
      try{
