      let record=existing ? await readCommunity(existing.id) : {version:0,status:'active',tags:[],craft:'',region:''};
      if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;detailRecord=record;detailKind='community';detailHeader(existing ? record.name || 'Official community' : 'New official community');
      detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-help','Official community details use the canonical community API. Nothing here creates a member account, invitation or upload.'));
      const editable=can('communities','write',record),view=makeForm();
      field(view.fields,'name','Community name',record.name,{required:true,min:3,max:64});field(view.fields,'craft','Community craft',record.craft,{required:true,choices:[['','Choose a craft'],...CRAFTS.map(c=>[c,label(c)])]});
      field(view.fields,'region','Region (optional)',record.region,{choices:[['','Not supplied'],...REGIONS.map(r=>[r,r==='uae' ? 'UAE' : label(r)])]});
      const tags=field(view.fields,'tags','Tags',list(record.tags).join(', '),{max:500,help:'Up to 12 comma-separated tags, 40 characters each.'});
      field(view.fields,'description','Purpose / description',record.description,{required:true,min:12,max:600,multiline:true,wide:true});
      field(view.fields,'rules','Community rules',record.rules || (existing ? '' : 'Be kind. Credit artists. Share work you have permission to publish.'),{max:2000,multiline:true,wide:true});
      const cover=field(view.fields,'cover','Published cover image path (optional)',record.cover,{max:500,wide:true,help:'An allowed same-origin public Communiverse image. Uploads are managed separately.'});
      if(!editable)for(const input of view.form.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')){if(input.tagName==='SELECT')input.disabled=true;else input.readOnly=true;}
      const actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions'),submit=node('button','cv5-management-button cv5-management-button-primary',existing ? 'Save community' : 'Publish official community');submit.type='submit';submit.disabled=!editable;actions.append(submit);view.form.append(actions);
      const send=sender('management/communities/save');view.form.addEventListener('input',()=>{tags.setCustomValidity('');cover.setCustomValidity('');});
      view.form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!editable || sequence!==detailSerial)return;const fields=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(view.form)),parts=value(fields.tags).split(',').map(v=>v.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        tags.setCustomValidity(parts.length>12 || parts.some(t=>t.length>40) ? 'Use up to 12 tags, no more than 40 characters each.' : '');cover.setCustomValidity(safeCover(fields.cover) ? '' : 'Use an allowed public Communiverse image path.');
        save(view,async()=>{const result=await send({...fields,tags:parts,version:record.version,...(record.id ? {id:record.id} : {status:'active'})});if(result.community){record=result.community;detailRecord=record;}},'Official community saved.');
      });
      if(existing && editable)detail.append(button(record.status==='hidden' ? 'Restore community' : 'Hide community',()=>previewVisibility(record,'community'),record.status==='hidden' ? '' : 'danger'));
      if(existing && options.onOpenCommunity)detail.append(button('Open community',()=>options.onOpenCommunity(record.id)));
      }catch(error){if(alive && sequence===detailSerial && error.status!==401){detailHeader('Community details unavailable');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status',error.message || 'This community could not be checked.'));}}
    }
    async function readCommunity(id){
      if(typeof id!=='string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,119}$/.test(id))throw Error('Choose an available community.');
      let pageCursor=null;
      // Overview is the authoritative community list; there is no invented
      // community-detail route. Bound pagination before a visibility change.
      for(let page=0;page<20;page++){
        const data=await get('management/overview?community_limit=50'+(pageCursor ? '&community_cursor='+encodeURIComponent(pageCursor) : ''));
        if(data.capabilities?.canPublishOfficialCommunities!==true){const error=Object.assign(Error('Official-community management access changed.'),{status:403});lock(error);throw error;}
        const found=list(data.communities?.items).find(r=>r.id===id);if(found){if(!Number.isSafeInteger(found.version) || found.version<0)throw Error('This community version could not be checked. Refresh the list.');return found;}pageCursor=data.communities?.nextCursor;if(!pageCursor)break;
      }throw Error('This community could not be rechecked. Refresh the community list.');
    }
    async function previewVisibility(record,kind){
      if(busy)return;if(dirty){discardPrompt(()=>{dirty=false;previewVisibility(record,kind);});return;}
      const sequence=++detailSerial;detailHeader('Review visibility change');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status','Rechecking the current record…'));
      try{const fresh=kind==='onboarding' ? (await get('management/prospects?id='+encodeURIComponent(record.id))).prospect : await readCommunity(record.id);
        if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;if(!fresh || !can(kind==='onboarding' ? 'onboarding' : 'communities',kind==='onboarding' ? 'archive' : 'write',fresh))throw Error('This visibility change is unavailable.');
        renderVisibility(fresh,kind,sequence,kind==='onboarding' ? !fresh.archived : fresh.status==='hidden' ? 'active' : 'hidden');
      }catch(error){if(alive && sequence===detailSerial && error.status!==401){detailHeader('Visibility preview unavailable');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status',error.message));}}
    }
    function renderVisibility(record,kind,sequence,next){
      const lead=kind==='onboarding',restoring=lead ? next===false : next==='active',exact=record.name || record.id;
      detailHeader((restoring ? 'Restore ' : lead ? 'Archive ' : 'Hide ')+(record.name || 'unnamed lead'));
      detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-warning',lead ? 'Only this recruitment lead changes active/archive visibility. Its fields and history are retained. No account, invitation or contact message changes.' : 'Only this official community changes status to '+next+'. Its record and connected data are retained; no records are deleted.'));
      const impact=node('ul','cv5-management-impact'),item=node('li');item.append(node('span','',lead ? 'Recruitment lead visibility' : 'Official community visibility'),node('span','cv5-management-impact-count','1'));impact.append(item);detail.append(impact,node('p','cv5-management-help','Record '+record.id+' · version '+record.version+'. This is a reversible status change, not permanent deletion.'));
      const view=makeForm(),input=field(view.fields,'confirm','Type the exact '+(record.name ? 'name' : 'record ID')+' to confirm','',{required:true,wide:true,help:exact,max:200}),actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions'),confirm=node('button','cv5-management-button'+(restoring ? '' : ' cv5-management-button-danger'),restoring ? lead ? 'Restore lead' : 'Restore community' : lead ? 'Archive lead' : 'Hide community');confirm.type='submit';confirm.disabled=true;
      input.addEventListener('input',()=>{confirm.disabled=input.value!==exact;});actions.append(button('Cancel',()=>{if(!busy)closeDetail();}),confirm);view.form.append(actions);const send=sender(lead ? 'management/prospects/archive' : 'management/communities/save');
      view.form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(busy || !alive || sequence!==detailSerial || input.value!==exact)return;busy=true;confirm.disabled=true;view.out.textContent='Rechecking the record version…';
        try{const fresh=lead ? (await get('management/prospects?id='+encodeURIComponent(record.id))).prospect : await readCommunity(record.id);if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;
          if(!fresh || fresh.version!==record.version || fresh.name!==record.name || (lead ? fresh.archived!==record.archived : fresh.status!==record.status)){if(fresh)renderVisibility(fresh,kind,sequence,next);notice('The record changed. Review the updated visibility preview and confirm again.',true);return;}
          if(input.value!==exact)return;
          await send({id:record.id,version:record.version,...(lead ? {archived:next} : {status:next})});if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;
          closeDetail(false);await loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});notice(lead ? restoring ? 'Recruitment lead restored.' : 'Recruitment lead archived.' : restoring ? 'Official community restored.' : 'Official community hidden.');
        }catch(error){if(alive && view.form.isConnected)view.out.textContent=error.message || 'No completed change has been confirmed. Reopen the preview before retrying.';}
        finally{busy=false;confirm.disabled=input.value!==exact;}
      });input.focus();
    }
    const fingerprint = preview => JSON.stringify({id:preview.entity?.id,name:preview.entity?.name,kind:preview.entity?.kind,total:preview.total,affected:list(preview.affected).map(r=>[r.table,r.count]).sort()});
    async function previewChange(record,kind) {
      if(busy || dirty){if(dirty)discardPrompt(()=>{dirty=false;previewChange(record,kind);});return;}
      const sequence=++detailSerial;detailHeader('Review permanent deletion');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status','Checking the exact affected records…'));
      try{const preview=await post('delete-preview',{id:record.id,kind},true);if(alive && sequence===detailSerial)renderPreview(preview,record,kind,sequence);}
      catch(error){if(alive && sequence===detailSerial && error.status!==401){detailHeader('Impact preview unavailable');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status','This action is unavailable until its exact impact can be checked. '+(error.message || 'Try again.')));}}
    }
    function renderPreview(preview,record,kind,sequence) {
      if(!preview.entity?.id || preview.entity.id!==record.id || preview.entity.kind!==kind || typeof preview.entity.name!=='string' || !preview.entity.name || !Array.isArray(preview.affected) || !preview.affected.every(r=>typeof r.table==='string' && Number.isSafeInteger(r.count) && r.count>=0) || !Number.isSafeInteger(preview.total) || preview.total!==preview.affected.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.count,0)){
        detailHeader('Impact preview unavailable');detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-status','The server did not return a complete impact preview. No change is available.'));return;
      }
      detailHeader('Permanently delete '+preview.entity.name);
      detail.append(node('p','cv5-management-warning','This permanently removes this entry and the connected records listed below. This action cannot be undone.'));
      const impact=node('ul','cv5-management-impact');for(const item of preview.affected){const row=node('li');row.append(node('span','',item.label || label(item.table?.replace(/^cv_/,''))),node('span','cv5-management-impact-count',Number(item.count)));impact.append(row);}detail.append(impact,node('p','cv5-management-help',preview.total+' records affected.'));
      const view=makeForm();const input=field(view.fields,'confirm','Type the exact name to confirm','',{required:true,wide:true,help:preview.entity.name,max:200});
      const actions=node('div','cv5-management-card-actions'),confirm=node('button','cv5-management-button cv5-management-button-danger','Delete permanently');confirm.type='submit';confirm.disabled=true;
      input.addEventListener('input',()=>{confirm.disabled=input.value!==preview.entity.name;});actions.append(button('Cancel',()=>{if(!busy)closeDetail();}),confirm);view.form.append(actions);
      view.form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
        e.preventDefault();if(busy || !alive || sequence!==detailSerial || input.value!==preview.entity.name)return;
        busy=true;confirm.disabled=true;view.out.textContent='Checking impact again…';
        try{
          const refreshed=await post('delete-preview',{id:record.id,kind},true);
          if(!alive || sequence!==detailSerial)return;
          if(fingerprint(refreshed)!==fingerprint(preview)){renderPreview(refreshed,record,kind,sequence);notice('The impact changed. Review the updated records and confirm again.',true);return;}
          if(input.value!==preview.entity.name){view.out.textContent='Type the exact name again before confirming.';return;}
          await post('delete',{id:record.id,kind,confirm:input.value},true);
          if(!alive)return;closeDetail(false);await loadTab({preserveToolbar:true});notice('Deletion confirmed by the server.');
        }catch(error){if(alive && view.form.isConnected)view.out.textContent=error.message || 'No completed change has been confirmed. Reopen the impact preview before retrying.';}
        finally{busy=false;confirm.disabled=input.value!==preview.entity.name;}
      });input.focus();
    }
    function unmount() {
      if(!alive)return;alive=false;serial++;detailSerial++;if(timer)window.clearTimeout(timer);for(const c of pending)c.abort();pending.clear();readControllers.clear();
      destroyTools();window.removeEventListener('cv-chat-reset',lock);window.removeEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityChanged);window.removeEventListener('pagehide',unmount);
      rows=[];metadata={};capabilities={};detailRecord=null;form=null;person=null;root.remove();if(current===controller)current=null;
    }
    const controller={unmount,refresh:()=>boot(),selectTab:navigate};current=controller;
    function identityChanged(event){const incoming=event.detail?.identity??event.detail?.person??event.detail,identity=incoming?.person??incoming;if(identity?.id!==person?.id || !verifiedStaff(identity))lock();}
    window.addEventListener('cv-chat-reset',lock);window.addEventListener('communiverse:identity-change',identityChanged);window.addEventListener('pagehide',unmount);
    renderHeader();setTabs();boot().catch(()=>{});return controller;
  }
  window.cvManagement={mount,unmount:()=>current?.unmount()};
})();

;
/* Server-backed task progress. Canonical tasks, native forms and permissions stay intact. */
(()=>{'use strict';if(window.cvWorkProgress)return;
const API='/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/v4/work/';
const node=(tag,cls,value)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(value!==undefined)e.textContent=String(value??'');return e};
const button=(label,fn,cls='cv-quiet-button')=>{const b=node('button',cls,label);b.type='button';if(fn)b.addEventListener('click',fn);return b};
const list=v=>Array.isArray(v)?v:[],percent=v=>v!==null&&v!==undefined&&v!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(v))&&Number(v)>=0&&Number(v)<=100?Number(v):null;
let state=null,actor='',blockedActor='',generation=0,scheduled=false,stopped=false,serial=0,panel=null;
const records=new Map(),projectSets=new Map(),loads=new Map(),controllers=new Set(),painted=new WeakMap(),documents=new Map(),projectOriginals=new WeakMap();
function normalize(raw){
 if(!raw||typeof raw.task_id!=='string')return null;
 const source=['checklist','manual','status','unset'].includes(raw.source)?raw.source:'unset';
 return {...raw,source,percent:source==='unset'?null:percent(raw.percent),items:list(raw.items).filter(i=>i&&typeof i.id==='string').map(i=>({...i,title:String(i.title??''),done:i.done===true||i.done===1})).sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.position)||0)-(Number(b.position)||0))};
}
function unpack(data){if(Array.isArray(data))return data;if(Array.isArray(data?.records))return data.records;if(Array.isArray(data?.progress))return data.progress;return data?.progress?.task_id?[data.progress]:data?.record?.task_id?[data.record]:data?.task_id?[data]:[];}
function teardownDocuments(){for(const entry of documents.values()){try{if(typeof entry.cleanup==='function')entry.cleanup();else entry.cleanup?.unmount?.()}catch{}entry.section.remove()}documents.clear();}
function restoreProjectPresentation(){document.querySelectorAll('[data-cvp-native-project]').forEach(card=>{const old=projectOriginals.get(card);if(old){if(old.bar?.isConnected)old.bar.hidden=old.hidden;if(old.meta?.isConnected)old.meta.textContent=old.text;projectOriginals.delete(card)}delete card.dataset.cvpNativeProject;});}
function clearIdentity(){generation++;for(const c of controllers)c.abort();controllers.clear();loads.clear();records.clear();projectSets.clear();panel?.section.remove();panel=null;teardownDocuments();restoreProjectPresentation();document.querySelectorAll('.cvp-task-progress,.cvp-project-progress').forEach(n=>n.remove());state=null;if(actor)blockedActor=actor;actor='';}
function failIdentity(message){clearIdentity();window.cvWorkPolish?.revoke(message);window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh?.();}
function apply(next){
 if(!next?.person?.id)return;
 const id=String(next.person.id);if(blockedActor===id)return;
 if(actor&&actor!==id)clearIdentity();blockedActor='';actor=id;state=next;enhance();void load({},false).catch(()=>{});
}
function task(id){return state?.tasks?.find(t=>String(t.id)===String(id));}
function validVersion(value,min=0){return Number.isInteger(value)&&value>=min;}
function canEdit(t){const p=state?.person,r=records.get(String(t?.id));if(!t||!p)return false;if(typeof r?.can_edit==='boolean')return r.can_edit;return !!(state.permissions?.admin||t.owner_id===p.id||t.created_by===p.id||state.projects?.find(x=>x.id===t.project_id)?.owner_id===p.id);}
function canManual(t,r){return typeof r?.can_manual==='boolean'?r.can_manual:list(r?.items).length===0&&t?.status!=='done';}
function progressFor(t){const r=records.get(String(t?.id)),newerNative=validVersion(r?.native_version,1)&&validVersion(t?.version,1)&&r.native_version>t.version;return t?.status==='done'&&!newerNative?{...r,task_id:t.id,percent:100,source:'status',items:r?.items||[]}:r||{task_id:t?.id,percent:null,source:'unset',items:[]};}
function editor(record,t){
 const id=record?.updated_by||t?.updated_by,name=record?.updated_by_name||t?.updated_by_name||state?.team?.find(p=>p.id===id)?.name;
 const raw=record?.updated_at||t?.updated_at;
 if(!id&&!name)return 'Last editor not recorded';
 let stamp='';if(raw){const d=new Date(String(raw).includes('T')?raw:String(raw).replace(' ','T')+'Z');if(Number.isFinite(+d))stamp=new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}).format(d);}
 return 'Updated by '+(name||String(id))+(stamp?' · '+stamp:'');
}
function sourceText(r){return r.source==='checklist'?String(Number(r.completed)||0)+' of '+String(Number(r.total)||0)+' checklist items complete':r.source==='manual'?'Recorded progress':r.source==='status'?'Task completed':'Progress not set';}
function ring(value,label){
 const box=node('span','cvp-progress-ring'),svg=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','svg');svg.setAttribute('viewBox','0 0 40 40');svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');svg.setAttribute('focusable','false');
 for(const [kind,offset]of [['track',0],['value',value===null?100:100-value]]){const c=document.createElementNS(svg.namespaceURI,'circle');for(const [k,v]of Object.entries({cx:20,cy:20,r:16,fill:'none',pathLength:100,'stroke-dasharray':100,'stroke-dashoffset':offset,transform:'rotate(-90 20 20)',class:'cvp-progress-'+kind}))c.setAttribute(k,String(v));svg.append(c)}
 box.append(svg,node('span','cvp-progress-number',value===null?'—':new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:1}).format(value)+'%'));
 box.setAttribute('role',value===null?'img':'progressbar');box.setAttribute('aria-label',label);box.setAttribute('aria-valuetext',value===null?'Progress not set':value+' percent');
 if(value!==null){box.setAttribute('aria-valuemin','0');box.setAttribute('aria-valuemax','100');box.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(value))}else box.dataset.unset='true';return box;
}
function decorateTask(card,t){
 const r=progressFor(t),signature=JSON.stringify([actor,r.percent,r.source,r.completed,r.total,editor(r,t)]);let out=card.querySelector('.cvp-task-progress');
 if(out&&painted.get(out)===signature)return;
 if(!out){out=node('div','cvp-task-progress');out.setAttribute('aria-label','Task progress and last editor');card.append(out)}
 const text=node('div','cvp-progress-copy');text.append(node('span','cvp-progress-source',sourceText(r)),node('small','cvp-progress-editor',editor(r,t)));out.replaceChildren(ring(r.percent,(t.title||'Task')+' progress'),text);painted.set(out,signature);
}
function projectProgress(id){
 const p=state?.projects?.find(p=>String(p.id)===String(id)),tasks=list(state?.tasks).filter(t=>String(t.project_id)===String(id)&&t.status!=='archived');
 const set=projectSets.get(String(id));const values=set?set.map(id=>progressFor(task(id)||{id})):tasks.map(progressFor);
 const expected=p?.total===undefined?values.length:Number(p.total),known=values.filter(r=>r.percent!==null&&r.percent!==undefined);
 return {percent:expected>0&&values.length===expected&&known.length===expected?known.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.percent,0)/expected:null,known:known.length,total:Math.max(expected||0,values.length),records:values};
}
function decorateProject(card,id){
 // One progress indicator. Retain the factual completed-task count, without
 // presenting its status ratio as a second competing completion percentage.
 const legacy=card.querySelector('progress.cv-ws-progress'),meta=card.querySelector('p.cv-ws-meta');
 if(legacy){let old=projectOriginals.get(card);if(!old||old.bar!==legacy||old.meta!==meta){old={bar:legacy,hidden:legacy.hidden,meta,text:meta?.textContent};projectOriginals.set(card,old)}
  card.dataset.cvpNativeProject='true';legacy.hidden=true;
  const count=meta?.textContent.match(/^(\d+ of \d+ tasks) done(?: · \d+%)?(.*)$/);if(count){old.text=meta.textContent;meta.textContent=count[1]+' completed'+count[2]}
 }
 const p=projectProgress(id),signature=JSON.stringify([actor,p.percent,p.known,p.total,p.records.map(r=>[r.task_id,r.updated_by,r.updated_by_name,r.updated_at])]);let out=card.querySelector('.cvp-project-progress');if(out&&painted.get(out)===signature)return;
 if(!out){out=node('div','cvp-project-progress');card.append(out)}
 const time=r=>{const value=String(r.updated_at||''),d=new Date(value.includes('T')?value:value.replace(' ','T')+'Z');return Number.isFinite(+d)?+d:0;};
 const latest=p.records.filter(r=>(r.updated_by||r.updated_by_name)&&time(r)>0).sort((a,b)=>time(b)-time(a))[0];
 const copy=node('div','cvp-progress-copy');copy.append(node('span','cvp-progress-source',p.percent===null?'Progress coverage: '+p.known+' of '+p.total+' tasks':'Average recorded task progress'),node('small','cvp-progress-editor',latest?'Latest task update · '+editor(latest,task(latest.task_id)):p.total?'Task editors not recorded.':'No tasks recorded yet.'));
 out.replaceChildren(ring(p.percent,'Project task progress'),copy);painted.set(out,signature);
}
async function request(path,options={}){
 const scope=generation,id=actor;if(!id||stopped)throw Error('Reopen Work to verify your account.');const controller=new AbortController();controllers.add(controller);const timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),20000);
 try{const r=await fetch(API+path,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',...options,signal:controller.signal,headers:{Accept:'application/json',...(options.body?{'Content-Type':'application/json'}:{}),...options.headers}});let d;try{d=await r.json()}catch{throw Error('Work returned an unreadable response. Try again.')}
  if(scope!==generation||actor!==id)throw Object.assign(Error('Your account changed.'),{stale:true});
  if(r.status===401||d?.person?.id&&String(d.person.id)!==id){failIdentity('Your account changed or expired. Reopening Work…');throw Object.assign(Error('Reopen Work after signing in.'),{stale:true})}
  if(!r.ok)throw Object.assign(Error(typeof d?.error==='string'?d.error:'Progress could not be saved. Try again.'),{status:r.status});return d;
 }catch(e){if(e.name==='AbortError')throw Object.assign(Error('Work took too long to respond. Your entry is retained; try again.'),{stale:scope!==generation});throw e}finally{clearTimeout(timeout);controllers.delete(controller)}
}
async function load(query={},force=false){
 const key=actor+':'+new URLSearchParams(query),entry=loads.get(key);if(entry?.pending)return entry.pending;if(!force&&entry&&Date.now()-entry.at<30000)return entry.value;
 const scope=generation,id=actor;
 const pending=(async()=>{
  const collected=new Map(),cursors=new Set();let cursor='';
  for(let page=0;page<20;page++){
   const params=new URLSearchParams({limit:'100',...query});if(cursor)params.set('cursor',cursor);
   const data=await request('progress?'+params);if(scope!==generation||actor!==id)return [];
   for(const r of unpack(data).map(normalize).filter(Boolean))collected.set(r.task_id,r);
   if(data?.nextCursor===null||data?.nextCursor===undefined)break;
   if(typeof data.nextCursor!=='string'||!data.nextCursor||cursors.has(data.nextCursor))throw Error('Work returned an invalid progress cursor. Try again.');
   cursor=data.nextCursor;cursors.add(cursor);if(page===19)throw Error('Progress is too large to load at once. Open a project or task to narrow the scope.');
  }
  const next=[...collected.values()];for(const r of next)records.set(r.task_id,r);
  if(query.project_id)projectSets.set(String(query.project_id),next.map(r=>r.task_id));
  loads.set(key,{at:Date.now(),value:next});enhance();if(panel&&next.some(r=>r.task_id===panel.id))paintPanel(panel);return next;
 })().catch(error=>{if(scope===generation)loads.set(key,{at:Date.now(),value:[]});throw error});loads.set(key,{pending});return pending;
}
function attachDocuments(form,type,id){
 if(!id||!actor)return;const key=actor+':'+type+':'+id;let entry=documents.get(key);
 if(entry&&entry.form!==form){try{if(typeof entry.cleanup==='function')entry.cleanup();else entry.cleanup?.unmount?.()}catch{}entry.section.remove();documents.delete(key);entry=null;}
 if(!entry){const section=node('section','cvp-work-documents');section.setAttribute('aria-label','Attached documents');section.append(node('h3','','Documents'));const target=node('div','cvp-documents-target'),status=node('p','cvp-progress-status');status.setAttribute('role','status');section.append(target,status);form.after(section);entry={form,section,target,status,type,id,mounted:false};documents.set(key,entry);}
 if(!entry.mounted&&typeof window.CommuniverseTools?.mountAttachments==='function'){
  try{entry.cleanup=window.CommuniverseTools.mountAttachments({target:entry.target,type,id,identity:state.person});entry.mounted=true;entry.status.textContent='';entry.section.querySelector('button')?.remove()}catch{entry.status.textContent='Documents could not open. Try again.';}
 }
 if(!entry.mounted&&!entry.section.querySelector('button')){entry.status.textContent='Document tools are not loaded yet.';entry.section.append(button('Open documents',()=>attachDocuments(form,type,id)));}
}
function mountPanel(form,id){
 if(panel?.form===form&&panel.actor===actor&&panel.id===id)return;
 panel?.section.remove();const section=node('section','cvp-progress-detail');section.setAttribute('aria-label','Task progress and checklist');form.after(section);
 panel={form,id,actor,section,scope:generation,busy:false,dirty:false,drafts:new Map(),keys:new Map(),message:'',error:false};const mounted=panel;paintPanel(panel);void load({task_id:id},true).catch(e=>panelError(mounted,e));
}
function panelError(p,error){if(!p||p!==panel||p.scope!==generation||error.stale)return;p.busy=false;p.message=error.status===409?'This progress changed. Your entry is retained; review the latest version and try again.':error.message;p.error=true;if(error.status===403){records.delete(p.id);p.section.replaceChildren(node('p','cvp-progress-status is-error','Task progress is unavailable. Reopen the task to check your access.'));return}paintPanel(p,true);}
function inputField(p,key,label,value,type='text'){
 const wrap=node('label','cvp-progress-field',label),input=node('input');input.type=type;input.value=p.drafts.has(key)?p.drafts.get(key):String(value??'');input.dataset.field=key;input.id='cvp-progress-input-'+(++serial);wrap.htmlFor=input.id;
 input.addEventListener('input',()=>{p.dirty=true;p.drafts.set(key,input.value)});wrap.append(input);return {wrap,input};
}
function paintPanel(p,force=false){
 if(!p||p!==panel||p.scope!==generation||!p.form.isConnected)return;
 const t=task(p.id)||{id:p.id},r=progressFor(t),editable=canEdit(t);let summary=p.section.querySelector('.cvp-progress-summary');
 const description=sourceText(r),lastEditor=editor(r,t),sig=JSON.stringify([r.percent,description,lastEditor]);
 if(!summary){summary=node('div','cvp-progress-summary');p.section.prepend(summary)}
 if(p.summary!==sig){const copy=node('div','cvp-progress-copy');copy.append(node('h3','','Task progress'),node('p','cvp-progress-source',description),node('small','cvp-progress-editor',lastEditor));summary.replaceChildren(ring(r.percent,'Task progress'),copy);p.summary=sig;}
 if(p.dirty&&!force&&!p.busy){const out=p.section.querySelector('.cvp-progress-status');if(out&&p.renderVersion!==r.version)out.textContent='Progress changed elsewhere. Your entry is retained; saving will check its version.';return}
 p.section.querySelector('.cvp-progress-editor-body')?.remove();const body=node('div','cvp-progress-editor-body'),status=node('p','cvp-progress-status'+(p.error?' is-error':''),p.message);status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
 const loaded=records.has(p.id);p.renderVersion=r.version;
 if(!loaded){body.append(node('p','cvp-progress-help','Loading recorded progress…'),button('Retry progress',()=>{void load({task_id:p.id},true).catch(e=>panelError(p,e))}));}
 else{
  const manual=node('form','cvp-manual-progress'),field=inputField(p,'manual','Progress (%)',r.source==='manual'?r.percent:'','number');field.input.min='0';field.input.max='100';field.input.step='1';field.input.required=true;
  const save=button('Save progress',null,'cv-button');save.type='submit';const checklist=list(r.items),manualDisabled=!canManual(t,r),versionsLoaded=validVersion(r.version);field.input.disabled=!editable||manualDisabled||!versionsLoaded||p.busy;save.disabled=!editable||manualDisabled||!versionsLoaded||p.busy;
  manual.append(field.wrap,save);manual.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const value=field.input.value.trim(),n=percent(value);if(n===null||!Number.isInteger(n)){p.message='Enter a whole percentage from 0 to 100.';p.error=true;paintPanel(p,true);return;}void mutate(p,'progress',{task_id:p.id,version:r.version,manual_percent:n});});body.append(manual);
  if(manualDisabled)body.append(node('p','cvp-progress-help',r.source==='status'&&r.percent===100?'Completed tasks show 100% from their canonical status.':checklist.length?'Checklist items determine progress. Manual progress is available when the checklist is empty.':'Manual progress is unavailable for this task.'));
  const heading=node('h4','','Checklist');body.append(heading);const items=node('ul','cvp-progress-items');
  for(const item of checklist){
   const row=node('li','cvp-checklist-item');row.dataset.itemId=item.id;const label=node('label','cvp-checklist-label'),check=node('input'),itemEditable=editable&&versionsLoaded&&validVersion(item.version,1);check.type='checkbox';check.checked=item.done;check.disabled=!itemEditable||p.busy;label.append(check,node('span','',item.title));row.append(label);
   check.addEventListener('change',()=>{const next=check.checked;check.checked=item.done;void mutate(p,'checklist',{task_id:p.id,action:'update',id:item.id,version:item.version,progress_version:r.version,done:next});});
   if(editable){const edit=node('details','cvp-checklist-edit'),toggle=node('summary','','Edit item');toggle.setAttribute('aria-label','Edit '+item.title);const form=node('form','cvp-checklist-edit-form'),title=inputField(p,'item:'+item.id,'Item',item.title);title.input.maxLength=200;title.input.required=true;title.input.disabled=p.busy;
    const update=button('Save item',null),remove=button('Delete item',()=>{remove.hidden=true;confirm.hidden=false;cancel.hidden=false;prompt.hidden=false;}),confirm=button('Confirm deletion',()=>mutate(p,'checklist',{task_id:p.id,action:'delete',id:item.id,version:item.version,progress_version:r.version})),cancel=button('Keep item',()=>{remove.hidden=false;confirm.hidden=true;cancel.hidden=true;prompt.hidden=true;}),prompt=node('p','cvp-progress-help','Delete this checklist item?');
    update.type='submit';for(const b of [update,remove,confirm,cancel])b.disabled=!itemEditable||p.busy;title.input.disabled=!itemEditable||p.busy;confirm.hidden=true;cancel.hidden=true;prompt.hidden=true;
    form.append(title.wrap,update,remove,prompt,confirm,cancel);form.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const value=title.input.value.trim();if(value)void mutate(p,'checklist',{task_id:p.id,action:'update',id:item.id,version:item.version,progress_version:r.version,title:value});});edit.append(toggle,form);row.append(edit);
   }items.append(row);
  }
  if(!checklist.length)items.append(node('li','cvp-progress-help','No checklist items recorded.'));body.append(items);
  if(editable){const add=node('form','cvp-checklist-add'),field=inputField(p,'add','New checklist item','');field.input.maxLength=200;field.input.required=true;field.input.disabled=!versionsLoaded||p.busy;const send=button('Add item',null,'cv-quiet-button');send.type='submit';send.disabled=!versionsLoaded||p.busy;add.append(field.wrap,send);add.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const value=field.input.value.trim();if(value)void mutate(p,'checklist',{task_id:p.id,action:'add',version:r.version,title:value});});body.append(add);}
  else body.append(node('p','cvp-progress-help','The task owner, creator or project lead can update progress.'));
 }
 body.append(status);p.section.append(body);
}
async function mutate(p,path,payload){
 const t=task(p.id)||{id:p.id},r=records.get(p.id);
 if(p!==panel||p.busy||p.scope!==generation||!p.form.isConnected||!canEdit(t))return;
 if(path==='progress'&&!canManual(t,r))return;
 if(!validVersion(payload.version,path==='checklist'&&payload.action!=='add'?1:0)||path==='checklist'&&payload.action!=='add'&&!validVersion(payload.progress_version)){p.message='Refresh progress to load a valid version before saving.';p.error=true;paintPanel(p,true);return;}
 const fingerprint=JSON.stringify([path,payload]);if(!p.keys.has(fingerprint))p.keys.set(fingerprint,crypto.randomUUID());const requestId=p.keys.get(fingerprint);
 p.busy=true;p.message='Saving…';p.error=false;paintPanel(p,true);
 try{await request(path,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({...payload,requestId})});if(p!==panel||p.scope!==generation)return;
  p.dirty=false;p.drafts.clear();p.keys.clear();await load({task_id:p.id},true);if(p!==panel||p.scope!==generation)return;p.message='Progress saved.';p.error=false;
 }catch(e){if(e.status===409){try{await load({task_id:p.id},true)}catch{}}panelError(p,e);return}
 finally{p.busy=false;if(p===panel&&p.scope===generation)paintPanel(p,true)}
}
function enhance(){
 if(!actor||!state||stopped)return;const host=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-content');
 if(host){for(const card of host.querySelectorAll('.cvp-task[data-task-id]')){const t=task(card.dataset.taskId);if(t)decorateTask(card,t)}for(const b of host.querySelectorAll('[data-ws-project]')){const card=b.closest('.cv-ws-card');if(card)decorateProject(card,b.dataset.wsProject)}}
 const dialog=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog');
 if(dialog?.open){const form=dialog.querySelector('#cv-ws-task-form'),project=dialog.querySelector('#cv-ws-project-form');
  if(form?.dataset.id){mountPanel(form,String(form.dataset.id));attachDocuments(form,'task',String(form.dataset.id));}
  else{panel?.section.remove();panel=null;const file=dialog.querySelector('form[data-ws-enhanced=file][data-target]'),fallback=file?.dataset.target;
   // Read-only native project details have no editable project form. Its
   // retained file form supplies the canonical ID, not a guessed click target.
   const id=project?.dataset.id||(state.projects?.some(p=>String(p.id)===fallback)?fallback:''),anchor=project||file;
   if(id&&anchor){attachDocuments(anchor,'project',String(id));const entry=documents.get(actor+':project:'+id);if(entry){decorateProject(entry.section,String(id));void load({project_id:String(id)}).catch(()=>{});}}
  }
 }else{panel?.section.remove();panel=null;teardownDocuments();}
 for(const [key,entry]of documents)if(!entry.form.isConnected){try{if(typeof entry.cleanup==='function')entry.cleanup();else entry.cleanup?.unmount?.()}catch{}entry.section.remove();documents.delete(key)}
}
function schedule(){if(scheduled||stopped)return;scheduled=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;const native=window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.getState();if(native?.person?.id&&String(native.person.id)!==blockedActor){if(actor&&String(native.person.id)!==actor){failIdentity('Your account changed. Reopening Work…');return;}actor=String(native.person.id);state=native;}enhance();});}
const observer=new MutationObserver(schedule);observer.observe(document.body||document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['open','data-id']});
window.addEventListener('cv-tools-ready',schedule);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden&&actor)void load({},true).catch(()=>{});});
window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{stopped=true;observer.disconnect();clearIdentity();},{once:true});
window.cvWorkProgress={apply,decorateTask,enhance,refresh:()=>load({},true),clearIdentity,release:'communiverse-work-progress-20261012',normalize,progressFor,projectProgress};
schedule();
})();

/* A presentation of canonical workspace tasks, not a second task store. */
(()=>{'use strict';if(window.cvWorkPolish)return;
const STATES=[['todo','To do'],['doing','In progress'],['blocked','Blocked'],['review','For review'],['done','Done']];
const node=(tag,cls,text)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=String(text??'');return e};
const button=(text,fn,cls='cv-quiet-button')=>{const e=node('button',cls,text);e.type='button';if(fn)e.addEventListener('click',fn);return e};
const options=(select,values)=>{for(const [key,title]of values){const e=node('option','',title);e.value=key;select.append(e)}};
let current=null,lastActor='',lockedActor='',layout='board',lane='todo',owner='',fingerprint='',saving=false,dragged='',timer=null,polling=false;
function destroy(){if(current){current.region.remove();current.original.hidden=false;current.state=null;current.tasks=[];current.cards?.clear();current=null}fingerprint='';clearInterval(timer);timer=null;dragged=''}
function revoke(message='Your account changed. Reopening Work…'){
 const host=current?.host||document.querySelector('#cv-ws-content');lockedActor=current?.actor||lastActor||window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.getState()?.person?.id||'';
 destroy();owner='';lastActor='';saving=false;polling=false;layout='board';lane='todo';window.cvWorkProgress?.clearIdentity();
 const dialog=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog');if(dialog?.open)dialog.close();document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog-content')?.replaceChildren();
 if(host){const out=node('p','cvp-work-status is-error',message);out.setAttribute('role','status');host.replaceChildren(out)}
}
function apply(view,state){
 const host=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-content');if(!host||!state?.person||!['projects','my-work'].includes(view)){destroy();return}
 if(lockedActor&&state.person.id===lockedActor)return;if(lockedActor)lockedActor='';
 window.cvWorkProgress?.apply(state);
 const project=host.querySelector('[data-ws-task-new]')?.dataset.wsTaskNew||'';
 let original=view==='my-work'?host.querySelector('.cv-ws-list'):project?host.querySelector('.cv-ws-board'):null;
 if(view==='my-work'&&!original){const heading=host.querySelector('.cv-ws-section-heading');if(heading){original=node('div','cv-ws-list');heading.after(original);for(const e of [...host.children])if(e.matches('.cv-ws-row,.cv-ws-empty'))original.append(e)}}
 if(!original){destroy();const grid=view==='projects'&&!project?host.querySelector('.cv-ws-grid'):null;if(grid&&!(state.projects||[]).length&&!host.querySelector('.cvp-work-project-empty')){const empty=node('section','cv-ws-empty cvp-work-project-empty');empty.append(node('h3','','Create your first project'),node('p','','Add tasks, assign owners and follow progress in a shared board.'));const create=button('Create project',null,'cv-button');create.dataset.wsCreateProject='';empty.append(create);grid.after(empty)}return}
 const actor=state.person.id;
 if(current&&current.actor!==actor){current.original.replaceChildren();const d=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog');if(d?.open)d.close();document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog-content')?.replaceChildren();}
 if(lastActor!==actor){owner='';lastActor=actor}
 const retainedNotice=current&&current.actor===actor&&current.view===view&&current.project===project?current.notice:null;
 if(current&&(current.host!==host||current.original!==original||current.actor!==actor||current.view!==view||current.project!==project))destroy();
 let tasks=(state.tasks||[]).filter(t=>t.status!=='archived'&&(view==='my-work'?t.owner_id===actor:t.project_id===project));
 const identity=JSON.stringify([actor,state.person.role,view,project,tasks.map(t=>[t.id,t.version,t.status,t.title,t.description,t.owner_id,t.created_by,t.due_date,t.priority,t.approval_required]),(state.team||[]).map(p=>[p.id,p.name]),(state.projects||[]).map(p=>[p.id,p.owner_id]),state.permissions]);
 if(current&&identity===fingerprint)return;
 if(!current){const region=node('section','cvp-work');region.setAttribute('aria-label',view==='my-work'?'Your task board':'Project task board');original.hidden=true;original.after(region);current={host,region,original,actor,view,project,notice:retainedNotice,cards:new Map()};startRefresh()}
 fingerprint=identity;current.state=state;current.tasks=tasks;render();
}
function notice(text,error=false){if(!current)return;current.notice={text,error};const e=current.region.querySelector('.cvp-work-status');if(e){e.textContent=text;e.classList.toggle('is-error',error)}}
function canEdit(task){const s=current.state,p=s.person;return !!(s.permissions?.admin||task.owner_id===p.id||task.created_by===p.id||s.projects?.find(x=>x.id===task.project_id)?.owner_id===p.id)}
function canMove(task,status){return canEdit(task)&&(!task.approval_required||status!=='done'||current.state.person.role==='ceo')}
function due(raw){if(!raw)return 'No due date';const d=new Date(String(raw).slice(0,10)+'T12:00:00Z');return Number.isFinite(+d)?new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{day:'numeric',month:'short'}).format(d):'Date unavailable'}
function cardDescription(value){
 const text=typeof value==='string'?value:'';
 // Older generated descriptions echo assignment/status fields. Those can
 // become stale after a handoff; cards use the current canonical fields.
 // Keep the original description intact in native details and every save.
 return /^\s*Owner:\s*.+?\s+Status:\s*(?:todo|doing|blocked|review|done)\s+Priority:\s*(?:low|normal|high|urgent)\s+Due date:\s*\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\s+Approval required:\s*(?:yes|no|true|false)\s*$/i.test(text)?'':text;
}
function taskCard(task){
 const signature=JSON.stringify([task,current.actor,current.state.person.role,current.state.permissions,current.state.projects?.find(p=>p.id===task.project_id)?.owner_id,current.state.team?.find(p=>p.id===task.owner_id)?.name]);
 const retained=current.cards.get(task.id);if(retained?.signature===signature)return retained.card;
 const card=node('article','cvp-task');card.dataset.state=task.status;card.dataset.taskId=task.id;
 const title=button(task.title,null,'cvp-task-title');title.dataset.wsTask=task.id;title.setAttribute('aria-label','Open task: '+task.title);card.append(title);
 const description=cardDescription(task.description);if(description)card.append(node('p','cvp-task-description',description));
 const meta=node('div','cvp-task-meta');const person=current.state.team?.find(p=>p.id===task.owner_id);
 const avatar=node('span','cvp-task-avatar',String(person?.name||task.owner_id||'?').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(x=>Array.from(x)[0]).join('').toUpperCase());avatar.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
 meta.append(avatar,node('span','',person?.name||task.owner_id||'Unassigned'),node('span','',due(task.due_date)));card.append(meta);
 const tags=node('div','cvp-task-tags');tags.append(node('span','cvp-task-state',STATES.find(s=>s[0]===task.status)?.[1]||'Unknown status'));
 if(task.priority&&task.priority!=='normal')tags.append(node('span','cvp-task-priority',({urgent:'Urgent',high:'High priority',low:'Low priority'})[task.priority]||task.priority));
 if(task.approval_required)tags.append(node('span','cvp-task-approval','CEO sign-off required'));card.append(tags);
 if(canEdit(task)){
  const details=node('details','cvp-task-move'),summary=node('summary','','Move task');summary.setAttribute('aria-label','Move '+task.title);const choices=node('div','cvp-task-move-options');
  for(const [key,label]of STATES)if(key!==task.status){const b=button(label,()=>move(task.id,key));b.dataset.moveState=key;b.disabled=saving||!canMove(task,key);if(b.disabled)b.title='CEO sign-off is required to complete this task.';choices.append(b)}
  details.append(summary,choices);card.append(details);card.draggable=!saving;
  card.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{if(saving){e.preventDefault();return}dragged=task.id;e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',task.id);e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';card.classList.add('is-dragging')});
  card.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{dragged='';card.classList.remove('is-dragging');current?.region.querySelectorAll('.is-drop-target').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('is-drop-target'))});
 }
 window.cvWorkProgress?.decorateTask(card,task,current.state);current.cards.set(task.id,{card,signature});return card;
}
function paintSaving(){if(!current)return;for(const card of current.region.querySelectorAll('.cvp-task')){const task=current.tasks.find(t=>t.id===card.dataset.taskId);card.draggable=!!task&&canEdit(task)&&!saving;for(const b of card.querySelectorAll('[data-move-state]'))b.disabled=saving||!canMove(task,b.dataset.moveState);}}
function render(){
 if(!current)return;const {region,tasks,state}=current;const toolbar=node('div','cvp-work-toolbar'),switcher=node('div','cvp-work-switch');switcher.setAttribute('aria-label','Task view');
 for(const [key,title]of [['board','Board'],['list','List']]){const b=button(title,()=>{layout=key;render()},'');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(layout===key));switcher.append(b)}toolbar.append(switcher);
 if(current.view==='projects'){const label=node('label','cvp-work-filter','Owner');const select=node('select');select.setAttribute('aria-label','Filter tasks by owner');options(select,[['','Everyone'],...(state.team||[]).map(p=>[p.id,p.name])]);select.value=owner;select.addEventListener('change',()=>{owner=select.value;render()});label.append(select);toolbar.append(label)}
 const statusLabel=node('label','cvp-work-mobile-filter','Status');const select=node('select');select.setAttribute('aria-label','Board column');options(select,STATES);select.value=lane;select.addEventListener('change',()=>{lane=select.value;render()});statusLabel.append(select);toolbar.append(statusLabel);
 const help=node('p','cvp-work-help','Open a card for details. Use Move task or drag between columns to update its status.');const status=node('p','cvp-work-status');status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
 if(current.notice){status.textContent=current.notice.text;status.classList.toggle('is-error',current.notice.error)}
 const selected=current.view==='projects'&&owner?tasks.filter(t=>t.owner_id===owner):tasks;
 const body=node('div',layout==='board'?'cvp-work-board':'cvp-work-list');body.setAttribute('aria-label',layout==='board'?'Tasks by status':'Task list');
 if(layout==='board')for(const [key,title]of STATES){
  const column=node('section','cvp-work-lane');column.dataset.state=key;column.dataset.mobileActive=String(key===lane);const heading=node('h3','',title),rows=selected.filter(t=>t.status===key);heading.append(node('span','cvp-work-count',rows.length));column.append(heading);
  for(const task of rows)column.append(taskCard(task));if(!rows.length)column.append(node('p','cvp-work-empty','No tasks in this column.'));
  column.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{const task=tasks.find(t=>t.id===dragged);if(task&&canMove(task,key)&&task.status!==key){e.preventDefault();column.classList.add('is-drop-target')}});
  column.addEventListener('dragleave',e=>{if(!column.contains(e.relatedTarget))column.classList.remove('is-drop-target')});
  column.addEventListener('drop',e=>{e.preventDefault();column.classList.remove('is-drop-target');if(dragged)move(dragged,key);dragged=''});body.append(column);
 }else{for(const task of selected)body.append(taskCard(task));if(!selected.length)body.append(node('p','cvp-work-empty',owner?'No tasks are assigned to this person.':'No tasks yet.'))}
 region.replaceChildren(toolbar,help,status,body);for(const id of current.cards.keys())if(!tasks.some(t=>t.id===id))current.cards.delete(id);paintSaving();window.cvWorkProgress?.enhance();
}
async function move(id,status){
 const scope=current,task=scope?.tasks.find(t=>t.id===id);if(!task||saving||task.status===status||!canMove(task,status))return;
 const sameScope=()=>current&&current.actor===scope.actor&&current.view===scope.view&&current.project===scope.project;
 saving=true;paintSaving();notice('Saving task status…');
 try{
  const response=await fetch('/communiverse/api/workspace/tasks',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({id:task.id,version:task.version,title:task.title,description:task.description||'',owner_id:task.owner_id,status,priority:task.priority||'normal',due_date:task.due_date||'',approval_required:!!task.approval_required,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()})});
  const data=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Object.assign(Error(data.error||'Task status could not be saved.'),{status:response.status});
  if(!sameScope())return;await window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh();if(sameScope())notice('Task status saved.');
 }catch(error){if(sameScope()){if([401,409].includes(error.status))await window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh();if(sameScope())notice(error.status===409?'This task changed. Its latest version is shown; your move was not applied.':error.message,true)}}
 finally{saving=false;if(sameScope()){fingerprint='';apply(scope.view,window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.getState()||current.state)}paintSaving()}
}
function startRefresh(){timer=setInterval(async()=>{
 if(document.hidden||saving||polling||!current||!current.region.isConnected)return;const scope=current;polling=true;
 try{const r=await fetch('/communiverse/api/workspace/overview',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(current!==scope)return;if(r.status===401){revoke('Your session expired. Reopening Work…');await window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh();return}if(!r.ok)return;const data=await r.json();if(current!==scope)return;
  if(!data.person?.id||data.person.id!==scope.actor){revoke();await window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh();return}
  await window.cvWorkProgress?.refresh();if(current!==scope)return;
  const latest=(data.tasks||[]).map(t=>[t.id,t.version,t.status]);const existing=(scope.state.tasks||[]).map(t=>[t.id,t.version,t.status]);if(JSON.stringify(latest)!==JSON.stringify(existing)){
   if(document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog')?.open)notice('Task updates are available. Close the detail view to refresh.');else await window.__cvWorkspaceNative?.refresh();
  }
 }catch{/* The visible task state is retained; writes still verify versions. */}
 finally{polling=false}
 },30000)}
window.cvWorkPolish={apply,destroy,revoke,release:'communiverse-polish-20261012-v5'};
window.addEventListener('pagehide',destroy,{once:true});
})();

/* Presentation only. Enhances the EXISTING private cvCosmos DOM from the
 * retained social-cosmos-refresh-client.txt / worker/cosmos/client.js.
 * Never creates a panel, retrieves history, changes an account, fetches an API,
 * sends a prompt, or synthesizes an action from answer prose.
 * Parent: load management.css, then cvCosmosCards.mount(document) once.
 * New answers/rerenders are observed; unmount disconnects and removes own classes.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.cvCosmosCards) return;
  let active = null;
  const PANEL = '#cv-chat-heads .cv-cosmos-panel';
  const INTERACTIVE = 'button,input,select,textarea,summary,form,[role=button],[contenteditable=true]';
  const UNAVAILABLE = 'Record link unavailable. Ask Cosmos to recheck the source or access.';
  const KINDS = {
    document:['Document','Open document'],documents:['Document','Open document'],
    sheet:['Sheet','Open sheet'],notebook:['Notebook','Open notebook'],note:['Note','Open note'],folder:['Folder','Open folder'],
    row:['Sheet row','View row'],rows:['Sheet row','View row'],sheet_row:['Sheet row','View row'],
    task:['Task','Open task'],tasks:['Task','Open task'],project:['Project','Open project'],projects:['Project','Open project'],
    source:['Source','Open source'],sources:['Source','Open source'],
    meeting:['Meeting','Open meeting'],meetings:['Meeting','Open meeting']
  };
  // Syntax validation is not an access grant. The canonical page/API must
  // recheck ACLs; callers supply only records allowed for the current audience.
  function canonicalURL(value) {
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value || value.length > 4096 || /[\u0000-\u0020\u007f\\]/.test(value) || value.startsWith('//')) return '';
    try {
      // Inspect before URL normalization removes dot segments. Reject encoded
      // separators/traversal as well as protocol-relative and credential URLs.
      const raw = value.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/i,'').split(/[?#]/,1)[0];
      let path = raw;
      for (let pass=0; pass<3; pass++) {
        if (/[\u0000-\u0020\u007f\\]/.test(path) || /%2f|%5c/i.test(path) || path.split('/').some(part => part==='.' || part==='..')) return '';
        const decoded = decodeURIComponent(path);
        if (decoded===path) break;
        path = decoded;
      }
      if (path.includes('%') || path.split('/').some(part => part==='.' || part==='..') || /[\u0000-\u0020\u007f\\]/.test(path)) return '';
      const url = new URL(value,location.origin);
      if (url.origin !== location.origin || url.username || url.password || !['http:','https:'].includes(url.protocol)) return '';
      if (/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f\\]/.test(decodeURIComponent(url.search+url.hash))) return '';
      // Known UI families only: never APIs, auth/sign-out, asset downloads or
      // a model-supplied redirect destination masquerading as a record.
      if (!/^\/communiverse\/(?:$|workspace\/?$|(?:discover|artists?|events?|communities|plug|people|galler(?:y|ies))(?:\/|$))/.test(url.pathname)) return '';
      if ([...url.searchParams.keys()].some(key => /^(?:redirect|redirect_uri|redirect_url|return|returnTo|next|continue|callback|logout|signout|action)$/i.test(key))) return '';
      return url.pathname+url.search+url.hash;
    } catch { return ''; }
  }
  function safeExistingLink(link) { return !!canonicalURL(link.getAttribute('href')); }
  const text = value => typeof value==='string' ? value.trim() : '';
  function node(tag,className,value) {
    const element=document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) element.className=className;
    if (value) element.textContent=value;
    return element;
  }
  // Optional renderer for the main response owner. It accepts the existing
  // server record shape (kind/document_kind, id, title, url, status,
  // description) plus explicit source_label/scope_label metadata. No ID is
  // converted into a URL, and no source, scope, percentage or action is guessed.
  function renderRecord(record) {
    if (!record || typeof record!=='object' || !text(record.title)) return null;
    const kind=text(record.kind).toLowerCase();
    const type=kind==='documents' || kind==='document' ? text(record.document_kind).toLowerCase() || kind : kind;
    const labels=Object.hasOwn(KINDS,type) ? KINDS[type] : ['Record','Open record'];
    const card=node('article','cv-cosmos-record');
    if (text(record.id)) card.dataset.recordId=record.id;
    card.dataset.recordKind=type || 'record';
    card.append(node('span','cv-cosmos-eyebrow',labels[0]),node('h3','',record.title));
    const status=text(record.status);
    if (status) {
      const badge=node('span','cv-cosmos-status',status);
      if (/^(done|completed|approved)$/i.test(status)) badge.dataset.state='done';
      else if (/^(blocked|rejected)$/i.test(status)) badge.dataset.state='blocked';
      card.append(badge);
    }
    const excerpt=text(record.excerpt) || text(record.description);
    if (excerpt) card.append(node('p','cv-cosmos-excerpt',excerpt));
    const source=text(record.source_label),scope=text(record.scope_label);
    card.append(node('p','cv-cosmos-source',[source ? 'Source: '+source : 'Source details unavailable.',scope].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')));
    let url=record.available===false || record.unavailable===true ? '' : canonicalURL(record.url);
    if (url && text(record.id)) {
      const key=/^(document|documents|sheet|notebook|note|folder)$/.test(type) ? 'document' : /^(row|rows|sheet_row)$/.test(type) ? 'row' : /^(task|tasks)$/.test(type) ? 'task' : /^(project|projects)$/.test(type) ? 'project' : /^(meeting|meetings)$/.test(type) ? 'meeting' : '';
      if (key && new URL(url,location.origin).searchParams.get(key)!==record.id) url='';
    }
    if (url) {
      const link=node('a','cvch-action cv-cosmos-open',labels[1]);
      link.setAttribute('href',url);card.append(link);
    } else card.append(node('p','cv-cosmos-unavailable',UNAVAILABLE));
    return card;
  }
  function mount(root = document) {
    if (!root || typeof root.querySelectorAll !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Cosmos cards need an existing DOM root.');
    if (active) active.unmount();
    const touched = new Map();
    const invalid = new Map(), notices = new Map();
    let stopped = false, frame = null;
    const belongs = node => node.isConnected && (root===document || root.contains(node)) && node.closest(PANEL);
    function mark(node,className) {
      if (!node || node.classList.contains(className)) return;
      node.classList.add(className);
      if (!touched.has(node)) touched.set(node,new Set());
      touched.get(node).add(className);
    }
    function cleanup(node,classes) { for (const name of classes) node.classList.remove(name); }
    function unmark(node,className) {
      if (!touched.get(node)?.delete(className)) return;
      node.classList.remove(className);
      if (!touched.get(node).size) touched.delete(node);
    }
    function restoreLink(link) {
      const saved=invalid.get(link);if (!saved) return;
      for (const [name,added] of [['aria-disabled','true'],['title',UNAVAILABLE]]) if (link.getAttribute(name)===added) {
        if (saved[name]===null) link.removeAttribute(name);else link.setAttribute(name,saved[name]);
      }
      // Never restore an unsafe href, including on unmount/reset.
      invalid.delete(link);unmark(link,'cv5-cosmos-link-unavailable');
    }
    function notice(record,needed) {
      const existing=notices.get(record);
      if (!needed) {existing?.remove();notices.delete(record);return;}
      if (existing?.parentNode===record || record.querySelector('.cv-cosmos-unavailable')) return;
      existing?.remove();const note=node('p','cv5-cosmos-unavailable',UNAVAILABLE);
      record.append(note);notices.set(record,note);
    }
    function refresh() {
      frame = null;
      if (stopped) return;
      // Detached answers must not retain one account's history in this enhancer.
      for (const [node,classes] of touched) if (!belongs(node)) {
        cleanup(node,classes); touched.delete(node);
      }
      for (const link of invalid.keys()) if (!belongs(link)) restoreLink(link);
      for (const [record,note] of notices) if (!belongs(record)) {note.remove();notices.delete(record);}
      const panels = root.matches?.(PANEL) ? [root] : [...root.querySelectorAll(PANEL)];
      for (const panel of panels) {
        mark(panel,'cv5-private-cosmos');
        mark(panel.querySelector('.cv-cosmos-log'),'cv5-cosmos-log');
        for (const answer of panel.querySelectorAll('.cv-cosmos-log .cv-cosmos-answer')) {
          mark(answer,'cv5-cosmos-answer');
          mark(answer.querySelector(':scope > header'),'cv5-cosmos-heading');
          mark(answer.querySelector(':scope > p'),'cv5-cosmos-summary');
          for (const section of answer.querySelectorAll('.cv-cosmos-section')) mark(section,'cv5-cosmos-section');
          mark(answer.querySelector('.cv-cosmos-records'),'cv5-cosmos-records');
          mark(answer.querySelector(':scope > footer'),'cv5-cosmos-footer');
          for (const record of answer.querySelectorAll('.cv-cosmos-record')) {
            mark(record,'cv5-cosmos-record');
            for (const meta of record.querySelectorAll('.cv-cosmos-source,.cv-cosmos-unavailable,.cv-cosmos-status')) mark(meta,meta.classList.contains('cv-cosmos-source') ? 'cv5-cosmos-source' : meta.classList.contains('cv-cosmos-status') ? 'cv5-cosmos-status' : 'cv5-cosmos-unavailable');
            const links = [...record.querySelectorAll('a')];
            let unavailable=false;
            for (const link of links) {
              if (!safeExistingLink(link)) {
                // No URL is invented, repaired, fetched or made trusted by a
                // model label. A malformed old link remains non-navigable.
                if (link.hasAttribute('href')) link.removeAttribute('href');
                if (!invalid.has(link)) invalid.set(link,{'aria-disabled':link.getAttribute('aria-disabled'),title:link.getAttribute('title')});
                link.setAttribute('aria-disabled','true');
                link.setAttribute('title',UNAVAILABLE);
                unmark(link,'cv5-cosmos-record-action');
                mark(link,'cv5-cosmos-link-unavailable');
                unavailable=true;
              } else {
                restoreLink(link);
                mark(link,'cv5-cosmos-record-action');
              }
            }
            // Only decorate; never turn the whole record into a competing hit
            // target over native buttons, disclosures, forms or secondary links.
            notice(record,unavailable || (!links.length && !record.querySelector(INTERACTIVE)));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    function schedule() {
      if (stopped || frame !== null) return;
      frame = requestAnimationFrame(refresh);
    }
    const observer = new MutationObserver(schedule);
    observer.observe(root === document ? document.documentElement : root,
      {subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['href','class']});
    function reset() {
      // Existing cvCosmos owns reset/render/history. Only release presentation
      // references, then decorate whatever that owner renders next.
      for (const [node,classes] of touched) cleanup(node,classes);
      touched.clear();for (const link of invalid.keys()) restoreLink(link);
      for (const note of notices.values()) note.remove();notices.clear();schedule();
    }
    function unmount() {
      if (stopped) return;
      stopped = true; observer.disconnect();
      if (frame !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      frame = null; window.removeEventListener('cv-chat-reset',reset);
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide',unmount);
      for (const [node,classes] of touched) cleanup(node,classes);
      touched.clear();for (const link of invalid.keys()) restoreLink(link);
      for (const note of notices.values()) note.remove();notices.clear();if (active === controller) active = null;
    }
    const controller = {refresh,unmount}; active = controller;
    window.addEventListener('cv-chat-reset',reset);window.addEventListener('pagehide',unmount);
    refresh(); return controller;
  }
  window.cvCosmosCards = {mount,renderRecord,canonicalURL,refresh:() => active?.refresh(),unmount:() => active?.unmount()};
})();

 catch{message('Conversations could not open. Please try again.');return}
 window.cvChat?.close?.();returnFocus=document.activeElement;

},true);

})();

/* Canonical circles stay intact; generic category photos become identity covers. */
(()=>{'use strict';const grid=document.querySelector('#cv-circles');if(!grid)return;
const prefix='/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/v4/management/',records=new Map();
const DEFAULT=/^\/communiverse\/_public\/20261008-marketplace-1\/(?:process-(?:ceramics|textiles|paper|glass|wood|metal)|wire|paper|glass|print|silver|basket)\.jpg$/;
const palettes=[['#e8f0fc','#37588c'],['#eaf1ed','#3d6656'],['#f4eee7','#765941'],['#efeafa','#645386'],['#f6eaf0','#84516b'],['#e7f2f4','#3c6871']];
let alive=true,frame=0,person=null,capabilities=null,refreshing=false;
function cover(host,record){if(!host||host.classList.contains('cvp-community-cover')||!record||record.cover&&!DEFAULT.test(record.cover))return;
 const seed=[...record.id].reduce((n,c)=>n+c.charCodeAt(0),0),color=palettes[seed%palettes.length];host.classList.add('cvp-community-cover');host.style.setProperty('--cv-community-surface',color[0]);host.style.setProperty('--cv-community-ink',color[1]);
 const initials=document.createElement('span');initials.className='cvp-community-monogram';initials.textContent=record.name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(w=>!['&','and','the','in','on','from','of','for'].includes(w.toLowerCase())).slice(0,2).map(w=>Array.from(w)[0]).join('').toUpperCase();initials.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
 const title=document.createElement('span');title.className='cvp-community-place';title.textContent=record.name;host.append(initials,title);
}
function render(){frame=0;if(!alive)return;const editor=document.querySelector('#cv-circle-editor');if(editor?.open&&person?.kind==='staff'&&capabilities?.canPublishOfficialCommunities&&!new URL(location.href).searchParams.has('circle')){const set=(e,t)=>{if(e&&e.textContent!==t)e.textContent=t};set(editor.querySelector('#cv-circle-editor-title'),'Create a community');set(editor.querySelector('[type=submit]'),'Publish community');const policy=[...editor.querySelectorAll('p')].find(e=>e.textContent.startsWith('Circles are public.')||e.dataset.cvpPolicy==='official');if(policy){policy.dataset.cvpPolicy='official';set(policy,'Official communities are public and credited to Communiverse. Member profiles are required to follow and share.')}set(editor.querySelector('#cv-circle-publish-note'),'Your workspace permissions are checked before publication.')}for(const card of grid.querySelectorAll('[data-circle-id]'))cover(card.querySelector('.cv-circle-cover'),records.get(card.dataset.circleId));
 const selected=new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('circle'),hero=document.querySelector('#cv-circle-header .cv-circle-hero > img'),record=records.get(selected);if(hero&&record&&(!record.cover||DEFAULT.test(record.cover))){const wrapper=document.createElement('div');hero.before(wrapper);wrapper.append(hero);cover(wrapper,record)}}
function schedule(){if(!frame)frame=requestAnimationFrame(render)}
async function refresh(){if(refreshing)return;refreshing=true;try{let offset=0;for(let i=0;i<4&&alive;i++){const r=await fetch('/communiverse/api/circles?offset='+offset,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)break;const d=await r.json();for(const c of d.circles||[])if(typeof c.id==='string'&&typeof c.name==='string')records.set(c.id,{id:c.id,name:c.name,cover:c.cover||''});schedule();if(d.next===null||!Number.isSafeInteger(d.next)||d.next<=offset)break;offset=d.next}}catch{/* Original photographs remain if metadata cannot be verified. */}finally{refreshing=false}}
async function identity(){try{const r=await fetch('/communiverse/api/identity/me',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const d=await r.json();person=d.person;if(person?.kind==='staff'){const response=await fetch(prefix+'overview',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(response.ok)capabilities=(await response.json()).capabilities}schedule()}catch{}}
window.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
 const form=e.target;if(!form.matches?.('#cv-circle-form')||person?.kind!=='staff')return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 const out=form.querySelector('[role=status]'),submit=form.querySelector('[type=submit]');if(!capabilities?.canPublishOfficialCommunities){if(out)out.textContent='Your account cannot publish an official community. Ask a workspace administrator to review this request.';return}if(!form.reportValidity())return;
 const data=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));data.tags=String(data.tags||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);data.status='active';data.requestId=form.dataset.officialRequestId||crypto.randomUUID();form.dataset.officialRequestId=data.requestId;
 if(new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('circle')){if(out)out.textContent='Open Manage Communiverse to edit an official community with version checks.';return}
 submit.disabled=true;if(out)out.textContent='Creating your official community…';
 try{const r=await fetch(prefix+'communities/save',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(data)});const result=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw Error(result.error||'Your community could not be created.');if(!/^[a-z0-9-]{2,80}$/.test(result.community?.id||''))throw Error('The community could not be confirmed.');location.assign('/communiverse/communities/?circle='+encodeURIComponent(result.community.id))}
 catch(error){if(out)out.textContent=error.message;submit.disabled=false}
},true);
const observer=new MutationObserver(schedule);observer.observe(grid,{subtree:true,childList:true});const detail=document.querySelector('#cv-circle-header');if(detail)observer.observe(detail,{subtree:true,childList:true});const editor=document.querySelector('#cv-circle-editor');if(editor)observer.observe(editor,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['open']});
refresh();identity();window.addEventListener('popstate',schedule);window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{alive=false;observer.disconnect();cancelAnimationFrame(frame);records.clear();person=null;capabilities=null},{once:true});
})();
